
## File: ./stock.opnameCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Stock Opname (Koreksi Stok Fisik) - Backend Controller
  Description   : Langsung posting saat disimpan. Baris dengan selisih
                   menghasilkan 1 stock_movements ADJUSTMENT (IN/OUT
                   tergantung arah selisih). Baris tanpa selisih tetap
                   tersimpan sebagai audit trail tapi tidak posting movement.
  Depends on    : stock_opname_schema.sql
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. READ (Grid Utama)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read':
        $sql = "
            SELECT
                h.id, h.opname_no, h.opname_date,
                h.warehouse_id, wh.name AS warehouse_name,
                h.bin_location_id, bl.code AS bin_code,
                h.status, h.remark,
                COALESCE((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stock_opname_dtl d WHERE d.opname_hdr_id = h.id), 0) AS total_item,
                COALESCE((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stock_opname_dtl d WHERE d.opname_hdr_id = h.id AND d.variance_qty != 0), 0) AS total_selisih
            FROM stock_opname_hdr h
            LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wh ON wh.id = h.warehouse_id
            LEFT JOIN bin_locations bl ON bl.id = h.bin_location_id
            ORDER BY h.opname_date DESC, h.id DESC
            LIMIT 250
        ";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. READ: detail 1 opname (buat panel detail read-only)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_detail_items':
        $opnameId = intval($_POST['opname_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($opnameId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }
        $stmt = $db->prepare("
            SELECT d.item_name, ig.name AS grade_name, d.system_qty, d.counted_qty, d.variance_qty
            FROM stock_opname_dtl d
            LEFT JOIN item_grade ig ON ig.id = d.grade_id
            WHERE d.opname_hdr_id = :id
            ORDER BY d.id ASC
        ");
        $stmt->execute(['id' => $opnameId]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. READ COMBO: Gudang
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_warehouse':
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM m_warehouse WHERE active = true ORDER BY name ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. READ COMBO: Bin Location per Gudang
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_bin_by_warehouse':
        $warehouseId = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($warehouseId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }
        $stmt = $db->prepare("
            SELECT id, code, name
            FROM bin_locations
            WHERE warehouse_id = :warehouse_id AND active = true
            ORDER BY code ASC
        ");
        $stmt->execute(['warehouse_id' => $warehouseId]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. SNAPSHOT: saldo sistem SAAT INI untuk semua item yang punya
    //    saldo != 0 di gudang(+bin) terpilih -- jadi starting point
    //    buat user input hasil hitung fisik.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_stock_snapshot':
        $warehouseId   = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        $binLocationId = !empty($_POST['bin_location_id']) ? intval($_POST['bin_location_id']) : null;

        if ($warehouseId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT
                sm.item_id, i.name AS item_name, sm.grade_id, ig.name AS grade_name,
                SUM(CASE WHEN sm.movement_type = 'IN' THEN sm.qty ELSE -sm.qty END) AS system_qty
            FROM stock_movements sm
            LEFT JOIN items i ON i.id = sm.item_id
            LEFT JOIN item_grade ig ON ig.id = sm.grade_id
            WHERE sm.warehouse_id = :warehouse_id
              AND sm.bin_location_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM :bin_location_id
            GROUP BY sm.item_id, i.name, sm.grade_id, ig.name
            HAVING SUM(CASE WHEN sm.movement_type = 'IN' THEN sm.qty ELSE -sm.qty END) != 0
            ORDER BY i.name ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['warehouse_id' => $warehouseId, 'bin_location_id' => $binLocationId]);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. CREATE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'create':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $warehouseId   = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        $binLocationId = !empty($_POST['bin_location_id']) ? intval($_POST['bin_location_id']) : null;
        $opnameDate    = trim($_POST['opname_date'] ?? '');
        $remark        = trim($_POST['remark'] ?? '');
        $items         = json_decode($_POST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);

        if ($warehouseId <= 0 || empty($opnameDate) || !is_array($items) || count($items) === 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Lengkapi gudang, tanggal, dan minimal 1 item yang dihitung.']);
            exit;
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $opnameNo = CreateRefNo('stock_opname_hdr', 'opname_no', 'SO-OPN');

            DbOrFail($db, $db->insert('stock_opname_hdr', [
                'opname_no'       => $opnameNo,
                'opname_date'     => $opnameDate,
                'warehouse_id'    => $warehouseId,
                'bin_location_id' => $binLocationId,
                'status'          => 'posted',
                'remark'          => $remark,
                'created_by'      => $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null
            ]), 'insert stock_opname_hdr');

            $opnameId = $db->lastInsertId();
            if (!$opnameId) {
                $stmtId = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM stock_opname_hdr WHERE opname_no = :no");
                $stmtId->execute(['no' => $opnameNo]);
                $opnameId = $stmtId->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['id'] ?? 0;
            }

            $totalVariance = InsertOpnameItemsAndAdjust($db, $opnameId, $opnameNo, $opnameDate, $warehouseId, $binLocationId, $items);

            $db->commit();
            $msg = "Stock Opname {$opnameNo} berhasil disimpan.";
            $msg .= $totalVariance > 0 ? " {$totalVariance} item dengan selisih sudah diposting ke Kartu Stok." : " Tidak ada selisih ditemukan.";
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => $msg]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                $db->rollBack();
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal simpan data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. DELETE (reverse adjustment stock movement)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'delete':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $stmtOld = $db->prepare("SELECT opname_no FROM stock_opname_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtOld->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $opnameNo = $stmtOld->fetchColumn();
            if (!$opnameNo) {
                throw new Exception("Data opname tidak ditemukan.");
            }

            ReverseStockMovementByReference($db, 'adjustment', $id);

            $stmtDelD = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM stock_opname_dtl WHERE opname_hdr_id = :id");
            $stmtDelD->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $stmtDelH = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM stock_opname_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtDelH->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Stock Opname {$opnameNo} berhasil dihapus & penyesuaian stok dibatalkan."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                $db->rollBack();
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal menghapus data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
}

// ==================================================================
// HELPER: InsertOpnameItemsAndAdjust
// Insert semua baris hitung (termasuk yang cocok/tanpa selisih, buat
// audit trail), tapi cuma posting stock_movements ADJUSTMENT untuk
// baris yang ada selisih. Return jumlah baris yang ada selisih.
// ==================================================================
function InsertOpnameItemsAndAdjust($db, $opnameId, $opnameNo, $opnameDate, $warehouseId, $binLocationId, array $items) {
    $varianceCount = 0;

    foreach ($items as $it) {
        $itemId     = intval($it['item_id'] ?? 0);
        $itemName   = trim($it['item_name'] ?? '');
        $gradeId    = !empty($it['grade_id']) ? intval($it['grade_id']) : null;
        $systemQty  = round(floatval($it['system_qty'] ?? 0), 4);
        $countedQty = round(floatval($it['counted_qty'] ?? 0), 4);
        $remark     = trim($it['remark'] ?? '');

        if ($itemId <= 0) {
            continue;
        }

        $variance = round($countedQty - $systemQty, 4);

        DbOrFail($db, $db->insert('stock_opname_dtl', [
            'opname_hdr_id'   => $opnameId,
            'item_id'         => $itemId,
            'item_name'       => $itemName,
            'grade_id'        => $gradeId,
            'bin_location_id' => $binLocationId,
            'system_qty'      => $systemQty,
            'counted_qty'     => $countedQty,
            'variance_qty'    => $variance,
            'remark'          => $remark
        ]), 'insert stock_opname_dtl');

        if (abs($variance) < 0.0001) {
            continue; // cocok, gak perlu movement
        }

        $varianceCount++;

        PostStockMovement($db, [
            'movement_date'   => $opnameDate,
            'item_id'         => $itemId,
            'grade_id'        => $gradeId,
            'warehouse_id'    => $warehouseId,
            'bin_location_id' => $binLocationId,
            'movement_type'   => $variance > 0 ? 'IN' : 'OUT',
            'qty'             => abs($variance),
            'reference_type'  => 'adjustment',
            'reference_id'    => $opnameId,
            'reference_no'    => $opnameNo,
        ]);
    }

    return $varianceCount;
}

## File: ./stock.cardCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Stock Card (Kartu Stok) Monitoring - Backend Controller
  Description   : Laporan saldo & mutasi stok per item + gudang. Read-only,
                   semua dihitung langsung dari stock_movements (satu-satunya
                   sumber kebenaran, sama prinsip dengan deposit_reportCRD.php).
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read_summary';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. COMBO: Gudang (+ opsi "Semua Gudang" ditambahkan di client)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_warehouse':
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM m_warehouse WHERE active = true ORDER BY name ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. COMBO: Item (search remote, supaya tidak load semua item sekaligus)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_item_combo':
        $query = trim($_POST['query'] ?? '');
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM items WHERE is_active = true";
        $params = [];
        if ($query !== '') {
            $sql .= " AND name ILIKE :query";
            $params['query'] = '%' . $query . '%';
        }
        $sql .= " ORDER BY name ASC LIMIT 50";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute($params);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. RINGKASAN: saldo per item (+ grade) di gudang terpilih
    //    (warehouse_id = 0 -> semua gudang digabung, via view_stock_balance_total)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_summary':
        $warehouseId = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        $onlyNonZero = ($_POST['only_non_zero'] ?? '1') === '1';

        if ($warehouseId > 0) {
            $sql = "
                SELECT
                    vb.item_id, vb.grade_id, i.name AS item_name,
                    vb.warehouse_id, vb.warehouse_name,
                    vb.balance_qty, vb.avg_unit_cost
                FROM view_stock_balance vb
                LEFT JOIN items i ON i.id = vb.item_id
                WHERE vb.warehouse_id = :warehouse_id
                " . ($onlyNonZero ? " AND vb.balance_qty <> 0 " : "") . "
                ORDER BY i.name ASC
            ";
            $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->execute(['warehouse_id' => $warehouseId]);
        } else {
            $sql = "
                SELECT
                    vt.item_id, vt.grade_id, i.name AS item_name,
                    NULL AS warehouse_id, 'Semua Gudang' AS warehouse_name,
                    vt.balance_qty, NULL AS avg_unit_cost
                FROM view_stock_balance_total vt
                LEFT JOIN items i ON i.id = vt.item_id
                WHERE 1=1
                " . ($onlyNonZero ? " AND vt.balance_qty <> 0 " : "") . "
                ORDER BY i.name ASC
            ";
            $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->execute();
        }

        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. DETAIL MUTASI per item (+grade opsional, +warehouse opsional)
    //    warehouse_id = 0 -> gabung semua gudang, running balance dihitung
    //    total lintas gudang (urut tanggal).
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_mutation':
        $itemId      = intval($_POST['item_id'] ?? 0);
        $gradeId     = intval($_POST['grade_id'] ?? 0);
        $warehouseId = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);

        if ($itemId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $where = "sm.item_id = :item_id";
        $params = ['item_id' => $itemId];

        if ($gradeId > 0) {
            $where .= " AND sm.grade_id = :grade_id";
            $params['grade_id'] = $gradeId;
        }
        if ($warehouseId > 0) {
            $where .= " AND sm.warehouse_id = :warehouse_id";
            $params['warehouse_id'] = $warehouseId;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT
                sm.movement_date, sm.movement_type, sm.qty, sm.unit_cost,
                sm.reference_type, sm.reference_no, sm.remark,
                wh.name AS warehouse_name,
                SUM(CASE WHEN sm.movement_type = 'IN' THEN sm.qty ELSE -sm.qty END)
                    OVER (ORDER BY sm.movement_date ASC, sm.id ASC) AS running_balance
            FROM stock_movements sm
            LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wh ON wh.id = sm.warehouse_id
            WHERE {$where}
            ORDER BY sm.movement_date ASC, sm.id ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute($params);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
}

## File: ./supplier.paymentCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Supplier Payment - Backend Controller (Full CRUD + AutoPostJournal Engine)
  Called from   : js/purchase_payment.js
  Note          : Pola mengikuti paymentCRD.php (AR) persis, dibalik sisi
                  debit-kreditnya untuk AP:
                    Payment Entry (AR): Debit Kas/Bank (override) / Kredit Piutang
                    Supplier Payment (AP): Debit Hutang Usaha / Kredit Kas/Bank (override)
                  reference_type = 'supplier_payment' (dipakai utk rule AP
                  MAUPUN Advance -- tidak ada enum terpisah spt customer_deposit)
*/
/*
  PERUBAHAN (perbaikan dari versi yang sudah berjalan)
  - create/update/delete kini SATU transaksi (sebelumnya create autocommit: bila jurnal gagal,
    header + item + paid_amount tetap tersimpan dan user melihat error -> retry = pembayaran dobel).
  - validasi server: invoice ada, milik supplier yang dipilih, nominal <= sisa tagihan, tidak dobel.
  - paid_amount dihitung ulang dari supplier_payment_items (bukan +/- manual dengan GREATEST).
  - saldo deposit tidak boleh negatif (hapus/ubah pembayaran yang uang mukanya sudah terpakai ditolak).
  - nomor SPAY & JV satu-transaksi (butuh CreateRefNoDb di function.all.php baru; ada fallback).
  - task baru read_payment_method; task read_* membaca $_REQUEST (POST maupun GET).
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. READ (Grid Utama)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read':
        $sql = "
            SELECT p.id, p.payment_no, p.payment_date, p.supplier_id,
                   s.name AS supplier_name,
                   p.account_id, (coa.account_code || ' - ' || coa.account_name) AS account_name,
                   p.payment_method, p.total_amount, COALESCE(p.allocated_amount, 0) AS allocated_amount,
                   COALESCE(p.excess_amount, 0) AS excess_amount,
                   COALESCE(p.deposit_applied, 0) AS deposit_applied,
                   p.reference_no, p.notes
            FROM supplier_payments p
            LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = p.supplier_id
            LEFT JOIN chart_of_accounts coa ON coa.id = p.account_id
            ORDER BY p.payment_date DESC, p.id DESC
            LIMIT 250
        ";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;
// ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1B. READ PAYMENT DETAIL (Alokasi Invoice yang Dibayar)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_payment_detail':
        $paymentId = intval($_REQUEST['payment_id'] ?? $_REQUEST['payment_id'] ?? 0);

        if ($paymentId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT 
                pi.inv_no AS invoice_no,
                pi.supplier_invoice_no,
                TO_CHAR(pi.invoice_date, 'DD-MM-YYYY') AS invoice_date,
                COALESCE(pi.total_amount, 0) AS total_amount,
                COALESCE(pi.total_amount - pi.paid_amount, 0) AS unpaid_amount,
                COALESCE(spi.amount_paid, 0) AS amount_paid
            FROM supplier_payment_items spi
            INNER JOIN purchase_invoice_hdr pi ON spi.invoice_id = pi.id
            WHERE spi.supplier_payment_id = :payment_id
            ORDER BY pi.id ASC
        ";

        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['payment_id' => $paymentId]);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;



    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. READ SINGLE (Form Edit)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_edit':
        $id = intval($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
        $stmt = $db->prepare("
            SELECT p.*, (p.payment_date)::text AS payment_date
            FROM supplier_payments p
            WHERE p.id = :id
        ");
        $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $row = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => (bool)$row, 'data' => $row ?: new stdClass()]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. READ COMBO & UNPAID PURCHASE INVOICES
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_supplier':
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM m_suppliers WHERE active = true ORDER BY name ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    case 'read_account':
        $sql = "
            SELECT id, (account_code || ' - ' || account_name) AS account_name
            FROM chart_of_accounts
            WHERE is_active = true
              AND parent_id IS NOT NULL
              AND (
                  account_type IN ('cash', 'bank')
                  OR parent_id = (SELECT id FROM chart_of_accounts WHERE account_code = '1100' LIMIT 1)
              )
            ORDER BY account_code ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3B. READ METODE PEMBAYARAN
    //     Bila kolom payment_method bertipe ENUM, nilainya diambil dari DB
    //     (supaya combo di form tidak pernah mengirim nilai yang ditolak enum).
    //     Bila bukan enum, dipakai daftar default.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_payment_method':
        $rows = [];
        try {
            $rows = $db->query("
                SELECT e.enumlabel AS value
                FROM pg_attribute a
                JOIN pg_type t ON t.oid = a.atttypid
                JOIN pg_enum e ON e.enumtypid = t.oid
                WHERE a.attrelid = 'supplier_payments'::regclass
                  AND a.attname = 'payment_method'
                ORDER BY e.enumsortorder
            ")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        } catch (Exception $e) {
            $rows = [];
        }
        if (empty($rows)) {
            $rows = [['value' => 'BANK_TRANSFER'], ['value' => 'CASH'], ['value' => 'CHEQUE']];
        }
        foreach ($rows as &$mr) {
            $mr['label'] = ucwords(strtolower(str_replace('_', ' ', $mr['value'])));
        }
        unset($mr);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $rows]);
        break;

    case 'read_unpaid_invoices':
        $supplierId = intval($_REQUEST['supplier_id'] ?? 0);
        $paymentId  = intval($_REQUEST['payment_id'] ?? 0);

        if ($supplierId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => 0, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        // payment_id = 0 → mode tambah: amount_paid default ke unpaid_amount (sisa hutang)
        // payment_id > 0 → mode edit: amount_paid dari supplier_payment_items (alokasi tersimpan)
        $sql = "
            SELECT
                i.id AS invoice_id,
                i.inv_no AS invoice_no,
                i.supplier_invoice_no,
                i.invoice_date,
                i.total_amount,
                i.paid_amount,
                ((COALESCE(i.total_amount, 0) - COALESCE(i.paid_amount, 0)) + COALESCE(pi.amount_paid, 0)) AS unpaid_amount,
                CASE
                    WHEN :payment_id2 = 0
                        THEN ((COALESCE(i.total_amount, 0) - COALESCE(i.paid_amount, 0)) + COALESCE(pi.amount_paid, 0))
                    ELSE COALESCE(pi.amount_paid, 0)
                END AS amount_paid
            FROM purchase_invoice_hdr i
            LEFT JOIN supplier_payment_items pi ON pi.invoice_id = i.id AND pi.supplier_payment_id = :payment_id
            WHERE i.supplier_id = :supplier_id
              AND i.status = 'posted'
              AND (((COALESCE(i.total_amount, 0) - COALESCE(i.paid_amount, 0)) + COALESCE(pi.amount_paid, 0)) > 0.01 OR pi.id IS NOT NULL)
            ORDER BY i.invoice_date ASC, i.id ASC
        ";

        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['supplier_id' => $supplierId, 'payment_id' => $paymentId, 'payment_id2' => $paymentId]);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Saldo Deposit di Supplier -- dihitung dari journal_lines (net
    // debit-kredit akun 1400 Uang Muka Pembelian) terkait supplier_payments
    // milik supplier ini. Tidak ada tabel ledger baru.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_supplier_deposit_balance':
        $supplierId = intval($_REQUEST['supplier_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($supplierId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'balance' => 0]);
            exit;
        }
        $balance = GetSupplierDepositBalance($db, $supplierId);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'balance' => $balance]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. CREATE  (satu transaksi: header + item + paid_amount + jurnal)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'create':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $rawTotal   = str_replace(',', '', $_POST['total_amount'] ?? '0');
        $totalPaid  = round(floatval($rawTotal), 2);
        $depositApplied = round(floatval($_POST['deposit_applied'] ?? 0), 2);
        $supplierId = intval($_POST['supplier_id'] ?? 0);
        $accountId  = intval($_POST['account_id'] ?? 0);
        $payDate    = trim($_POST['payment_date'] ?? '');
        $method     = trim($_POST['payment_method'] ?? 'BANK_TRANSFER');
        $refNo      = trim($_POST['reference_no'] ?? '');
        $notes      = trim($_POST['notes'] ?? '');
        $items      = json_decode($_POST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);

        if ($supplierId <= 0 || empty($payDate) || ($totalPaid <= 0 && $depositApplied <= 0)) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Lengkapi data pembayaran. Total dibayar atau saldo deposit yang dipakai harus > 0.']);
            exit;
        }
        if ($totalPaid > 0 && $accountId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Pilih akun Kas/Bank sumber dana.']);
            exit;
        }
        $dtChk = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $payDate);
        if (!$dtChk || $dtChk->format('Y-m-d') !== $payDate) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Format Tanggal Bayar tidak valid (harus YYYY-MM-DD).']);
            exit;
        }

        if ($db->inTransaction()) {
            try { $db->rollBack(); } catch (Exception $ex) {}
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            // Serialisasi per supplier: dua pembayaran bersamaan tidak bisa
            // sama-sama memakai saldo deposit / melunasi invoice yang sama.
            SpLockSupplier($db, $supplierId);
            $before = [$supplierId => GetSupplierDepositBalance($db, $supplierId)];

            $alloc = SpNormalizeItems($items);
            $allocatedTotal = round(array_sum($alloc), 2);

            if ($depositApplied > 0) {
                $availableDeposit = $before[$supplierId];
                if ($depositApplied > $availableDeposit + 0.01) {
                    throw new Exception("Saldo deposit yang dipakai (Rp " . number_format($depositApplied, 2) . ") melebihi saldo tersedia (Rp " . number_format($availableDeposit, 2) . ").");
                }
                if ($depositApplied > $allocatedTotal + 0.01) {
                    throw new Exception('Saldo deposit yang dipakai tidak boleh melebihi total alokasi ke invoice.');
                }
            }

            $cashNeeded = round($allocatedTotal - $depositApplied, 2);
            if ($totalPaid + 0.01 < $cashNeeded) {
                throw new Exception("Total dibayar + saldo deposit belum cukup menutupi alokasi invoice.");
            }
            $excessAmount = round($totalPaid - $cashNeeded, 2);

            // invoice ada, milik supplier ini, nominal <= sisa tagihan (baris dikunci)
            SpValidateAllocations($db, $supplierId, $alloc);

            $paymentNo = SpNextRefNo($db, 'supplier_payments', 'payment_no', 'SPAY');

            $createdBy = null;
            $rawUserId = intval($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($rawUserId > 0) {
                $stmtUser = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM m_user_system WHERE id = :uid LIMIT 1");
                $stmtUser->execute(['uid' => $rawUserId]);
                if ($stmtUser->fetchColumn()) {
                    $createdBy = $rawUserId;
                }
            }

            $okHeader = $db->insert('supplier_payments', [
                'payment_no'       => $paymentNo,
                'payment_date'     => $payDate,
                'supplier_id'      => $supplierId,
                'account_id'       => $totalPaid > 0 ? $accountId : null,
                'payment_method'   => $method,
                'total_amount'     => $totalPaid,
                'allocated_amount' => $allocatedTotal,
                'excess_amount'    => $excessAmount,
                'deposit_applied'  => $depositApplied,
                'reference_no'     => $refNo,
                'notes'            => $notes,
                'created_by'       => $createdBy
            ]);
            if ($okHeader === false) {
                throw new Exception('ERROR ASLI DB HEADER: ' . $db->getResult());
            }

            $stmtP = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM supplier_payments WHERE payment_no = :no ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1");
            $stmtP->execute(['no' => $paymentNo]);
            $paymentId = intval($stmtP->fetchColumn());
            if ($paymentId <= 0) {
                throw new Exception('ERROR ASLI DB HEADER: id pembayaran tidak terbaca setelah insert.');
            }

            foreach ($alloc as $invId => $amt) {
                $okItem = $db->insert('supplier_payment_items', [
                    'supplier_payment_id' => $paymentId,
                    'invoice_id'          => $invId,
                    'amount_paid'         => $amt
                ]);
                if ($okItem === false) {
                    throw new Exception("ERROR ASLI DB ITEM (Invoice ID {$invId}): " . $db->getResult());
                }
            }

            // paid_amount dihitung dari item (sumber kebenaran), bukan ditambah manual
            SpRecalcInvoicePaid($db, array_keys($alloc));

            try {
                PostSupplierPaymentJournal($db, $paymentId, $paymentNo, $payDate, $accountId, $allocatedTotal, $excessAmount, $depositApplied);
            } catch (Exception $e) {
                throw new Exception('ERROR ASLI POST JURNAL: ' . $e->getMessage());
            }

            SpAssertDepositNotNegative($db, $before);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'id' => $paymentId, 'payment_no' => $paymentNo, 'message' => "Pembayaran {$paymentNo} berhasil disimpan."]);
        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                try { $db->rollBack(); } catch (Exception $ex) {}
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
            exit;
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. UPDATE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'update':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 3) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $id          = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $supplierId  = intval($_POST['supplier_id'] ?? 0);
        $accountId   = intval($_POST['account_id'] ?? 0);
        $payDate     = trim($_POST['payment_date'] ?? '');
        $method      = trim($_POST['payment_method'] ?? 'BANK_TRANSFER');
        $refNo       = trim($_POST['reference_no'] ?? '');
        $notes       = trim($_POST['notes'] ?? '');
        $items       = json_decode($_POST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);

        $rawTotal  = str_replace(',', '', $_POST['total_amount'] ?? '0');
        $totalPaid = round(floatval($rawTotal), 2);
        $depositApplied = round(floatval($_POST['deposit_applied'] ?? 0), 2);

        if ($id <= 0 || $supplierId <= 0 || empty($payDate) || ($totalPaid <= 0 && $depositApplied <= 0)) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Data edit tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }
        if ($totalPaid > 0 && $accountId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Pilih akun Kas/Bank sumber dana.']);
            exit;
        }
        $dtChk = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $payDate);
        if (!$dtChk || $dtChk->format('Y-m-d') !== $payDate) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Format Tanggal Bayar tidak valid (harus YYYY-MM-DD).']);
            exit;
        }

        if ($db->inTransaction()) {
            try { $db->rollBack(); } catch (Exception $ex) {}
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $stmtOldPay = $db->prepare("SELECT payment_no, supplier_id FROM supplier_payments WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
            $stmtOldPay->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $oldPay = $stmtOldPay->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$oldPay) {
                throw new Exception("Data pembayaran tidak ditemukan.");
            }
            $oldPayNo       = $oldPay['payment_no'];
            $oldSupplierId  = intval($oldPay['supplier_id']);

            // kunci supplier lama & baru (urut id supaya tidak deadlock) + catat saldo deposit awal
            $sids = array_values(array_unique([$oldSupplierId, $supplierId]));
            sort($sids);
            $before = [];
            foreach ($sids as $sid) {
                SpLockSupplier($db, $sid);
                $before[$sid] = GetSupplierDepositBalance($db, $sid);
            }

            $stmtOldItems = $db->prepare("SELECT invoice_id FROM supplier_payment_items WHERE supplier_payment_id = :id");
            $stmtOldItems->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $oldInvoiceIds = array_map('intval', $stmtOldItems->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN));

            $alloc = SpNormalizeItems($items);
            SpLockInvoices($db, array_merge($oldInvoiceIds, array_keys($alloc)));

            ReverseSupplierPaymentJournal($db, $id);

            $stmtDelItems = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM supplier_payment_items WHERE supplier_payment_id = :id");
            $stmtDelItems->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $allocatedTotal = round(array_sum($alloc), 2);

            if ($depositApplied > 0) {
                // dihitung SETELAH jurnal lama dibalik -> pemakaian deposit oleh pembayaran ini sendiri sudah kembali
                $availableDeposit = GetSupplierDepositBalance($db, $supplierId);
                if ($depositApplied > $availableDeposit + 0.01) {
                    throw new Exception("Saldo deposit yang dipakai melebihi saldo tersedia (Rp " . number_format($availableDeposit, 2) . ").");
                }
                if ($depositApplied > $allocatedTotal + 0.01) {
                    throw new Exception('Saldo deposit yang dipakai tidak boleh melebihi total alokasi ke invoice.');
                }
            }

            $cashNeeded = round($allocatedTotal - $depositApplied, 2);
            if ($totalPaid + 0.01 < $cashNeeded) {
                throw new Exception('Total dibayar + saldo deposit belum cukup menutupi alokasi invoice.');
            }
            $excessAmount = round($totalPaid - $cashNeeded, 2);

            // item lama sudah dihapus -> "pembayaran lain" saja yang dihitung sebagai terbayar
            SpValidateAllocations($db, $supplierId, $alloc);

            $okUpd = $db->update('supplier_payments', [
                'payment_date'     => $payDate,
                'supplier_id'      => $supplierId,
                'account_id'       => $totalPaid > 0 ? $accountId : null,
                'payment_method'   => $method,
                'total_amount'     => $totalPaid,
                'allocated_amount' => $allocatedTotal,
                'excess_amount'    => $excessAmount,
                'deposit_applied'  => $depositApplied,
                'reference_no'     => $refNo,
                'notes'            => $notes
            ], "id = " . intval($id));

            if ($okUpd === false) {
                throw new Exception("GAGAL UPDATE HEADER: " . $db->getResult());
            }

            foreach ($alloc as $invId => $amt) {
                $okInsItem = $db->insert('supplier_payment_items', ['supplier_payment_id' => $id, 'invoice_id' => $invId, 'amount_paid' => $amt]);
                if ($okInsItem === false) {
                    throw new Exception("GAGAL INSERT ITEM UPDATE (Invoice ID {$invId}): " . $db->getResult());
                }
            }

            SpRecalcInvoicePaid($db, array_merge($oldInvoiceIds, array_keys($alloc)));

            PostSupplierPaymentJournal($db, $id, $oldPayNo, $payDate, $accountId, $allocatedTotal, $excessAmount, $depositApplied);

            SpAssertDepositNotNegative($db, $before);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Pembayaran {$oldPayNo} berhasil diperbarui."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                try { $db->rollBack(); } catch (Exception $ex) {}
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ERROR UPDATE: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
            exit;
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. DELETE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'delete':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID data tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        if ($db->inTransaction()) {
            try { $db->rollBack(); } catch (Exception $ex) {}
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $stmtOldPay = $db->prepare("SELECT payment_no, supplier_id FROM supplier_payments WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
            $stmtOldPay->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $oldPay = $stmtOldPay->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$oldPay) {
                throw new Exception("Data pembayaran tidak ditemukan.");
            }
            $oldPayNo = $oldPay['payment_no'];
            $delSupplierId = intval($oldPay['supplier_id']);

            SpLockSupplier($db, $delSupplierId);
            $before = [$delSupplierId => GetSupplierDepositBalance($db, $delSupplierId)];

            $stmtOldItems = $db->prepare("SELECT invoice_id FROM supplier_payment_items WHERE supplier_payment_id = :id");
            $stmtOldItems->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $oldInvoiceIds = array_map('intval', $stmtOldItems->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN));
            SpLockInvoices($db, $oldInvoiceIds);

            ReverseSupplierPaymentJournal($db, $id);

            $stmtDelItems = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM supplier_payment_items WHERE supplier_payment_id = :id");
            $stmtDelItems->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $stmtDelHeader = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM supplier_payments WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtDelHeader->execute(['id' => $id]);

            SpRecalcInvoicePaid($db, $oldInvoiceIds);

            // uang muka dari pembayaran ini mungkin sudah dipakai pembayaran lain
            SpAssertDepositNotNegative($db, $before);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Pembayaran {$oldPayNo} berhasil dihapus & jurnal dibatalkan."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                try { $db->rollBack(); } catch (Exception $ex) {}
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ERROR DELETE: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
            exit;
        }
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
}

// ==================================================================
// HELPER LOKAL
// ==================================================================
function PostSupplierPaymentJournal($db, $paymentId, $paymentNo, $payDate, $cashAccountId, $allocatedTotal, $excessAmount, $depositApplied = 0) {
    $cashPortionAp = round($allocatedTotal - $depositApplied, 2);

    // (0) Porsi yang dilunasi pakai saldo deposit di supplier -> Debit
    //     Hutang Usaha (2100, fixed) / Kredit Uang Muka Pembelian (1400,
    //     fixed). Tidak ada akun yang perlu di-override di sini.
    if ($depositApplied > 0) {
        $resultDep = AutoPostJournal($db, [
            'rule_code'      => 'SUP_PAY_DEP',
            'reference_type' => 'supplier_payment',
            'reference_id'   => $paymentId,
            'journal_date'   => $payDate,
            'amount'         => $depositApplied,
            'description'    => 'Pelunasan Hutang Pakai Deposit ' . $paymentNo,
        ]);
        if (!$resultDep['success']) {
            throw new Exception($resultDep['message']);
        }
    }

    // (1) Porsi yang mengurangi Hutang Usaha pakai kas/bank -> Debit Hutang Usaha (fixed) / Kredit Kas-Bank (override)
    if ($cashPortionAp > 0) {
        $resultAp = AutoPostJournal($db, [
            'rule_code'         => 'SUP_PAY_AP',
            'reference_type'    => 'supplier_payment',
            'reference_id'      => $paymentId,
            'journal_date'      => $payDate,
            'amount'            => $cashPortionAp,
            'description'       => 'Pembayaran Hutang ' . $paymentNo,
            'credit_account_id' => $cashAccountId, // akun Kas/Bank pilihan user
        ]);
        if (!$resultAp['success']) {
            throw new Exception($resultAp['message']);
        }
    }

    // (2) Porsi lebih bayar -> Debit Uang Muka Pembelian (fixed) / Kredit Kas-Bank (override)
    if ($excessAmount > 0) {
        $resultAdv = AutoPostJournal($db, [
            'rule_code'         => 'SUP_PAY_ADV',
            'reference_type'    => 'supplier_payment',
            'reference_id'      => $paymentId,
            'journal_date'      => $payDate,
            'amount'            => $excessAmount,
            'description'       => 'Uang Muka Pembelian dari Pembayaran ' . $paymentNo,
            'credit_account_id' => $cashAccountId, // akun Kas/Bank pilihan user
        ]);
        if (!$resultAdv['success']) {
            throw new Exception($resultAdv['message']);
        }
    }

    if ($allocatedTotal <= 0 && $excessAmount <= 0 && $depositApplied <= 0) {
        throw new Exception('Tidak ada nominal untuk diposting ke jurnal.');
    }
}

function ReverseSupplierPaymentJournal($db, $paymentId) {
    // Kedua rule (AP & Advance) sama-sama pakai reference_type
    // 'supplier_payment' -- satu panggilan ini menghapus keduanya sekaligus.
    ReverseJournalByReference($db, 'supplier_payment', $paymentId);
}

/*
  Hitung saldo deposit tersedia di seorang supplier, langsung dari
  journal_lines (net debit-kredit akun 1400 Uang Muka Pembelian), dilacak
  lewat reference_type='supplier_payment' + reference_id = id di
  supplier_payments milik supplier ini.
*/
function GetSupplierDepositBalance($db, $supplierId) {
    $sql = "
        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(jl.debit - jl.credit), 0) AS balance
        FROM journal_lines jl
        JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jl.journal_id
        JOIN chart_of_accounts coa ON coa.id = jl.account_id AND coa.account_code = '1400'
        WHERE je.reference_type = 'supplier_payment'
          AND je.reference_id IN (
              SELECT id FROM supplier_payments WHERE supplier_id = :supplier_id
          )
    ";
    $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
    $stmt->execute(['supplier_id' => $supplierId]);
    $balance = floatval($stmt->fetchColumn());
    return round($balance, 2);
}


// ==================================================================
// HELPER TAMBAHAN (validasi server, penguncian, hitung ulang paid_amount)
// ==================================================================

/** Placeholder :p0,:p1,... untuk klausa IN. */
function SpInList(array $ids, $prefix, array &$params) {
    $ph = [];
    foreach (array_values($ids) as $i => $v) {
        $ph[] = ':' . $prefix . $i;
        $params[$prefix . $i] = (int)$v;
    }
    return implode(',', $ph);
}

/** Nomor referensi satu-transaksi (lihat CreateRefNoDb di function.all.php); fallback ke CreateRefNo. */
function SpNextRefNo($db, $table, $column, $prefix) {
    $no = function_exists('CreateRefNoDb')
        ? CreateRefNoDb($db, $table, $column, $prefix)
        : CreateRefNo($table, $column, $prefix);
    if ($no === '' || $no === null) {
        throw new Exception('Gagal generate nomor dokumen (' . $prefix . ').');
    }
    return $no;
}

/** Kunci per supplier selama transaksi (advisory lock, lepas otomatis saat COMMIT/ROLLBACK). */
function SpLockSupplier($db, $supplierId) {
    $supplierId = intval($supplierId);
    if ($supplierId <= 0) { return; }
    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(7101, CAST(:sid AS integer))");
    $st->execute(['sid' => $supplierId]);
}

/** Baris items dari browser -> [invoice_id => nominal]. Baris kosong dilewati; invoice dobel ditolak. */
function SpNormalizeItems($items) {
    $alloc = [];
    if (!is_array($items)) { return $alloc; }
    foreach ($items as $it) {
        $invId = intval($it['invoice_id'] ?? 0);
        $amt   = round(floatval($it['amount_paid'] ?? 0), 2);
        if ($invId <= 0 || $amt <= 0) { continue; }
        if (isset($alloc[$invId])) {
            throw new Exception('Invoice yang sama tidak boleh muncul dua kali dalam satu pembayaran.');
        }
        $alloc[$invId] = $amt;
    }
    return $alloc;
}

/** Kunci baris invoice (urut id). */
function SpLockInvoices($db, array $ids) {
    $ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', $ids))));
    if (empty($ids)) { return; }
    sort($ids);
    $params = [];
    $in = SpInList($ids, 'l', $params);
    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM purchase_invoice_hdr WHERE id IN ($in) ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE");
    $st->execute($params);
}

/**
 * Invoice harus ada, milik supplier yang dipilih, dan nominal <= sisa tagihan.
 * Sisa = total - SUM(item pembayaran yang ada di DB saat ini); pada update, item lama
 * pembayaran ini sudah dihapus lebih dulu, jadi hanya pembayaran LAIN yang dihitung.
 */
function SpValidateAllocations($db, $supplierId, array $alloc) {
    if (empty($alloc)) { return; }
    $ids = array_keys($alloc);
    SpLockInvoices($db, $ids);

    $params = [];
    $in = SpInList($ids, 'v', $params);
    $st = $db->prepare("
        SELECT inv.id, inv.inv_no, inv.supplier_id,
               COALESCE(inv.total_amount, 0) AS total_amount,
               COALESCE((SELECT SUM(i.amount_paid) FROM supplier_payment_items i WHERE i.invoice_id = inv.id), 0) AS paid_now
        FROM purchase_invoice_hdr inv
        WHERE inv.id IN ($in)
    ");
    $st->execute($params);
    $map = [];
    foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) { $map[intval($r['id'])] = $r; }

    foreach ($alloc as $invId => $amt) {
        if (!isset($map[$invId])) {
            throw new Exception("Invoice (id {$invId}) tidak ditemukan.");
        }
        $r = $map[$invId];
        if (intval($r['supplier_id']) !== intval($supplierId)) {
            throw new Exception("Invoice {$r['inv_no']} milik supplier yang berbeda dengan pembayaran ini.");
        }
        $outstanding = round((float)$r['total_amount'] - (float)$r['paid_now'], 2);
        if ($amt > $outstanding + 0.01) {
            throw new Exception("Pembayaran untuk invoice {$r['inv_no']} (Rp " . number_format($amt, 2) . ") melebihi sisa tagihan (Rp " . number_format($outstanding, 2) . ").");
        }
    }
}

/** paid_amount = SUM(item pembayaran). Idempotent, tidak ada GREATEST(0, ...) yang menyembunyikan selisih. */
function SpRecalcInvoicePaid($db, array $ids) {
    $ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', $ids))));
    if (empty($ids)) { return; }
    sort($ids);
    $params = [];
    $in = SpInList($ids, 'r', $params);
    $st = $db->prepare("
        UPDATE purchase_invoice_hdr AS h
           SET paid_amount = COALESCE((SELECT SUM(i.amount_paid) FROM supplier_payment_items i WHERE i.invoice_id = h.id), 0)
         WHERE h.id IN ($in)
    ");
    $st->execute($params);
}

/**
 * Saldo deposit supplier tidak boleh menjadi negatif akibat operasi ini.
 * $before = [supplier_id => saldo sebelum operasi]. Bila saldo sudah negatif sebelumnya dan
 * tidak bertambah buruk, operasi tetap diizinkan.
 */
function SpAssertDepositNotNegative($db, array $before) {
    foreach ($before as $sid => $b) {
        $after = GetSupplierDepositBalance($db, $sid);
        if ($after < -0.01 && $after < $b - 0.01) {
            throw new Exception("Saldo deposit supplier akan menjadi negatif (Rp " . number_format($after, 2) . "). Uang muka dari pembayaran ini sudah dipakai pembayaran lain -- batalkan/ubah pembayaran yang memakainya terlebih dahulu.");
        }
    }
}

## File: ./poCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/**
 * poCRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Backend Controller Purchase Order (po.js).
 * Pola sama dengan rabCRD.php: PDO (class crud), dispatch lewat $_REQUEST['task'],
 * respons JSON {success, message, data, totalCount}.
 *
 * Sumber data: purchase_orders, po_items, budget_items (RAB), materials,
 * m_suppliers, m_currency, grn_hdr/grn_dtl (qty diterima).
 *
 * Aturan:
 *  1. Item PO wajib dari RAB proyek tsb (po_items.budget_item_id -> budget_items.id).
 *  2. Total qty di-PO per baris RAB (semua PO non-Dibatalkan) tidak boleh melebihi
 *     qty_plan + toleransi RAB.
 *  3. PO hanya boleh diubah / dihapus saat status Draft.
 *  4. Alur status: Draft -> Diajukan -> Disetujui -> Selesai; Dibatalkan dari
 *     Draft/Diajukan/Disetujui selama belum ada barang diterima (GRN).
 *     Diajukan / Disetujui wajib punya supplier dan minimal 1 item.
 *  5. [MULTI CURRENCY PO] Header PO: mata uang + kurs PO secara keseluruhan (default IDR).
 *     Setiap item PO punya mata uang & kurs sendiri (currency_id, exch_rate di po_items):
 *     - Material beli lokal  => IDR, kurs 1
 *     - Material beli impor  => mis. USD + kurs KMK
 *     Butuh migrasi: sql/2026-10-02.po_item_currency.sql
 * ============================================================
 */

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
ob_start();

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}
header('Content-Type: application/json');

$db = new crud();

const PO_EPS = 0.0001;
const PO_TRANSITIONS = [
    'Draft'     => ['Diajukan', 'Dibatalkan'],
    'Diajukan'  => ['Disetujui', 'Draft', 'Dibatalkan'],
    'Disetujui' => ['Selesai', 'Dibatalkan'],
    'Selesai'   => [],
    'Dibatalkan' => [],
];

class PoBusinessError extends Exception {}

function sendJsonResponse($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    $response = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    if ($success) {
        if (is_array($data) && isset($data['data'])) {
            $response['data'] = $data['data'];
            if (isset($data['totalCount'])) $response['totalCount'] = $data['totalCount'];
        } else {
            $response['data'] = $data;
        }
    }
    if (ob_get_length()) { ob_clean(); }
    echo json_encode($response, JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
    exit;
}

function po_fmt($n): string {
    return rtrim(rtrim(number_format((float)$n, 3, '.', ''), '0'), '.');
}

/** Qty yang sudah di-PO per baris RAB proyek (PO Dibatalkan & PO $exclPoId tidak dihitung). */
function po_ordered_map($db, int $projectId, int $exclPoId = 0): array {
    $st = $db->prepare("
        SELECT poi.budget_item_id, SUM(poi.qty) AS q
          FROM po_items poi
          JOIN purchase_orders po ON po.id = poi.po_id
         WHERE po.project_id = :p AND po.status <> 'Dibatalkan' AND po.id <> :x AND poi.budget_item_id IS NOT NULL
         GROUP BY poi.budget_item_id");
    $st->execute([':p' => $projectId, ':x' => $exclPoId]);
    $map = [];
    foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $map[(int)$r['budget_item_id']] = (float)$r['q'];
    return $map;
}

/** Total qty yang sudah diterima (GRN) untuk satu PO. */
function po_received_total($db, int $poId): float {
    $st = $db->prepare("
        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(d.qty_received), 0)
          FROM grn_dtl d
          JOIN grn_hdr h ON h.id = d.grn_hdr_id
         WHERE h.po_id = :id AND COALESCE(LOWER(h.status), '') NOT IN ('cancelled','canceled','dibatalkan','void','reversed')");
    $st->execute([':id' => $poId]);
    return (float)$st->fetchColumn();
}

if (empty($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.');
}

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';

try {
    switch ($task) {

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Master untuk combo
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_project_options':
            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, name FROM public.projects ORDER BY name ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows);
            break;

        case 'list_suppliers':
            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, name FROM public.m_suppliers WHERE active IS NOT FALSE ORDER BY name ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows);
            break;

        case 'list_currencies':
            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, init, name FROM public.m_currency ORDER BY init ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Kurs default PO: kurs pajak KMK (tax_exchange_rate_*) pada tanggal PO.
         * Bila tanggal PO tidak masuk periode KMK mana pun -> pakai kurs terakhir.
         * Nilai = IDR per 1 unit mata uang (rate_per_unit; bila kosong rate / unit_multiplier).
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'get_exchange_rate':
            $cid  = (int)($_REQUEST['currency_id'] ?? 0);
            $date = (string)($_REQUEST['date'] ?? '');
            $dt   = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $date);
            if (!$dt || $dt->format('Y-m-d') !== $date) $date = date('Y-m-d');

            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT init FROM public.m_currency WHERE id = :id");
            $st->execute([':id' => $cid]);
            $curRow = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$curRow) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Mata uang tidak ditemukan.');
            $code = strtoupper(trim((string)$curRow['init']));

            if ($code === 'IDR') {
                sendJsonResponse(true, ['rate' => 1, 'source' => 'IDR']);
            }

            $base = "SELECT COALESCE(d.rate_per_unit, d.rate / NULLIF(d.unit_multiplier, 0)) AS rate,
                            h.kmk_no, h.start_date, h.end_date
                       FROM public.tax_exchange_rate_dtl d
                       JOIN public.tax_exchange_rate_hdr h ON h.id = d.hdr_id
                      WHERE (d.currency_id = :cid OR UPPER(d.currency_code) = :code)";

            // 1) periode KMK yang memuat tanggal PO
            $st = $db->prepare($base . " AND CAST(:d AS date) BETWEEN h.start_date AND h.end_date
                                          ORDER BY h.start_date DESC, h.id DESC LIMIT 1");
            $st->execute([':cid' => $cid, ':code' => $code, ':d' => $date]);
            $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            $source = 'periode';

            // 2) belum ada data untuk tanggal tsb -> kurs terakhir yang tersedia
            if (!$r || $r['rate'] === null) {
                $st = $db->prepare($base . " ORDER BY h.end_date DESC, h.id DESC LIMIT 1");
                $st->execute([':cid' => $cid, ':code' => $code]);
                $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                $source = 'terakhir';
            }

            if (!$r || $r['rate'] === null) {
                sendJsonResponse(true, ['rate' => null, 'source' => 'none']);
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, [
                'rate'       => round((float)$r['rate'], 4),
                'source'     => $source,
                'kmk_no'     => $r['kmk_no'],
                'start_date' => date('d/m/Y', strtotime($r['start_date'])),
                'end_date'   => date('d/m/Y', strtotime($r['end_date'])),
            ]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Baris RAB proyek + kuota yang masih boleh di-PO
         * (po_id = PO yang sedang diedit, agar qty miliknya sendiri tidak dihitung)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_rab_items':
            $prj  = (int)($_REQUEST['project_id'] ?? 0);
            $excl = (int)($_REQUEST['po_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($prj <= 0) sendJsonResponse(true, []);

            // Ambil info mata uang anggaran proyek supaya PO tahu default currency RAB
            $stPrjCur = $db->prepare("
                SELECT p.budget_currency_id,
                       COALESCE(UPPER(TRIM(cur.init)), 'IDR') AS rab_currency_code,
                       COALESCE(NULLIF(p.budget_exch_rate, 0), 1) AS rab_exch_rate
                  FROM public.projects p
                  LEFT JOIN public.m_currency cur ON cur.id = p.budget_currency_id
                 WHERE p.id = :p");
            $stPrjCur->execute([':p' => $prj]);
            $prjCur = $stPrjCur->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT bi.id AS budget_item_id, bi.material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name,
                       COALESCE(NULLIF(bi.unit, ''), m.unit, '') AS unit,
                       bi.qty_plan, COALESCE(bi.tolerance_pct, 0) AS tolerance_pct,
                       COALESCE(bi.unit_price_plan, 0) AS unit_price_plan
                  FROM public.budget_items bi
                  JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = bi.material_id
                 WHERE bi.project_id = :p AND bi.category = 'Material' AND COALESCE(bi.qty_plan, 0) > 0
                 ORDER BY m.material_name ASC");
            $st->execute([':p' => $prj]);
            $ordered = po_ordered_map($db, $prj, $excl);

            $data = [];
            foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) {
                $plan = (float)$r['qty_plan'];
                $ord  = $ordered[(int)$r['budget_item_id']] ?? 0.0;
                $data[] = [
                    'budget_item_id'     => (int)$r['budget_item_id'],
                    'material_id'        => (int)$r['material_id'],
                    'material_code'      => $r['material_code'],
                    'material_name'      => $r['material_name'],
                    'unit'               => $r['unit'],
                    'qty_plan'           => $plan,
                    'tolerance_pct'      => (float)$r['tolerance_pct'],
                    'unit_price_plan'    => (float)$r['unit_price_plan'],
                    'qty_ordered'        => $ord,
                    'qty_remaining'      => max($plan - $ord, 0),
                    'qty_quota'          => max($plan * (1 + (float)$r['tolerance_pct'] / 100) - $ord, 0),
                    // Info mata uang RAB (untuk saran default currency item)
                    'rab_currency_id'    => $prjCur ? (int)$prjCur['budget_currency_id'] : null,
                    'rab_currency_code'  => $prjCur ? $prjCur['rab_currency_code'] : 'IDR',
                    'rab_exch_rate'      => $prjCur ? (float)$prjCur['rab_exch_rate'] : 1.0,
                ];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, $data);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Daftar PO (grid utama)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_pos':
            $start = (int)($_REQUEST['start'] ?? 0);
            $limit = (int)($_REQUEST['limit'] ?? 250);
            $query = trim($_REQUEST['query'] ?? '');

            $where = ''; $params = [];
            if ($query !== '') {
                $where = "WHERE (po.po_number ILIKE :query OR p.name ILIKE :query OR s.name ILIKE :query)";
                $params[':query'] = '%' . $query . '%';
            }

            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*)
                                  FROM purchase_orders po
                                  JOIN public.projects p ON p.id = po.project_id
                                  LEFT JOIN public.m_suppliers s ON s.id = po.supplier_id {$where}");
            $st->execute($params);
            $totalCount = (int)$st->fetchColumn();

            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT po.id, po.po_number, po.po_date, po.project_id, p.name AS project_name,
                       po.supplier_id, s.name AS supplier_name, po.status, po.notes,
                       po.currency_id, c.init AS currency_code, po.exch_rate,
                       COUNT(i.id) AS item_count,
                       COALESCE(SUM(i.qty * i.unit_price), 0) AS total_amount
                  FROM purchase_orders po
                  JOIN public.projects p ON p.id = po.project_id
                  LEFT JOIN public.m_suppliers s ON s.id = po.supplier_id
                  LEFT JOIN public.m_currency c ON c.id = po.currency_id
                  LEFT JOIN po_items i ON i.po_id = po.id
                  {$where}
                 GROUP BY po.id, p.name, s.name, c.init
                 ORDER BY po.po_date DESC, po.id DESC
                 LIMIT :limit OFFSET :start");
            foreach ($params as $k => $v) $st->bindValue($k, $v);
            $st->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $st->bindValue(':start', $start, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $st->execute();
            $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
                $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
                $r['project_id'] = (int)$r['project_id'];
                $r['supplier_id'] = $r['supplier_id'] !== null ? (int)$r['supplier_id'] : null;
                $r['currency_id'] = $r['currency_id'] !== null ? (int)$r['currency_id'] : null;
                $r['exch_rate'] = (float)$r['exch_rate'];
                $r['item_count'] = (int)$r['item_count'];
                $r['total_amount'] = (float)$r['total_amount'];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, ['data' => $rows, 'totalCount' => $totalCount]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Detail PO (header + item + qty diterima + aksi status yang diizinkan)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'load_po':
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT po.id, po.po_number, po.po_date, po.project_id, p.name AS project_name,
                       po.supplier_id, s.name AS supplier_name, po.status, po.notes,
                       po.currency_id, c.init AS currency_code, po.exch_rate
                  FROM purchase_orders po
                  JOIN public.projects p ON p.id = po.project_id
                  LEFT JOIN public.m_suppliers s ON s.id = po.supplier_id
                  LEFT JOIN public.m_currency c ON c.id = po.currency_id
                 WHERE po.id = :id");
            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
            $hdr = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$hdr) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'PO tidak ditemukan.');
            $hdr['exch_rate'] = (float)$hdr['exch_rate'];

            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT i.id, i.budget_item_id, i.material_id, m.material_code, i.description, i.unit,
                       i.qty, i.unit_price,
                       i.currency_id, COALESCE(UPPER(TRIM(ic.init)), 'IDR') AS item_currency_code,
                       COALESCE(NULLIF(i.exch_rate, 0), 1) AS exch_rate,
                       COALESCE(g.q, 0) AS qty_received
                  FROM po_items i
                  LEFT JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = i.material_id
                  LEFT JOIN public.m_currency ic ON ic.id = i.currency_id
                  LEFT JOIN (
                        SELECT d.po_item_id, SUM(d.qty_received) AS q
                          FROM grn_dtl d JOIN grn_hdr h ON h.id = d.grn_hdr_id
                         WHERE COALESCE(LOWER(h.status), '') NOT IN ('cancelled','canceled','dibatalkan','void','reversed')
                         GROUP BY d.po_item_id
                   ) g ON g.po_item_id = i.id
                 WHERE i.po_id = :id ORDER BY i.id");
            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
            $items = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            $received = 0.0;
            foreach ($items as &$it) {
                $it['id'] = (int)$it['id'];
                $it['budget_item_id'] = $it['budget_item_id'] !== null ? (int)$it['budget_item_id'] : null;
                $it['material_id'] = $it['material_id'] !== null ? (int)$it['material_id'] : null;
                $it['currency_id'] = $it['currency_id'] !== null ? (int)$it['currency_id'] : null;
                $it['qty'] = (float)$it['qty'];
                $it['unit_price'] = (float)$it['unit_price'];
                $it['exch_rate'] = (float)$it['exch_rate'];
                $it['qty_received'] = (float)$it['qty_received'];
                $received += $it['qty_received'];
            }

            $allowed = PO_TRANSITIONS[$hdr['status']] ?? [];
            if ($received > PO_EPS) {      // sudah ada barang diterima -> tidak boleh dibatalkan / dikembalikan
                $allowed = array_values(array_diff($allowed, ['Dibatalkan', 'Draft']));
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, ['header' => $hdr, 'items' => $items, 'allowed_actions' => $allowed]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Simpan PO (baru / ubah). Hanya status Draft yang boleh diubah.
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'save_po':
            $id    = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $prj   = (int)($_POST['project_id'] ?? 0);
            $sup   = (int)($_POST['supplier_id'] ?? 0) ?: null;
            $cur   = (int)($_POST['currency_id'] ?? 0) ?: null;
            $rate  = (float)($_POST['exch_rate'] ?? 1);
            $date  = (string)($_POST['po_date'] ?? '');
            $notes = trim((string)($_POST['notes'] ?? '')) ?: null;
            $items = json_decode($_POST['items'] ?? '[]', true);
            if ($rate <= 0) $rate = 1;

            try {
                $d = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $date);
                if (!$d || $d->format('Y-m-d') !== $date) throw new PoBusinessError('Tanggal PO tidak valid.');
                if ($prj <= 0) throw new PoBusinessError('Proyek wajib dipilih.');
                if (!is_array($items) || !count($items)) throw new PoBusinessError('Minimal satu material harus diisi.');

                $db->beginTransaction();
                try {
                    if ($id > 0) {
                        $st = $db->prepare("SELECT status FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
                        $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
                        $cur0 = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                        if (!$cur0) throw new PoBusinessError('PO tidak ditemukan.');
                        if ($cur0['status'] !== 'Draft') throw new PoBusinessError('Hanya PO berstatus Draft yang dapat diubah.');
                    }

                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.projects WHERE id = :id");
                    $st->execute([':id' => $prj]);
                    if (!$st->fetchColumn()) throw new PoBusinessError('Proyek tidak ditemukan.');
                    if ($sup) {
                        $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.m_suppliers WHERE id = :id");
                        $st->execute([':id' => $sup]);
                        if (!$st->fetchColumn()) throw new PoBusinessError('Supplier tidak ditemukan.');
                    }

                    if ($cur) {                       // IDR selalu berkurs 1, apa pun yang dikirim klien
                        $st = $db->prepare("SELECT UPPER(TRIM(init)) FROM public.m_currency WHERE id = :id");
                        $st->execute([':id' => $cur]);
                        if ($st->fetchColumn() === 'IDR') $rate = 1;
                    }

                    // Validasi item terhadap RAB proyek
                    $ordered = po_ordered_map($db, $prj, $id);
                    $stBi = $db->prepare("
                        SELECT bi.id, bi.material_id, bi.qty_plan, COALESCE(bi.tolerance_pct, 0) AS tol,
                               m.material_name, COALESCE(NULLIF(bi.unit, ''), m.unit, '') AS unit
                          FROM public.budget_items bi JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = bi.material_id
                         WHERE bi.id = :id AND bi.project_id = :p AND bi.category = 'Material'");
                    $tally = []; $clean = [];
                    foreach ($items as $n => $it) {
                        $bid      = (int)($it['budget_item_id'] ?? 0);
                        $qty      = round((float)($it['qty'] ?? 0), 3);
                        $price    = round((float)($it['unit_price'] ?? 0), 2);
                        $itemCur  = (int)($it['item_currency_id'] ?? 0) ?: null;
                        $itemRate = (float)($it['item_exch_rate'] ?? 1);
                        $no       = 'Baris ' . ($n + 1);
                        if ($bid <= 0 || $qty <= 0) throw new PoBusinessError("{$no}: material dan qty (> 0) wajib diisi.");
                        if ($price < 0) throw new PoBusinessError("{$no}: harga satuan tidak valid.");

                        // Validasi item_currency_id
                        if ($itemCur) {
                            $stCk = $db->prepare("SELECT UPPER(TRIM(init)) FROM public.m_currency WHERE id = :id");
                            $stCk->execute([':id' => $itemCur]);
                            $curCode = $stCk->fetchColumn();
                            if (!$curCode) throw new PoBusinessError("{$no}: mata uang item tidak ditemukan.");
                            if ($curCode === 'IDR') $itemRate = 1.0;
                        } else {
                            $itemRate = 1.0; // default IDR
                        }
                        if ($itemRate <= 0) $itemRate = 1.0;

                        $stBi->execute([':id' => $bid, ':p' => $prj]);
                        $bi = $stBi->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                        if (!$bi) throw new PoBusinessError("{$no}: material tidak ada di RAB proyek ini.");

                        $tally[$bid] = ($tally[$bid] ?? 0) + $qty;
                        $limit = (float)$bi['qty_plan'] * (1 + (float)$bi['tol'] / 100);
                        $ord   = $ordered[$bid] ?? 0.0;
                        if ($ord + $tally[$bid] > $limit + PO_EPS) {
                            throw new PoBusinessError(
                                "{$bi['material_name']}: qty PO " . po_fmt($tally[$bid]) . " melebihi sisa kuota RAB (" .
                                po_fmt(max($limit - $ord, 0)) . " {$bi['unit']}). Ubah RAB bila memang perlu tambah."
                            );
                        }
                        $clean[] = ['bid' => $bid, 'mid' => (int)$bi['material_id'], 'name' => $bi['material_name'],
                                    'unit' => $bi['unit'], 'qty' => $qty, 'price' => $price,
                                    'item_cur' => $itemCur, 'item_rate' => $itemRate];
                    }

                    if ($id > 0) {
                        $st = $db->prepare("
                            UPDATE purchase_orders
                               SET project_id = :p, supplier_id = :s, po_date = CAST(:d AS date), notes = :n,
                                   currency_id = :c, exch_rate = :r, updated_at = NOW()
                             WHERE id = :id");
                        $st->execute([':p' => $prj, ':s' => $sup, ':d' => $date, ':n' => $notes, ':c' => $cur, ':r' => $rate, ':id' => $id]);
                        $poNumber = $db->query("SELECT po_number FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = " . $id)->fetchColumn();
                        $db->prepare("DELETE FROM po_items WHERE po_id = :id")->execute([':id' => $id]);
                    } else {
                        if (!function_exists('CreateRefNo')) throw new Exception('CreateRefNo() tidak tersedia.');
                        $poNumber = CreateRefNo('purchase_orders', 'po_number', 'PO');
                        $st = $db->prepare("
                            INSERT INTO purchase_orders
                                (po_number, project_id, supplier_id, po_date, status, notes, currency_id, exch_rate)
                            VALUES (:no, :p, :s, CAST(:d AS date), 'Draft', :n, :c, :r)
                            RETURNING id");
                        $st->execute([':no' => $poNumber, ':p' => $prj, ':s' => $sup, ':d' => $date, ':n' => $notes, ':c' => $cur, ':r' => $rate]);
                        $id = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
                    }

                    $stIns = $db->prepare("
                        INSERT INTO po_items (po_id, budget_item_id, material_id, description, qty, unit, unit_price, currency_id, exch_rate)
                        VALUES (:po, :b, :m, :desc, :q, :u, :pr, :ic, :ir)");
                    foreach ($clean as $c) {
                        $stIns->execute([':po' => $id, ':b' => $c['bid'], ':m' => $c['mid'], ':desc' => $c['name'],
                                         ':q' => $c['qty'], ':u' => $c['unit'], ':pr' => $c['price'],
                                         ':ic' => $c['item_cur'], ':ir' => $c['item_rate']]);
                    }

                    $db->commit();
                    sendJsonResponse(true, ['id' => $id, 'po_number' => $poNumber], "PO {$poNumber} berhasil disimpan.");
                } catch (Throwable $e) {
                    if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                    throw $e;
                }
            } catch (PoBusinessError $e) {
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            }
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Ubah status PO
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'change_status':
            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $to = (string)($_POST['to'] ?? '');
            $db->beginTransaction();
            try {
                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT po_number, status, supplier_id FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
                $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
                $po = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                if (!$po) throw new PoBusinessError('PO tidak ditemukan.');
                if (!in_array($to, PO_TRANSITIONS[$po['status']] ?? [], true)) {
                    throw new PoBusinessError("Status {$po['status']} tidak dapat diubah ke {$to}.");
                }
                if (in_array($to, ['Dibatalkan', 'Draft'], true) && po_received_total($db, $id) > PO_EPS) {
                    throw new PoBusinessError('PO ini sudah ada barang yang diterima (GRN), tidak dapat dibatalkan / dikembalikan ke Draft.');
                }
                if (in_array($to, ['Diajukan', 'Disetujui'], true)) {
