                    if (!$po['supplier_id']) throw new PoBusinessError('Supplier wajib diisi sebelum PO diajukan / disetujui.');
                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM po_items WHERE po_id = :id");
                    $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
                    if ((int)$st->fetchColumn() === 0) throw new PoBusinessError('PO belum memiliki item.');
                }
                $st = $db->prepare("UPDATE purchase_orders SET status = :s, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = :id");
                $st->execute([':s' => $to, ':id' => $id]);
                $db->commit();
                sendJsonResponse(true, [], "PO {$po['po_number']} sekarang berstatus {$to}.");
            } catch (PoBusinessError $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                throw $e;
            }
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Hapus PO (hanya Draft, belum dipakai GRN)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'delete_po':
            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $db->beginTransaction();
            try {
                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT po_number, status FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
                $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
                $po = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                if (!$po) throw new PoBusinessError('PO tidak ditemukan.');
                if ($po['status'] !== 'Draft') throw new PoBusinessError('Hanya PO berstatus Draft yang dapat dihapus. Gunakan Batalkan untuk PO lain.');

                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM grn_hdr WHERE po_id = :id");
                $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
                if ((int)$st->fetchColumn() > 0) throw new PoBusinessError('PO sudah memiliki GRN, tidak dapat dihapus.');

                $db->prepare("DELETE FROM po_items WHERE po_id = :id")->execute([':id' => $id]);
                $db->prepare("DELETE FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = :id")->execute([':id' => $id]);
                $db->commit();
                sendJsonResponse(true, [], "PO {$po['po_number']} dihapus.");
            } catch (PoBusinessError $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                throw $e;
            }
            break;

        default:
            sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Aksi/Task tidak dikenal.');
            break;
    }
} catch (PDOException $e) {
    error_log('[poCRD] ' . $e->getMessage());
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Database Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    error_log('[poCRD] ' . $e->getMessage());
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'System Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
}

      file_put_contents($dirPhoto.$photoName, $foto);
      $table = "m_employee";
      $data = array(
        "photo"     => $_POST['photo'],
        "photo_url" => $dirPhoto.$photoName
      );
      $where = "id = " . intval($_POST["id"]);
      $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";
    }

    $db = new crud();
    $db->cmdQ($queryC);
    echo $db->getResult();
  break;

  // ==========================================
  // 6. DELETE
  // ==========================================
  case ('delete'):
    $queryC = '';
    $table = "m_employee";
    $data = array("active" => 'false');
    $where = "id = " . intval($_POST["id"]);
    $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";

    $db->cmdQ($queryC);
    echo $db->getResult();
  break;

  // ==========================================
  // 7. READ EMPLOYEE (Dropdown Combo / Lookup Store)
  // ==========================================
  case ('readEmployee'):
    $db = new crud();
    $field = "id employee_id, nik, employee_name, location_name, department_name, section_name";
    $table = "mat_view_employee_active";
    $where = "1=1";
    $order = "employee_name ASC";

    // Perbaikan: Gunakan method select() standar
    $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
    echo $db->getResult();
  break;

  default:
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Task tidak dikenali."]);
  break;
}

## File: ./clientsCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/**
 * clientsCRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Backend Controller untuk Master Klien (public.clients).
 * Dipulihkan untuk clients.js -- kontrak task & field mengikuti
 * persis apa yang sudah dipanggil di JS tsb:
 *   list, read_edit, create, update, delete
 *
 * Catatan: projects.client_id punya ON DELETE SET NULL, jadi hapus
 * klien yang masih dipakai proyek AMAN di level database (proyeknya
 * tidak ikut terhapus, cuma client_id-nya jadi kosong) -- sesuai
 * peringatan yang sudah ditampilkan clients.js sebelum submit delete.
 * ============================================================
 */

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

session_start();
header('Content-Type: application/json');

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';
$db   = new crud();

function PrepOrFailCL($stmt, $context = '') {
    if ($stmt === false) {
        throw new Exception(($context ? "[{$context}] " : '') . 'Query gagal dijalankan.');
    }
    return $stmt;
}
function clFetchAll($db, $sql, array $params = [], $ctx = '') {
    $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
    PrepOrFailCL($stmt, $ctx);
    $stmt->execute($params);
    return $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
}
function clFetchOne($db, $sql, array $params = [], $ctx = '') {
    $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
    PrepOrFailCL($stmt, $ctx);
    $stmt->execute($params);
    $row = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
    return $row === false ? null : $row;
}
function clExec($db, $sql, array $params = [], $ctx = '') {
    $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
    PrepOrFailCL($stmt, $ctx);
    $stmt->execute($params);
    return $stmt;
}

$access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);

try {
    switch ($task) {

        case 'list':
            $sql = "SELECT c.id, c.name, c.contact_person, c.phone, c.email, c.address, c.created_at,
                           (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects p WHERE p.client_id = c.id) AS project_count
                    FROM clients c
                    ORDER BY c.name ASC";
            $data = clFetchAll($db, $sql, [], 'list');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
            break;

        case 'read_edit':
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            $row = clFetchOne($db, "SELECT * FROM clients WHERE id = :id", ['id' => $id], 'read_edit');
            if (!$row) throw new Exception('Data klien tidak ditemukan.');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $row]);
            break;

        // --------------------------------------------------------------
        case 'create':
        case 'update':
            if ($task === 'create' && $access < 2) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk menambah klien.']);
                exit;
            }
            if ($task === 'update' && $access < 3) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk mengubah klien.']);
                exit;
            }

            $id             = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            $name           = trim($_REQUEST['name'] ?? '');
            $contact_person = trim($_REQUEST['contact_person'] ?? '');
            $phone          = trim($_REQUEST['phone'] ?? '');
            $email          = trim($_REQUEST['email'] ?? '');
            $address        = $_REQUEST['address'] ?? '';

            if ($name === '') throw new Exception('Nama Klien wajib diisi.');
            if ($email !== '' && !filter_var($email, FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) {
                throw new Exception('Format email tidak valid.');
            }

            if ($task === 'create') {
                $newId = (int)clFetchOne($db,
                    "INSERT INTO clients (name, contact_person, phone, email, address, created_at)
                     VALUES (:name, :cp, :phone, :email, :address, NOW())
                     RETURNING id",
                    ['name' => $name, 'cp' => $contact_person, 'phone' => $phone, 'email' => $email, 'address' => $address],
                    'insert clients')['id'];
                echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'id' => $newId, 'name' => $name, 'message' => "Klien '{$name}' berhasil ditambahkan."]);
            } else {
                if (!$id) throw new Exception('ID klien tidak valid.');
                clExec($db,
                    "UPDATE clients SET name = :name, contact_person = :cp, phone = :phone, email = :email, address = :address
                     WHERE id = :id",
                    ['name' => $name, 'cp' => $contact_person, 'phone' => $phone, 'email' => $email, 'address' => $address, 'id' => $id],
                    'update clients');
                echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'name' => $name, 'message' => "Klien '{$name}' berhasil diperbarui."]);
            }
            break;

        case 'delete':
            if ($access < 4) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk menghapus klien.']);
                exit;
            }
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            if (!$id) throw new Exception('ID klien tidak valid.');

            clExec($db, "DELETE FROM clients WHERE id = :id", ['id' => $id], 'delete clients');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Klien berhasil dihapus.']);
            break;

        default:
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
            break;
    }
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage() !== '' ? $e->getMessage() : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.']);
}

## File: ./m.customerCRD.php

<?php
if (!isset($_SESSION)) {
    session_start();
}
date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Jakarta');

include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";

// 1. Amankan penangkapan parameter POST dengan operator ??
$searchField = $_POST["query"] ?? null;
$offset      = isset($_POST["start"]) ? intval($_POST["start"]) : 0;
$limit       = isset($_POST["limit"]) ? intval($_POST["limit"]) : 250;

// 2. Amankan proses json2Array
$rawItemInfo = $_POST["iteminfo"] ?? '';
$dataArray   = !empty($rawItemInfo) ? json2Array($rawItemInfo) : [];

// 3. Tangani pembentukan string kondisi aman berbasis array
if (empty($dataArray)) {
    $CheckItemNum  = "[0]";
    $CheckItemChar = "['0']";
} else {
    $formattedData = is_array($dataArray) ? implode(",", $dataArray) : $dataArray;
    $CheckItemNum  = "[" . $formattedData . "]";
    $CheckItemChar = "['" . $formattedData . "']";
}

$db = new crud();
$task = $_POST['task'] ?? 'read';

switch ($task) {

    // ==========================================
    // 1. CREATE (Tambah Customer Lengkap)
    // ==========================================
    case ("create"):
    case ("add"):
        $queryC = '';

        // Master Customer
        $table = "m_customer";
        $data = array(
            "name"   => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? ''),
            "init"   => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),
            "remark" => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? ''),
            "active" => 'true'
        );
        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

        // Alamat Customer Utama
        $table = "m_address";
        $isCustType = isset($_POST["cust_type"]) ? ($_POST["cust_type"] === true || $_POST["cust_type"] === 'true' || $_POST["cust_type"] === '1') : false;
        $countryId  = $isCustType ? 1 : intval($_POST["country"] ?? 1);

        $data = array(
            "customer_id" => "currval('m_customer_id_seq'::regclass)",
            "active"      => 'true',
            "address"     => checksinglequote($_POST["address"] ?? ''),
            "country_id"  => $countryId
        );
        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

        // Data NPWP jika WNI (cust_type true)
        if ($isCustType) {
            $table = "customer_npwp";
            $data = array(
                "npwp_no"        => checksinglequote($_POST["npwp"] ?? ''),
                "active"         => 'true',
                "customer_id"    => "currval('m_customer_id_seq'::regclass)",
                "address_office" => !empty($_POST["ass_addr_off"]) ? checksinglequote($_POST["ass_addr_off"]) : null
            );
            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

            // Alamat NPWP
            $table = "m_address";
            $data = array(
                "npwp_id"    => "currval('customer_npwp_id_seq'::regclass)",
                "active"     => 'true',
                "street"     => checksinglequote($_POST["street"] ?? ''),
                "block"      => checksinglequote($_POST["block"] ?? ''),
                "street_no"  => checksinglequote($_POST["street_no"] ?? ''),
                "rt"         => checksinglequote($_POST["rt"] ?? ''),
                "rw"         => checksinglequote($_POST["rw"] ?? ''),
                "village_id" => (empty($_POST["village"]) || $_POST["village"] == 0) ? null : intval($_POST["village"]),
                "country_id" => 1
            );
            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
        }

        // Limit Kredit
        $limitCurrency = intval($_POST["limit_currency"] ?? 0);
        $limitAmount   = floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["limit_amount"] ?? 0));
        if ($limitCurrency > 0 && $limitAmount > 0) {
            $table = "customer_credit_limit";
            $data = array(
                "customer_id"  => "currval('m_customer_id_seq'::regclass)",
                "limit_amount" => $limitAmount,
                "currency_id"  => $limitCurrency,
                "from_date"    => "now()",
                "to_date"      => null
            );
            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
        }

        // Periode Pembayaran
        $paymentPeriod = floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["payment_period"] ?? 0));
        if ($paymentPeriod > 0) {
            $table = "customer_payment_period";
            $data = array(
                "customer_id" => "currval('m_customer_id_seq'::regclass)",
                "period"      => $paymentPeriod,
                "from_date"   => "now()",
                "to_date"     => null
            );
            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
        }

        // Kontak Kantor (Email, Mobile, Phone, Fax)
        $contactsConfig = [
            "office_email"  => 1,
            "office_mobile" => 2,
            "office_phone"  => 3,
            "office_fax"    => 4
        ];

        foreach ($contactsConfig as $postKey => $contactType) {
            if (!empty($_POST[$postKey])) {
                $table = "m_contact_no";
                $data = array(
                    "customer_id"       => "currval('m_customer_id_seq'::regclass)",
                    "supplier_id"       => null,
                    "contact_person_id" => null,
                    "contact_type"      => $contactType,
                    "contact_no"        => checksinglequote($_POST[$postKey])
                );
                $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
            }
        }

        // Array Kontak Person Tambahan
        $rawContacts  = $_POST["arrContact"] ?? '[]';
        $dataContacts = !empty($rawContacts) ? json_decode($rawContacts) : [];

        if (is_array($dataContacts)) {
            foreach ($dataContacts as $contact) {
                if (!empty($contact->name)) {
                    $table = "m_contact_person";
                    $data = array(
                        "customer_id" => "currval('m_customer_id_seq'::regclass)",
                        "supplier_id" => null,
                        "name"        => checksinglequote($contact->name ?? ''),
                        "position"    => checksinglequote($contact->position ?? ''),
                        "remark"      => checksinglequote($contact->remark ?? '')
                    );
                    $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

                    if (!empty($contact->address)) {
                        $table = "m_address";
                        $data = array(
                            "contact_person_id" => "currval('m_contact_person_id_seq'::regclass)",
                            "active"            => 'true',
                            "address"           => checksinglequote($contact->address)
                        );
                        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                    }

                    $subContactsMap = [
                        "email"  => 1,
                        "mobile" => 2,
                        "phone"  => 3
                    ];

                    foreach ($subContactsMap as $sKey => $sVal) {
                        if (!empty($contact->$sKey)) {
                            $table = "m_contact_no";
                            $data = array(
                                "contact_type"      => $sVal,
                                "contact_person_id" => "currval('m_contact_person_id_seq'::regclass)",
                                "contact_no"        => checksinglequote($contact->$sKey)
                            );
                            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        $db->cmdQ($queryC);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 2. READ (Daftar Customer)
    // ==========================================
    case ("read"):
        $queryParam = $_POST["query"] ?? '';
        $sResult    = searchData($queryParam, array("c.name", "c.init", "a.address"), $limit);
        $searchSQL  = is_array($sResult["searchSQL"]) ? '' : strval($sResult["searchSQL"]);

        $fields = "
            c.id, cn.id country, c.init, c.name, coalesce(c.remark,'') remark,
            replace(coalesce(a.address,''),'\n','<br>') address, n.npwp_no npwp,
            anp.street, anp.block, anp.street_no, anp.rt, anp.rw, anp.village_id, anp.address npwp_addr, anp.country_id,
            l.limit_amount, l.currency_id, cr.init || '' || to_char(l.limit_amount, '999,999,999,999.99') cr_limit,
            cnt.contact_name,
            case when lower(cn.name)='indonesia' then 1 else 2 end cst_type
        ";

        $table = "
            m_customer c
            left join m_address a on a.customer_id=c.id and a.active is true
            left join m_country cn on cn.id=a.country_id
            left join customer_npwp n on n.customer_id=c.id and n.active is true
            left join m_address anp on anp.npwp_id=n.id and anp.active is true
            left join customer_credit_limit l on l.customer_id=c.id and l.to_date is null
            left join m_currency cr on cr.id=l.currency_id
            left join (
                select customer_id, array_to_string(array_agg(name || ' (' || position || ')'), '<br>') contact_name
                from m_contact_person
                where customer_id > 0
                group by customer_id
            ) cnt on coalesce(cnt.customer_id, 0) = coalesce(c.id, 0)
        ";

        $where = "c.active is true " . $searchSQL;
        $order = "c.name ASC";

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 3. READ EDIT (Ambil Data Detail Customer)
    // ==========================================
    case ("readedit"):
    case ("read_edit"):
        $idCustomer = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

        $fields = "
            c.id, cn.id country, c.init, c.name, coalesce(c.remark,'') remark, a.id address_id, a.address,
            n.id npwp_id, n.npwp_no npwp, n.address_office as_addr, anp.id npwp_address_id,
            anp.street, anp.block, anp.street_no, anp.rt, anp.rw, anp.address npwp_addr, anp.country_id,
            anp.village_id village, nv.district, nv.city, nv.province, nv.postalcode postal_code,
            l.id customer_credit_limit_id, l.limit_amount, l.currency_id limit_currency,
            p.id payment_period_id, p.period payment_period,
            cnt.office_email_id, cnt.office_email, cnt.office_mobile_id, cnt.office_mobile,
            cnt.office_phone_id, cnt.office_phone, cnt.office_fax_id, cnt.office_fax,
            case when lower(cn.name)='indonesia' then 1 else 2 end cst_type
        ";

        $table = "
            m_customer c
            left join m_address a on a.customer_id=c.id and a.active is true
            left join m_country cn on cn.id=a.country_id
            left join customer_npwp n on n.customer_id=c.id and n.active is true
            left join m_address anp on anp.npwp_id=n.id and anp.active is true
            left join customer_credit_limit l on l.customer_id=c.id and l.to_date is null
            left join customer_payment_period p on p.customer_id=c.id and p.to_date is null
            left join m_currency cr on cr.id=l.currency_id
            left join (
                select customer_id,
                max(case when contact_type = 1 then id end) as office_email_id,
                max(case when contact_type = 1 then contact_no end) as office_email,
                max(case when contact_type = 2 then id end) as office_mobile_id,
                max(case when contact_type = 2 then contact_no end) as office_mobile,
                max(case when contact_type = 3 then id end) as office_phone_id,
                max(case when contact_type = 3 then contact_no end) as office_phone,
                max(case when contact_type = 4 then id end) as office_fax_id,
                max(case when contact_type = 4 then contact_no end) as office_fax
                from m_contact_no
                where customer_id > 0
                group by customer_id
            ) cnt on coalesce(cnt.customer_id, 0) = coalesce(c.id, 0)
            left join (
                select v.id village, v.postalcode, d.id district, c.id city, p.id province
                from m_village v
                left join m_district d on d.id=v.district_id
                left join m_city c on c.id=d.city_id
                left join m_province p on p.id=c.province_id
            ) nv on nv.village = anp.village_id
        ";

        $where = "c.id = " . $idCustomer;

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 4. WILAYAH & LOOKUP (Country, Prov, City, etc)
    // ==========================================
    case ("readcountry"):
        $db->select("m_country", "id, name", "1=1", "name ASC");
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ("readprov"):
        $countryId = intval($_POST['country_id'] ?? 0);
        $db->select("m_province", "id, name", "country_id = " . $countryId, "name ASC");
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ("readcity"):
        $provinceId = intval($_POST['province_id'] ?? 0);
        $db->select("m_city", "id, name", "province_id = " . $provinceId, "name ASC");
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ("readdistrict"):
        $cityId = intval($_POST['city_id'] ?? 0);
        $db->select("m_district", "id, name", "city_id = " . $cityId, "name ASC");
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ("readvillage"):
        $districtId = intval($_POST['district_id'] ?? 0);
        $db->select("m_village", "id, name, postalcode", "district_id = " . $districtId, "name ASC");
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ('readcontact'):
        $customerId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $fields = "
            p.id cont_id, p.customer_id, p.name, p.position, coalesce(p.remark,'') remark, a.id cont_address_id, a.address,
            cnt.cont_email_id, cnt.email, cnt.cont_mobile_id, cnt.mobile, cnt.cont_phone_id, cnt.phone, cdt.contact
        ";
        $table = "
            m_contact_person p
            left join m_address a on a.contact_person_id = p.id and a.active is true
            left join (
                select contact_person_id,
                    max(case when contact_type = 1 then id end) as cont_email_id,
                    max(case when contact_type = 1 then contact_no end) as email,
                    max(case when contact_type = 2 then id end) as cont_mobile_id,
                    max(case when contact_type = 2 then contact_no end) as mobile,
                    max(case when contact_type = 3 then id end) as cont_phone_id,
                    max(case when contact_type = 3 then contact_no end) as phone
                from m_contact_no
                where contact_person_id > 0
                group by contact_person_id
            ) cnt on coalesce(cnt.contact_person_id, 0) = coalesce(p.id, 0)
            left join (
                select contact_person_id, array_to_string(array_agg(
                    case
                        when contact_type=1 then 'Email'
                        when contact_type=2 then 'Mobile'
                        when contact_type=3 then 'Phone'
                        when contact_type=4 then 'Fax'
                    end
                    || ' : ' || contact_no), '<br>') contact
                from m_contact_no
                where contact_person_id > 0
                group by contact_person_id
            ) cdt on coalesce(cdt.contact_person_id, 0) = coalesce(p.id, 0)
        ";
        $where = "p.customer_id = " . $customerId;
        $order = "p.name ASC";

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 5. HISTORY DATA LOG
    // ==========================================
    case ('HistoryData'):
        $txId   = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $fields = "
            tx_id, date, count,
            case amend_name
                when 'init' then '[EDIT] - Init'
                when 'name' then '[EDIT] - Name'
                when 'description' then '[EDIT] - Description'
                when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
                else amend_name
            end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
            before_amendment, after_amendment, user_name, user_time
        ";
        $where   = "tx_id::int = " . $txId . " and table_name = 'm_customer'";
        $orderBy = "count desc";

        $db->select('history', "view_single_table_log", $fields, $where, $orderBy);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 6. UPDATE (Edit Customer)
    // ==========================================
    case ('update'):
    case ('edit'):
        $queryC = '';
        $customerId = intval($_POST["id"] ?? 0);
        $rawContacts = $_POST["arrContact"] ?? '[]';
        $dataContacts = !empty($rawContacts) ? json_decode($rawContacts) : [];

        if ($customerId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID Customer tidak valid."]);
            break;
        }

        // Cek kontak person lama yang perlu dihapus
        $checkDb = new crud();
        $checkDb->select("m_contact_person", "id, address_id", "customer_id = " . $customerId, "id ASC");
        $resCheckJSON = $checkDb->getResult();
        $resCheckArr  = json_decode($resCheckJSON, true);
        $dataCheck    = $resCheckArr["data"] ?? [];

        foreach ($dataCheck as $rowOld) {
            $dataFound = false;
            $cId    = $rowOld['id'];
            $addrId = $rowOld['address_id'] ?? 0;

            if (is_array($dataContacts)) {
                foreach ($dataContacts as $contact) {
                    if (isset($contact->cont_id) && $contact->cont_id == $cId) {
                        $dataFound = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!$dataFound) {
                if ($addrId > 0) {
                    $queryC .= deleteQ("m_address", "id = " . intval($addrId)) . ";";
                }
                $queryC .= deleteQ("m_contact_no", "contact_person_id = " . intval($cId)) . ";";
                $queryC .= deleteQ("m_contact_person", "id = " . intval($cId)) . ";";
            }
        }

        // Update Master Customer
        $table = "m_customer";
        $data = array(
            "name"   => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? ''),
            "init"   => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),
            "remark" => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? '')
        );
        $where = "id = " . $customerId;
        $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";

        // Update Alamat Customer
        $addressId  = intval($_POST["address_id"] ?? 0);
        $newAddress = checksinglequote($_POST["address"] ?? '');
        $custType   = $_POST["cust_type"] ?? false;
        $isCustType = ($custType === true || $custType === 'true' || $custType === '1');
        $countryId  = $isCustType ? 1 : intval($_POST["country"] ?? 1);

        if ($addressId > 0) {
            $queryC .= updateQ("m_address", array(
                "address"    => $newAddress,
                "country_id" => $countryId
            ), "id = " . $addressId) . ";";
        } else if (!empty($newAddress)) {
            $queryC .= insertQ("m_address", array(
                "customer_id" => $customerId,
                "active"      => 'true',
                "address"     => $newAddress,
                "country_id"  => $countryId
            )) . ";";
        }

        // Update NPWP jika WNI
        if ($isCustType) {
            $npwpId  = intval($_POST["npwp_id"] ?? 0);
            $npwpNo  = checksinglequote($_POST["npwp"] ?? '');
            $asOffice = !empty($_POST["ass_addr_off"]) ? checksinglequote($_POST["ass_addr_off"]) : null;

            if ($npwpId > 0) {
                $queryC .= updateQ("customer_npwp", array(
                    "npwp_no"        => $npwpNo,
                    "address_office" => $asOffice
                ), "id = " . $npwpId) . ";";

                $npwpAddrId = intval($_POST["npwp_address_id"] ?? 0);
                if ($npwpAddrId > 0) {
                    $queryC .= updateQ("m_address", array(
                        "street"     => checksinglequote($_POST["street"] ?? ''),
                        "block"      => checksinglequote($_POST["block"] ?? ''),
                        "street_no"  => checksinglequote($_POST["street_no"] ?? ''),
                        "rt"         => checksinglequote($_POST["rt"] ?? ''),
                        "rw"         => checksinglequote($_POST["rw"] ?? ''),
                        "village_id" => (empty($_POST["village"]) || $_POST["village"] == 0) ? null : intval($_POST["village"])
                    ), "id = " . $npwpAddrId) . ";";
                }
            } else if (!empty($npwpNo)) {
                $queryC .= insertQ("customer_npwp", array(
                    "npwp_no"        => $npwpNo,
                    "active"         => 'true',
                    "customer_id"    => $customerId,
                    "address_office" => $asOffice
                )) . ";";

                $queryC .= insertQ("m_address", array(
                    "npwp_id"    => "currval('customer_npwp_id_seq'::regclass)",
                    "active"     => 'true',
                    "street"     => checksinglequote($_POST["street"] ?? ''),
                    "block"      => checksinglequote($_POST["block"] ?? ''),
                    "street_no"  => checksinglequote($_POST["street_no"] ?? ''),
                    "rt"         => checksinglequote($_POST["rt"] ?? ''),
                    "rw"         => checksinglequote($_POST["rw"] ?? ''),
                    "village_id" => (empty($_POST["village"]) || $_POST["village"] == 0) ? null : intval($_POST["village"]),
                    "country_id" => 1
                )) . ";";
            }
        }

        // Limit Kredit
        $limitCurrency = intval($_POST["limit_currency"] ?? 0);
        $limitAmount   = floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["limit_amount"] ?? 0));
        $creditLimitId = intval($_POST["customer_credit_limit_id"] ?? 0);

        if ($limitCurrency > 0 && $limitAmount > 0) {
            if ($creditLimitId > 0) {
                $queryC .= updateQ("customer_credit_limit", array(
                    "limit_amount" => $limitAmount,
                    "currency_id"  => $limitCurrency
                ), "id = " . $creditLimitId) . ";";
            } else {
                $queryC .= insertQ("customer_credit_limit", array(
                    "customer_id"  => $customerId,
                    "limit_amount" => $limitAmount,
                    "currency_id"  => $limitCurrency,
                    "from_date"    => "now()",
                    "to_date"      => null
                )) . ";";
            }
        }

        // Periode Pembayaran
        $paymentPeriod   = floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["payment_period"] ?? 0));
        $paymentPeriodId = intval($_POST["payment_period_id"] ?? 0);

        if ($paymentPeriod > 0) {
            if ($paymentPeriodId > 0) {
                $queryC .= updateQ("customer_payment_period", array("period" => $paymentPeriod), "id = " . $paymentPeriodId) . ";";
            } else {
                $queryC .= insertQ("customer_payment_period", array(
                    "customer_id" => $customerId,
                    "period"      => $paymentPeriod,
                    "from_date"   => "now()",
                    "to_date"     => null
                )) . ";";
            }
        }

        // Kontak Utama
        $contactsConfig = [
            "office_email"  => ["id" => "office_email_id", "type" => 1],
            "office_mobile" => ["id" => "office_mobile_id", "type" => 2],
            "office_phone"  => ["id" => "office_phone_id", "type" => 3],
            "office_fax"    => ["id" => "office_fax_id", "type" => 4]
        ];

        foreach ($contactsConfig as $postKey => $cfg) {
            if (!empty($_POST[$postKey])) {
                $contactNoId = intval($_POST[$cfg["id"]] ?? 0);
                if ($contactNoId > 0) {
                    $queryC .= updateQ("m_contact_no", array("contact_no" => checksinglequote($_POST[$postKey])), "id = " . $contactNoId) . ";";
                } else {
                    $queryC .= insertQ("m_contact_no", array(
                        "customer_id"  => $customerId,
                        "contact_type" => $cfg["type"],
                        "contact_no"   => checksinglequote($_POST[$postKey])
                    )) . ";";
                }
            }
        }

        // Save Kontak Person Tambahan
        if (is_array($dataContacts)) {
            foreach ($dataContacts as $contact) {
                $contId = intval($contact->cont_id ?? 0);
                if ($contId > 0) {
                    $queryC .= updateQ("m_contact_person", array(
                        "name"     => checksinglequote($contact->name ?? ''),
                        "position" => checksinglequote($contact->position ?? ''),
                        "remark"   => checksinglequote($contact->remark ?? '')
                    ), "id = " . $contId) . ";";

                    if (!empty($contact->address)) {
                        $contAddrId = intval($contact->cont_address_id ?? 0);
                        if ($contAddrId > 0) {
                            $queryC .= updateQ("m_address", array("address" => checksinglequote($contact->address)), "id = " . $contAddrId) . ";";
                        } else {
                            $queryC .= insertQ("m_address", array(
                                "contact_person_id" => $contId,
                                "active"            => 'true',
                                "address"           => checksinglequote($contact->address)
                            )) . ";";
                        }
                    }

                    $subContacts = [
                        "email"  => ["id" => "cont_email_id", "type" => 1],
                        "mobile" => ["id" => "cont_mobile_id", "type" => 2],
                        "phone"  => ["id" => "cont_phone_id", "type" => 3]
                    ];

                    foreach ($subContacts as $sKey => $sCfg) {
                        if (!empty($contact->$sKey)) {
                            $subId = intval($contact->{$sCfg["id"]} ?? 0);
                            if ($subId > 0) {
                                $queryC .= updateQ("m_contact_no", array("contact_no" => checksinglequote($contact->$sKey)), "id = " . $subId) . ";";
                            } else {
                                $queryC .= insertQ("m_contact_no", array(
                                    "contact_person_id" => $contId,
                                    "contact_type"      => $sCfg["type"],
                                    "contact_no"        => checksinglequote($contact->$sKey)
                                )) . ";";
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    $queryC .= insertQ("m_contact_person", array(
                        "customer_id" => $customerId,
                        "name"        => checksinglequote($contact->name ?? ''),
                        "position"    => checksinglequote($contact->position ?? ''),
                        "remark"      => checksinglequote($contact->remark ?? '')
                    )) . ";";

                    if (!empty($contact->address)) {
                        $queryC .= insertQ("m_address", array(
                            "contact_person_id" => "currval('m_contact_person_id_seq'::regclass)",
                            "active"            => 'true',
                            "address"           => checksinglequote($contact->address)
                        )) . ";";
                    }

                    if (!empty($contact->email)) {
                        $queryC .= insertQ("m_contact_no", array(
                            "contact_type"      => 1,
                            "contact_person_id" => "currval('m_contact_person_id_seq'::regclass)",
                            "contact_no"        => checksinglequote($contact->email)
                        )) . ";";
                    }
                    if (!empty($contact->mobile)) {
                        $queryC .= insertQ("m_contact_no", array(
                            "contact_type"      => 2,
                            "contact_person_id" => "currval('m_contact_person_id_seq'::regclass)",
                            "contact_no"        => checksinglequote($contact->mobile)
                        )) . ";";
                    }
                    if (!empty($contact->phone)) {
                        $queryC .= insertQ("m_contact_no", array(
                            "contact_type"      => 3,
                            "contact_person_id" => "currval('m_contact_person_id_seq'::regclass)",
                            "contact_no"        => checksinglequote($contact->phone)
                        )) . ";";
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        $db->cmdQ($queryC);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 7. DELETE (Soft Delete Customer)
    // ==========================================
    case ('delete'):
        $deleteId = intval($_POST["id"] ?? 0);

        if ($deleteId > 0) {
            $table  = "m_customer";
            $data   = array("active" => 'false');
            $where  = "id = " . $deleteId;

            $queryC = updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";
            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
            echo $db->getResult();
        } else {
            echo json_encode([
                "success" => "false",
                "status"  => "Error",
                "message" => "ID Customer tidak valid untuk proses penghapusan."
            ]);
        }
    break;

    default:
        echo json_encode([
            "success" => "false",
            "status"  => "Error",
            "message" => "Task atau perintah tidak dikenali oleh sistem."
        ]);
    break;
}
?>

## File: ./bin.locationCRD.php

<?php
/*
  Document      : Bin Location (Lokasi Rak) - Backend Controller
  Description   : Master data lokasi rak per gudang & filtering dynamic bin per warehouse
  Depends on    : bin_location_schema.sql, crudClass.php
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. READ (Grid Utama Filter / Search)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read':
        $query       = $_POST['query'] ?? null;
        $warehouseId = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        $srch        = searchData($query, ['bl.code', 'bl.name', 'wh.name']);
        
        $where = "1=1" . ($srch['searchSQL'] ?? '');
        if ($warehouseId > 0) {
            $where .= " AND bl.warehouse_id = " . $warehouseId;
        }
        $limit = $srch['limit'] ?? 250;

        $sql = "
        SELECT bl.id, bl.warehouse_id, wh.name AS warehouse_name,
               loc.name AS location_name, 
               -- Atau wh.location AS location_name (sesuai struktur DB)
               bl.code, bl.name, bl.zone, bl.rack, bl.level, bl.active
        FROM bin_locations bl
        LEFT JOIN warehouses wh ON wh.id = bl.warehouse_id
        LEFT JOIN m_location loc ON loc.id = wh.location_id -- Atyesikan relasi tabel location jika ada
        WHERE {$where}
        ORDER BY wh.name ASC, bl.code ASC
        LIMIT " . intval($limit);
            
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        foreach ($data as &$row) {
            $row['active'] = ($row['active'] === true || $row['active'] === 't' || $row['active'] == 1 || $row['active'] === 'true');
        }

        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. READ BIN DETAIL (Detail Stok Barang Realtime di Bin)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_bin_detail':
        $binId = intval($_POST['bin_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($binId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Bin ID tidak valid.', 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT 
                sm.bin_location_id,
                i.init as item_code,
                i.name as item_name,
                COALESCE(g.name, '-') AS grade_name,
                SUM(CASE WHEN sm.movement_type = 'IN' THEN sm.qty ELSE -sm.qty END) AS qty
            FROM stock_movements sm
            JOIN items i ON i.id = sm.item_id
            LEFT JOIN item_grade g ON g.id = sm.grade_id
            WHERE sm.bin_location_id::int = :bin_id::int
            GROUP BY sm.bin_location_id, i.init, i.name, g.name
            HAVING SUM(CASE WHEN sm.movement_type = 'IN' THEN sm.qty ELSE -sm.qty END) > 0
            ORDER BY i.init ASC
        ";

        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['bin_id' => $binId]);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. READ COMBO: Gudang
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_warehouse':
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM warehouses WHERE active = true ORDER BY name ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. READ COMBO: Bin Location Cascading Filter per Gudang
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_bin_by_warehouse':
        $warehouseId = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? $_GET['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($warehouseId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $stmt = $db->prepare("
            SELECT id, code, name, zone, rack, level
            FROM bin_locations
            WHERE warehouse_id = :warehouse_id AND active = true
            ORDER BY code ASC
        ");
        $stmt->execute(['warehouse_id' => $warehouseId]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. CREATE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'create':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        SaveBinLocation($db, null, $_POST);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. UPDATE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'update':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 3) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }
        SaveBinLocation($db, $id, $_POST);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. DELETE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'delete':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stock_movements WHERE bin_location_id = :id");
        $stmtChk->execute(['id' => $id]);
        if (intval($stmtChk->fetchColumn()) > 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Bin Location ini sudah dipakai di transaksi stok. Silakan nonaktifkan saja.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->query("DELETE FROM bin_locations WHERE id = " . intval($id));
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Bin Location berhasil dihapus.']);
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
}

function SaveBinLocation($db, $id, $post) {
    $warehouseId = intval($post['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
    $code        = trim($post['code'] ?? '');
    $name        = trim($post['name'] ?? '');
    $zone        = trim($post['zone'] ?? '');
    $rack        = trim($post['rack'] ?? '');
    $level       = trim($post['level'] ?? '');
    $active      = isset($post['active']) && ($post['active'] === '1' || $post['active'] === 'true' || $post['active'] === true);

    if ($warehouseId <= 0) {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gudang wajib dipilih.']);
        return;
    }
    if ($code === '') {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Kode Bin Location wajib diisi.']);
        return;
    }

    $sqlDup = "SELECT id FROM bin_locations WHERE warehouse_id = :warehouse_id AND code = :code";
    $paramsDup = ['warehouse_id' => $warehouseId, 'code' => $code];
    if ($id) {
        $sqlDup .= " AND id != :id";
        $paramsDup['id'] = $id;
    }
    $stmtDup = $db->prepare($sqlDup);
    $stmtDup->execute($paramsDup);
    if ($stmtDup->fetch()) {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => "Kode '{$code}' sudah ada di gudang ini."]);
        return;
    }

    $data = [
        'warehouse_id' => $warehouseId,
        'code'         => $code,
        'name'         => $name !== '' ? $name : null,
        'zone'         => $zone !== '' ? $zone : null,
        'rack'         => $rack !== '' ? $rack : null,
        'level'        => $level !== '' ? $level : null,
        'active'       => $active ? 'true' : 'false'
    ];

    if ($id === null) {
        DbOrFail($db, $db->insert('bin_locations', $data), 'insert bin_locations');
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Bin Location '{$code}' berhasil ditambahkan."]);
    } else {
        DbOrFail($db, $db->update('bin_locations', $data, "id = {$id}"), 'update bin_locations');
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Bin Location '{$code}' berhasil diperbarui."]);
    }
}
exit;

## File: ./purchase.planningCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/**
 * purchase.planningCRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Laporan Purchase Planning: untuk tiap proyek, bandingkan Budget RAB
 * (budget_items, category 'Material') vs yang SUDAH di-PO-kan
 * (po_items.budget_item_id -> budget_items.id, tidak termasuk PO 'Dibatalkan'),
 * supaya kelihatan sisa material yang masih perlu dibeli.
 *
 * PERBAIKAN dari versi sebelumnya:
 * - [FIX FATAL] Query memakai rab_materials & m_materials yang SUDAH TIDAK ADA
 *   (dihapus saat konsolidasi ke materials/budget_items) ->
 *   'relation "rab_materials" does not exist'. Ditulis ulang memakai
 *   budget_items + materials. Jembatan lama (m_materials.material_code ->
 *   materials -> budget_items) tidak diperlukan lagi.
 * - Kontrak JSON dipertahankan PERSIS (rab_material_id, budget_qty, unit_price,
 *   qty_ordered, qty_remaining, remaining_value, dst) -- purchase.planning.js
 *   tidak perlu diubah. rab_material_id kini = budget_items.id, sehingga
 *   budget_item_id yang dikirim ke halaman PO langsung valid.
 *
 * TIDAK menulis apa pun ke PO -- murni laporan. Tombol "Buat PO dari
 * Terpilih" di frontend hanya membawa data terpilih ke halaman PO lewat
 * sessionStorage; PO-nya sendiri baru dibuat di sana.
 *
 * File related : - purchase.planning.php
 *                - purchase.planning.js
 * ============================================================
 */

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

session_start();
header('Content-Type: application/json');

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';

function PgOrFail($result, $context = '') {
    if ($result === false) {
        throw new Exception(($context ? "[{$context}] " : '') . pg_last_error());
    }
    return $result;
}

// Qty yang sudah di-PO-kan per baris RAB (PO Dibatalkan tidak dihitung).
// Dipakai di dua task supaya hitungannya selalu konsisten.
const PLAN_PO_SUM = "
    SELECT poi.budget_item_id, SUM(poi.qty) AS qty_ordered
      FROM po_items poi
      JOIN purchase_orders po ON po.id = poi.po_id
     WHERE po.status <> 'Dibatalkan' AND poi.budget_item_id IS NOT NULL
     GROUP BY poi.budget_item_id
";

try {
    switch ($task) {

        // ==========================================================
        // LIST PROJECTS -- Grid Utama (Proyek + Ringkasan Sisa Beli)
        // ==========================================================
        case 'list_projects':
            $searchQuery = trim($_REQUEST['query'] ?? '');
            $searchWhere = '';
            $searchParams = [];
            if ($searchQuery !== '') {
                $searchWhere = " WHERE p.name ILIKE $1";
                $searchParams[] = '%' . $searchQuery . '%';
            }

            $sql = "SELECT p.id, p.name,
                           COALESCE(agg.total_rab_count, 0) AS total_rab_count,
                           COALESCE(agg.material_count, 0) AS material_count,
                           COALESCE(agg.total_remaining_value, 0) AS total_remaining_value
                    FROM public.projects p
                    LEFT JOIN (
                        SELECT bi.project_id,
                               COUNT(*) AS total_rab_count,
                               COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE (bi.qty_plan - COALESCE(po_sum.qty_ordered, 0)) > 0.0001) AS material_count,
                               SUM(GREATEST(bi.qty_plan - COALESCE(po_sum.qty_ordered, 0), 0) * COALESCE(bi.unit_price_plan, 0)) AS total_remaining_value
                          FROM public.budget_items bi
                          JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = bi.material_id
                          LEFT JOIN (" . PLAN_PO_SUM . ") po_sum ON po_sum.budget_item_id = bi.id
                         WHERE bi.category = 'Material' AND COALESCE(bi.qty_plan, 0) > 0
                         GROUP BY bi.project_id
                    ) agg ON agg.project_id = p.id
                    {$searchWhere}
                    ORDER BY p.created_at DESC, p.id DESC";
            $result = PgOrFail(pg_query_params($sql, $searchParams), 'list_projects');
            $rows = pg_fetch_all($result) ?: [];
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $rows, 'totalCount' => count($rows)]);
            break;

        // ==========================================================
        // LOAD PLANNING -- Detail Sisa Beli per Material utk 1 Proyek
        // ==========================================================
        case 'load_planning':
            $projectId = (int)($_REQUEST['project_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($projectId <= 0) throw new Exception('Proyek belum dipilih');

            $sql = "SELECT bi.id AS rab_material_id, bi.material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name,
                           COALESCE(NULLIF(bi.unit, ''), m.unit, '') AS unit,
                           bi.qty_plan AS budget_qty,
                           COALESCE(bi.unit_price_plan, 0) AS unit_price,
                           COALESCE(po_sum.qty_ordered, 0) AS qty_ordered,
                           GREATEST(bi.qty_plan - COALESCE(po_sum.qty_ordered, 0), 0) AS qty_remaining,
                           (GREATEST(bi.qty_plan - COALESCE(po_sum.qty_ordered, 0), 0) * COALESCE(bi.unit_price_plan, 0)) AS remaining_value
                      FROM public.budget_items bi
                      JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = bi.material_id
                      LEFT JOIN (" . PLAN_PO_SUM . ") po_sum ON po_sum.budget_item_id = bi.id
                     WHERE bi.project_id = $1 AND bi.category = 'Material' AND COALESCE(bi.qty_plan, 0) > 0
                     ORDER BY m.material_name";
            $result = PgOrFail(pg_query_params($sql, [$projectId]), 'load_planning');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => pg_fetch_all($result) ?: []]);
            break;

        default:
            throw new Exception('Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task));
    }
} catch (Exception $e) {
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
}

## File: ./profitlossCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Profit & Loss (Rugi Laba) - CRUD & Report Handler
<<<<<<< Updated upstream
  Backend       : /rab-project/dev/php-crud/financial_profit_lossCRD.php
=======
  Backend       : /rab-project/live/php-crud/financial_profit_lossCRD.php
>>>>>>> Stashed changes
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task   = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read_profit_loss';
$access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
$db     = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==============================================================
    // READ: Data Periode
    case 'read_periods':
        $sql = "SELECT id, period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods ORDER BY start_date DESC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // READ: Laporan Rugi Laba Utama
    case 'read_profit_loss':
        $periodId = intval($_POST['period_id'] ?? $_REQUEST['period_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($periodId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode akuntansi wajib dipilih.']);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtP = $db->prepare("SELECT period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtP->execute(['id' => $periodId]);
        $period = $stmtP->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if (!$period) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode tidak ditemukan.']);
            exit;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type,
                   COALESCE(SUM(
                       CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'revenue' THEN l.credit - l.debit
                            WHEN c.account_type = 'expense' THEN l.debit - l.credit
                            ELSE 0 END
                   ), 0) AS amount
            FROM chart_of_accounts c
            LEFT JOIN journal_lines l ON l.account_id = c.id
            LEFT JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
                AND j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')
                AND j.journal_date BETWEEN :start_date AND :end_date
            WHERE c.is_active = true
              AND c.account_type IN ('revenue', 'expense')
            GROUP BY c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type
            ORDER BY c.account_code ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute([
            'start_date' => $period['start_date'],
            'end_date'   => $period['end_date']
        ]);
        $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $revenues = [];
        $expenses = [];
        $totalRevenue = 0;
        $totalExpense = 0;

        foreach ($rows as $r) {
            $amount = floatval($r['amount']);
            $item = [
                'id'           => $r['id'],
                'account_code' => $r['account_code'],
                'account_name' => $r['account_name'],
                'amount'       => $amount
            ];

            if ($r['account_type'] === 'revenue') {
                $revenues[] = $item;
                $totalRevenue += $amount;
            } else if ($r['account_type'] === 'expense') {
                $expenses[] = $item;
                $totalExpense += $amount;
            }
        }

        $netProfitLoss = $totalRevenue - $totalExpense;

        echo json_encode([
            'success'        => true,
            'period_name'    => $period['period_name'],
            'start_date'     => $period['start_date'],
            'end_date'       => $period['end_date'],
            'revenues'       => $revenues,
            'expenses'       => $expenses,
            'total_revenue'  => $totalRevenue,
            'total_expense'  => $totalExpense,
            'net_profit_loss'=> $netProfitLoss
        ]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // CREATE: Tambah Pemetaan Akun Baru (Pendapatan / Beban)
    case 'create':
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $code = trim($_POST['account_code'] ?? '');
        $name = trim($_POST['account_name'] ?? '');
        $type = trim($_POST['account_type'] ?? '');

        if (empty($code) || empty($name) || !in_array($type, ['revenue', 'expense'])) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Kode, Nama, dan Tipe (revenue/expense) wajib diisi.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->insert('chart_of_accounts', [
            'account_code' => $code,
            'account_name' => $name,
            'account_type' => $type,
            'is_active'    => true
        ]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Akun Rugi Laba berhasil ditambahkan.']);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // UPDATE: Update Informasi Akun Rugi Laba
    case 'update':
        if ($access < 3) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id   = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $code = trim($_POST['account_code'] ?? '');
        $name = trim($_POST['account_name'] ?? '');

        if ($id <= 0 || empty($code) || empty($name)) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID, Kode, dan Nama Akun tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->update('chart_of_accounts', [
            'account_code' => $code,
            'account_name' => $name
        ], "id = {$id}");
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Akun Rugi Laba berhasil diperbarui.']);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // DELETE: Soft Delete Akun (Nonaktifkan Akun)
    case 'delete':
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID Akun tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->update('chart_of_accounts', ['is_active' => false], "id = {$id}");
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Akun berhasil dinonaktifkan.']);
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task)]);
}

## File: ./m.materialsCRD.php
