## File: ./warehouse.issuingCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/**
 * warehouse.issuingCRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Backend Controller Warehouse Issuing (Material Out ke proyek) - warehouse.issuing.js
 * Pola sama dengan rabCRD.php: PDO (class crud), dispatch lewat $_REQUEST['task'],
 * respons JSON {success, message, data, totalCount}.
 *
 * Aturan bisnis:
 *  1. Issue hanya dari stok yang sudah di-RCV (wh_receiving_dtl, posted, belum reversed).
 *  2. Qty issue per material tidak boleh > sisa layer RCV di gudang tsb
 *     (sisa = qty RCV - retur pembelian posted - issue posted lain).
 *  3. Setiap issue wajib punya proyek tujuan (material_out_header.project_id).
 *  4. Nilai issue = biaya layer RCV yang diambil, urut FIFO (received_date, id).
 *     Jejak per layer disimpan di material_issue_allocations; total per baris di material_out_items.amount.
 *  5. Melebihi RAB / material tanpa RAB boleh, tapi wajib isi alasan overbudget.
 *  6. Issue posted tidak diedit; dibatalkan lewat reverse_issue (layer FIFO pulih otomatis).
 *
 * Butuh: sql/2026-09-30.warehouse_issuing.sql
 * ============================================================
 */

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
ob_start();

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}
header('Content-Type: application/json');

$db = new crud();

const GI_EPS = 0.00005;

class GiBusinessError extends Exception {}

function sendJsonResponse($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    $response = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    if ($success) {
        if (is_array($data) && isset($data['data'])) {
            $response['data'] = $data['data'];
            if (isset($data['totalCount'])) $response['totalCount'] = $data['totalCount'];
        } else {
            $response['data'] = $data;
        }
    }
    if (ob_get_length()) { ob_clean(); }
    echo json_encode($response, JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
    exit;
}

function gi_num(array $row, array $keys): array {
    foreach ($keys as $k) {
        if (array_key_exists($k, $row)) $row[$k] = (float)$row[$k];
    }
    return $row;
}

function gi_fmt($n): string {
    return rtrim(rtrim(number_format((float)$n, 4, '.', ''), '0'), '.');
}

/** Query dasar layer FIFO. Parameter: :wh (warehouse_id). Pemanggil menambah filter item/ORDER/FOR UPDATE. */
function gi_layer_sql(): string {
    return "
        SELECT d.id AS layer_id, d.item_id, d.grade_id, d.lot_no,
               d.unit_cost AS unit_cost,
               h.ref_no AS rcv_ref, h.received_date, h.id AS hdr_id,
               (d.qty_received - COALESCE(ret.q, 0) - COALESCE(al.q, 0)) AS remaining
          FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
          JOIN wh_receiving_hdr h ON h.id = d.wh_receiving_hdr_id
          LEFT JOIN (
                SELECT rd.wh_receiving_dtl_id AS id, SUM(rd.qty_returned) AS q
                  FROM purchase_return_dtl rd
                  JOIN purchase_return_hdr rh ON rh.id = rd.return_hdr_id
                 WHERE rh.status = 'posted'
                 GROUP BY rd.wh_receiving_dtl_id
          ) ret ON ret.id = d.id
          LEFT JOIN (
                SELECT a.layer_id AS id, SUM(a.qty) AS q
                  FROM material_issue_allocations a
                  JOIN material_out_items  i  ON i.id  = a.out_item_id
                  JOIN material_out_header oh ON oh.id = i.material_out_id
                 WHERE a.layer_source = 'WHRCV' AND oh.status = 'posted' AND oh.reversed_at IS NULL
                 GROUP BY a.layer_id
          ) al ON al.id = d.id
         WHERE h.warehouse_id = :wh AND h.status = 'posted' AND h.reversed_at IS NULL";
}

/** Layer FIFO (yang masih ada sisa) satu material di satu gudang, tertua dulu. */
function gi_layers($db, int $wh, int $item, bool $lock = false): array {
    $sql = gi_layer_sql() . " AND d.item_id = :item
            ORDER BY h.received_date ASC, h.id ASC, d.id ASC" . ($lock ? " FOR UPDATE OF d" : "");
    $st = $db->prepare($sql);
    $st->execute([':wh' => $wh, ':item' => $item]);
    $out = [];
    foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $l) {
        $l['remaining'] = (float)$l['remaining'];
        $l['unit_cost'] = (float)$l['unit_cost'];
        if ($l['remaining'] > GI_EPS) $out[] = $l;
    }
    return $out;
}

/** Alokasi FIFO. Return ['allocs','amount','available']. */
function gi_allocate(array $layers, float $qty): array {
    $available = 0.0;
    foreach ($layers as $l) $available += $l['remaining'];

    $need = $qty; $allocs = []; $amount = 0.0;
    foreach ($layers as $l) {
        if ($need <= GI_EPS) break;
        $take = min($l['remaining'], $need);
        $amt  = round($take * $l['unit_cost'], 2);
        $allocs[] = [
            'layer_id' => (int)$l['layer_id'], 'grade_id' => $l['grade_id'], 'lot_no' => $l['lot_no'],
            'rcv_ref' => $l['rcv_ref'], 'received_date' => $l['received_date'],
            'qty' => round($take, 4), 'unit_cost' => $l['unit_cost'], 'amount' => $amt,
        ];
        $amount += $amt;
        $need   -= $take;
    }
    return ['allocs' => $allocs, 'amount' => round($amount, 2), 'available' => round($available, 4)];
}

/** RAB proyek untuk satu material + pemakaian posted. */
function gi_budget($db, int $project, int $mat): array {
    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(qty_plan),0) AS plan, COALESCE(MAX(tolerance_pct),0) AS tol, COUNT(*) AS n
                          FROM budget_items WHERE project_id = :p AND material_id = :m");
    $st->execute([':p' => $project, ':m' => $mat]);
    $b = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(i.qty_out),0)
                          FROM material_out_items i
                          JOIN material_out_header h ON h.id = i.material_out_id
                         WHERE h.project_id = :p AND i.material_id = :m AND h.status = 'posted' AND h.reversed_at IS NULL");
    $st->execute([':p' => $project, ':m' => $mat]);
    $used = (float)$st->fetchColumn();

    $plan = (float)$b['plan'];
    return ['has_rab' => (int)$b['n'] > 0, 'limit' => $plan * (1 + (float)$b['tol'] / 100), 'used' => $used];
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- auth
if (empty($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.');
}
$userId = (int)$_SESSION['user_id'];

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';

try {
    switch ($task) {

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Master untuk combo header
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_project_options':
            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, name FROM public.projects ORDER BY name ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows);
            break;

        case 'list_warehouses':
            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, COALESCE(code,'') AS code, name, project_id
                                  FROM public.warehouses WHERE active IS TRUE ORDER BY name ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) { $r['id'] = (int)$r['id']; $r['project_id'] = $r['project_id'] !== null ? (int)$r['project_id'] : null; }
            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Stok tersedia (sisa RCV) per gudang + info RAB proyek
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'stock_available':
            $wh  = (int)($_REQUEST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
            $prj = (int)($_REQUEST['project_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($wh <= 0) sendJsonResponse(true, []);

            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT x.item_id AS material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name, COALESCE(m.unit,'') AS unit,
                       SUM(x.remaining) AS available
                  FROM (" . gi_layer_sql() . ") x
                  JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = x.item_id
                 GROUP BY x.item_id, m.material_code, m.material_name, m.unit
                HAVING SUM(x.remaining) > 0.00005
                 ORDER BY m.material_name ASC");
            $st->execute([':wh' => $wh]);
            $stock = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            $budget = []; $used = [];
            if ($prj > 0) {
                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT material_id, SUM(qty_plan) AS plan, MAX(tolerance_pct) AS tol
                                      FROM budget_items WHERE project_id = :p AND material_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY material_id");
                $st->execute([':p' => $prj]);
                foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $b) {
                    $budget[$b['material_id']] = ['plan' => (float)$b['plan'], 'tol' => (float)$b['tol']];
                }
                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT i.material_id, SUM(i.qty_out) AS q
                                      FROM material_out_items i JOIN material_out_header h ON h.id = i.material_out_id
                                     WHERE h.project_id = :p AND h.status = 'posted' AND h.reversed_at IS NULL
                                     GROUP BY i.material_id");
                $st->execute([':p' => $prj]);
                foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $u) $used[$u['material_id']] = (float)$u['q'];
            }

            $data = [];
            foreach ($stock as $s) {
                $mid = $s['material_id'];
                $b   = $budget[$mid] ?? null;
                $data[] = [
                    'material_id'   => (int)$mid,
                    'material_code' => $s['material_code'],
                    'material_name' => $s['material_name'],
                    'unit'          => $s['unit'],
                    'available'     => (float)$s['available'],
                    'has_rab'       => $b !== null,
                    'rab_limit'     => $b ? $b['plan'] * (1 + $b['tol'] / 100) : 0,
                    'rab_used'      => $used[$mid] ?? 0,
                ];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, $data);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Preview FIFO (tanpa menyimpan)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'preview_fifo':
            $wh  = (int)($_REQUEST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
            $mat = (int)($_REQUEST['material_id'] ?? 0);
            $qty = (float)($_REQUEST['qty'] ?? 0);
            if ($wh <= 0 || $mat <= 0 || $qty <= 0) {
                sendJsonResponse(true, ['ok' => false, 'available' => 0, 'amount' => 0, 'avg_cost' => 0, 'allocations' => []]);
            }
            $r = gi_allocate(gi_layers($db, $wh, $mat), $qty);
            $allocQty = array_sum(array_column($r['allocs'], 'qty'));
            sendJsonResponse(true, [
                'ok'          => $qty <= $r['available'] + GI_EPS,
                'available'   => $r['available'],
                'amount'      => $r['amount'],
                'avg_cost'    => $allocQty > 0 ? round($r['amount'] / $allocQty, 2) : 0,
                'allocations' => $r['allocs'],
            ]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Daftar issue (grid utama)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_issues':
            $start = (int)($_REQUEST['start'] ?? 0);
            $limit = (int)($_REQUEST['limit'] ?? 250);
            $query = trim($_REQUEST['query'] ?? '');

            $where = ''; $params = [];
            if ($query !== '') {
                $where = "WHERE (h.ref_no ILIKE :query OR p.name ILIKE :query OR w.name ILIKE :query OR h.received_by_mandor ILIKE :query)";
                $params[':query'] = '%' . $query . '%';
            }

            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*)
                                  FROM material_out_header h
                                  JOIN public.projects p   ON p.id = h.project_id
                                  JOIN public.warehouses w ON w.id = h.warehouse_id {$where}");
            $st->execute($params);
            $totalCount = (int)$st->fetchColumn();

            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT h.id, h.ref_no, h.issue_date, h.project_id, p.name AS project_name,
                       h.warehouse_id, w.name AS warehouse_name, h.received_by_mandor, h.status, h.remark,
                       COUNT(i.id) AS item_count,
                       COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN i.amount > 0 THEN i.amount ELSE i.qty_out * i.unit_cost END), 0) AS amount
                  FROM material_out_header h
                  JOIN public.projects p   ON p.id = h.project_id
                  JOIN public.warehouses w ON w.id = h.warehouse_id
                  LEFT JOIN material_out_items i ON i.material_out_id = h.id
                  {$where}
                 GROUP BY h.id, p.name, w.name
                 ORDER BY h.issue_date DESC, h.id DESC
                 LIMIT :limit OFFSET :start");
            foreach ($params as $k => $v) $st->bindValue($k, $v);
            $st->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $st->bindValue(':start', $start, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $st->execute();
            $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
                $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
                $r['project_id'] = (int)$r['project_id'];
                $r['warehouse_id'] = (int)$r['warehouse_id'];
                $r['item_count'] = (int)$r['item_count'];
                $r['amount'] = (float)$r['amount'];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, ['data' => $rows, 'totalCount' => $totalCount]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Detail issue + jejak FIFO
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'load_issue':
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT h.id, h.ref_no, h.issue_date, h.status, h.remark, h.received_by_mandor,
                       p.name AS project_name, w.name AS warehouse_name
                  FROM material_out_header h
                  JOIN public.projects p   ON p.id = h.project_id
                  JOIN public.warehouses w ON w.id = h.warehouse_id
                 WHERE h.id = :id");
            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
            $hdr = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$hdr) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Dokumen issue tidak ditemukan.');

            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT i.id, i.material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name, COALESCE(m.unit,'') AS unit,
                       i.qty_out, i.unit_cost, i.amount, i.lot_no, i.is_overbudget, i.overbudget_reason
                  FROM material_out_items i JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = i.material_id
                 WHERE i.material_out_id = :id ORDER BY i.id");
            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
            $items = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            $st = $db->prepare("
                SELECT a.out_item_id, a.layer_id, a.qty, a.unit_cost, a.amount, h.ref_no AS rcv_ref, h.received_date
                  FROM material_issue_allocations a
                  LEFT JOIN wh_receiving_dtl d ON d.id = a.layer_id
                  LEFT JOIN wh_receiving_hdr h ON h.id = d.wh_receiving_hdr_id
                 WHERE a.out_item_id IN (SELECT id FROM material_out_items WHERE material_out_id = :id)
                 ORDER BY a.id");
            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
            $byItem = [];
            foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $a) {
                $byItem[$a['out_item_id']][] = gi_num($a, ['qty', 'unit_cost', 'amount']);
            }
            foreach ($items as &$it) {
                $it = gi_num($it, ['qty_out', 'unit_cost', 'amount']);
                $it['is_overbudget'] = ($it['is_overbudget'] === true || $it['is_overbudget'] === 't' || $it['is_overbudget'] === '1');
                $it['allocations'] = $byItem[$it['id']] ?? [];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, ['header' => $hdr, 'items' => $items]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * POST ISSUE
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'save_issue':
            $prj   = (int)($_POST['project_id'] ?? 0);
            $wh    = (int)($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
            $date  = (string)($_POST['issue_date'] ?? '');
            $items = json_decode($_POST['items'] ?? '[]', true);

            try {
                $d = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $date);
                if (!$d || $d->format('Y-m-d') !== $date) throw new GiBusinessError('Tanggal issue tidak valid.');
                if ($prj <= 0) throw new GiBusinessError('Proyek tujuan wajib dipilih.');
                if ($wh <= 0)  throw new GiBusinessError('Gudang wajib dipilih.');
                if (!is_array($items) || !count($items)) throw new GiBusinessError('Minimal satu material harus di-issue.');

                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.projects WHERE id = :id");
                $st->execute([':id' => $prj]);
                if (!$st->fetchColumn()) throw new GiBusinessError('Proyek tidak ditemukan.');

                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.warehouses WHERE id = :id AND active IS TRUE");
                $st->execute([':id' => $wh]);
                if (!$st->fetchColumn()) throw new GiBusinessError('Gudang tidak ditemukan / tidak aktif.');

                if (!function_exists('CreateRefNo')) throw new Exception('CreateRefNo() tidak tersedia.');
                $ref = CreateRefNo('material_out_header', 'ref_no', 'GI');

                $db->beginTransaction();
                try {
                    $st = $db->prepare("
                        INSERT INTO material_out_header
                            (ref_no, project_id, warehouse_id, issue_date, issued_by, received_by_mandor, status, remark)
                        VALUES (:ref, :prj, :wh, CAST(:dt AS date), :uid, :mandor, 'posted', :remark)
                        RETURNING id");
                    $st->execute([
                        ':ref' => $ref, ':prj' => $prj, ':wh' => $wh, ':dt' => $date, ':uid' => $userId,
                        ':mandor' => trim((string)($_POST['received_by_mandor'] ?? '')) ?: null,
                        ':remark' => trim((string)($_POST['remark'] ?? '')) ?: null,
                    ]);
                    $hid = (int)$st->fetchColumn();

                    $stMat  = $db->prepare("SELECT material_name, COALESCE(unit,'') AS unit FROM public.materials WHERE id = :id");
                    $stItem = $db->prepare("
                        INSERT INTO material_out_items
                            (material_out_id, material_id, qty_out, unit_cost, amount, lot_no, is_overbudget, overbudget_reason, notes)
                        VALUES (:hid, :mat, :qty, :cost, :amt, :lot, :over, :reason, :notes)
                        RETURNING id");
                    $stAlloc = $db->prepare("
                        INSERT INTO material_issue_allocations (out_item_id, layer_source, layer_id, qty, unit_cost, amount)
                        VALUES (:iid, 'WHRCV', :layer, :qty, :cost, :amt)");
                    $stMove = $db->prepare("
                        INSERT INTO stock_movements
                            (movement_date, item_id, grade_id, warehouse_id, movement_type, qty, unit_cost,
                             reference_type, reference_id, reference_no, remark, created_by)
                        VALUES (CAST(:dt AS date), :item, :grade, :wh, 'OUT', :qty, :cost,
                                'MATERIAL_OUT', :hid, :ref, :remark, :uid)");

                    $grand = 0.0;
                    foreach ($items as $n => $it) {
                        $mat = (int)($it['material_id'] ?? 0);
                        $qty = round((float)($it['qty'] ?? 0), 4);
                        $no  = 'Baris ' . ($n + 1);
                        if ($mat <= 0 || $qty <= 0) throw new GiBusinessError("{$no}: material dan qty (> 0) wajib diisi.");

                        $stMat->execute([':id' => $mat]);
                        $mrow = $stMat->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                        if (!$mrow) throw new GiBusinessError("{$no}: material tidak ditemukan.");
                        $label = $mrow['material_name'];

                        // Qty issue <= sisa RCV (layer dikunci; baris sebelumnya di dokumen ini sudah tercatat)
                        $alloc = gi_allocate(gi_layers($db, $wh, $mat, true), $qty);
                        if ($qty > $alloc['available'] + GI_EPS) {
                            throw new GiBusinessError(
                                "{$label}: qty issue " . gi_fmt($qty) . " {$mrow['unit']} melebihi stok hasil RCV di gudang ini (tersedia " .
                                gi_fmt($alloc['available']) . ")."
                            );
                        }

                        // Overbudget terhadap RAB proyek
                        $bud    = gi_budget($db, $prj, $mat);
                        $over   = !$bud['has_rab'] || ($bud['used'] + $qty > $bud['limit'] + GI_EPS);
                        $reason = trim((string)($it['overbudget_reason'] ?? ''));
                        if ($over && $reason === '') {
                            throw new GiBusinessError($label . ': ' . ($bud['has_rab']
                                ? 'melebihi RAB proyek, alasan overbudget wajib diisi.'
                                : 'material tidak ada di RAB proyek, alasan overbudget wajib diisi.'));
                        }

                        $lots = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_column($alloc['allocs'], 'lot_no'))));
                        $stItem->execute([
                            ':hid' => $hid, ':mat' => $mat, ':qty' => $qty,
                            ':cost' => round($alloc['amount'] / $qty, 2), ':amt' => $alloc['amount'],
                            ':lot' => $lots ? substr(implode(',', $lots), 0, 50) : null,
                            ':over' => $over ? 't' : 'f',
                            ':reason' => $over ? substr($reason, 0, 100) : null,
                            ':notes' => trim((string)($it['notes'] ?? '')) ?: null,
                        ]);
                        $iid = (int)$stItem->fetchColumn();

                        foreach ($alloc['allocs'] as $a) {
                            $stAlloc->execute([':iid' => $iid, ':layer' => $a['layer_id'], ':qty' => $a['qty'],
                                               ':cost' => $a['unit_cost'], ':amt' => $a['amount']]);
                            $stMove->execute([
                                ':dt' => $date, ':item' => $mat, ':grade' => $a['grade_id'] ?: null, ':wh' => $wh,
                                ':qty' => $a['qty'], ':cost' => $a['unit_cost'], ':hid' => $hid, ':ref' => $ref,
                                ':remark' => 'Issue ke proyek #' . $prj . ' (FIFO dari ' . $a['rcv_ref'] . ')',
                                ':uid' => $userId,
                            ]);
                        }
                        $grand += $alloc['amount'];
                    }

                    $db->commit();
                    sendJsonResponse(true, ['id' => $hid, 'ref_no' => $ref, 'amount' => round($grand, 2)], "Issue {$ref} berhasil diposting.");
                } catch (Throwable $e) {
                    if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                    throw $e;
                }
            } catch (GiBusinessError $e) {
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            }
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * REVERSE ISSUE
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'reverse_issue':
            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $db->beginTransaction();
            try {
                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT id, ref_no, status FROM material_out_header WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
                $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
                $h = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                if (!$h) throw new GiBusinessError('Dokumen issue tidak ditemukan.');
                if ($h['status'] !== 'posted') throw new GiBusinessError('Dokumen sudah dibatalkan sebelumnya.');

                $st = $db->prepare("UPDATE material_out_header SET status = 'reversed', reversed_at = NOW(), reversed_by = :uid WHERE id = :id");
                $st->execute([':uid' => $userId, ':id' => $id]);

                // Stok kembali: ledger IN pembalik. Layer FIFO pulih otomatis (issue reversed tidak dihitung lagi).
                $st = $db->prepare("
                    INSERT INTO stock_movements
                        (movement_date, item_id, grade_id, warehouse_id, movement_type, qty, unit_cost,
                         reference_type, reference_id, reference_no, remark, created_by)
                    SELECT CURRENT_DATE, item_id, grade_id, warehouse_id, 'IN', qty, unit_cost,
                           'MATERIAL_OUT_REV', reference_id, reference_no, :remark, :uid
                      FROM stock_movements
                     WHERE reference_type = 'MATERIAL_OUT' AND reference_id = :id AND movement_type = 'OUT'");
                $st->execute([':remark' => 'Reverse issue ' . $h['ref_no'], ':uid' => $userId, ':id' => $id]);

                $db->commit();
                sendJsonResponse(true, [], 'Issue ' . $h['ref_no'] . ' dibatalkan, stok dikembalikan.');
            } catch (GiBusinessError $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                throw $e;
            }
            break;

        default:
            sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Aksi/Task tidak dikenal.');
            break;
    }
} catch (PDOException $e) {
    error_log('[warehouse.issuingCRD] ' . $e->getMessage());
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Database Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    error_log('[warehouse.issuingCRD] ' . $e->getMessage());
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'System Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
}

## File: ./generalledgerCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : General Ledger - Report Handler
  Called from   : js/m.generalledger.js
  Note          : - Laporan read-only, TIDAK ada create/update/delete.
                  - Filter Akun & Periode OPSIONAL:
                      account_id kosong/0 -> tampilkan SEMUA akun (dikelompokkan)
                      period_id  kosong/0 -> tampilkan SEMUA histori (tanpa
                                             batas tanggal, saldo awal = 0)
                  - Hanya jurnal berstatus 'posted' ATAU 'reversed' yang
                    dihitung. Mencakup SEMUA reference_type.
                  - Saldo berjalan dihitung PER AKUN pakai window function
                    (PARTITION BY account_id), ditambah saldo awal per akun
                    (0 kalau periode tidak difilter).
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read_ledger';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_accounts':
        $sql = "SELECT id, account_code, account_name, account_type
                FROM chart_of_accounts
                WHERE is_active = true
                ORDER BY account_code ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_periods':
        $sql = "SELECT id, period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods ORDER BY start_date DESC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // Laporan utama -- filter account_id & period_id keduanya OPSIONAL
    case 'read_ledger':
        $accountId = intval($_POST['account_id'] ?? 0);
        $periodId  = intval($_POST['period_id'] ?? 0);

        $startDate = null;
        $endDate   = null;
        $periodName = 'Semua Periode';

        if ($periodId > 0) {
            $stmtP = $db->prepare("SELECT period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtP->execute(['id' => $periodId]);
            $period = $stmtP->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$period) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode tidak ditemukan.']);
                exit;
            }
            $startDate  = $period['start_date'];
            $endDate    = $period['end_date'];
            $periodName = $period['period_name'];
        }

        $accountLabel = 'Semua Akun';
        if ($accountId > 0) {
            $stmtAcc = $db->prepare("SELECT account_code, account_name FROM chart_of_accounts WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtAcc->execute(['id' => $accountId]);
            $acc = $stmtAcc->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$acc) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akun tidak ditemukan.']);
                exit;
            }
            $accountLabel = $acc['account_code'] . ' - ' . $acc['account_name'];
        }

        // Bangun kondisi WHERE secara dinamis
        $conds  = ["j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')"];
        $params = [];
        if ($accountId > 0) {
            $conds[] = "l.account_id = :acc";
            $params['acc'] = $accountId;
        }
        if ($startDate !== null) {
            $conds[] = "j.journal_date BETWEEN :start_date AND :end_date";
            $params['start_date'] = $startDate;
            $params['end_date']   = $endDate;
        }
        $where = implode(' AND ', $conds);

        // Saldo awal per akun -- hanya relevan kalau periode difilter.
        // Kalau tidak difilter, seluruh histori sudah masuk ke query utama,
        // jadi saldo awal = 0 (tidak ada "sebelum" yang perlu diakumulasi).
        $openingSubquery = "0";
        if ($startDate !== null) {
            $openingCond = "l2.account_id = l.account_id AND j2.status IN ('posted','reversed') AND j2.journal_date < :ob_start_date";
            if ($accountId > 0) {
                $openingCond .= " AND l2.account_id = :ob_acc";
            }
            $openingSubquery = "(
                SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN c2.account_type IN ('asset','expense') THEN l2.debit - l2.credit ELSE l2.credit - l2.debit END), 0)
                FROM journal_lines l2
                JOIN journal_entries j2 ON j2.id = l2.journal_id
                JOIN chart_of_accounts c2 ON c2.id = l2.account_id
                WHERE {$openingCond}
            )";
            $params['ob_start_date'] = $startDate;
            if ($accountId > 0) $params['ob_acc'] = $accountId;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT j.journal_date, j.ref_no, l.description AS line_desc, j.description AS header_desc,
                   l.debit, l.credit, l.account_id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type,
                   {$openingSubquery} AS opening_balance,
                   SUM(CASE WHEN c.account_type IN ('asset','expense') THEN l.debit - l.credit ELSE l.credit - l.debit END)
                     OVER (PARTITION BY l.account_id ORDER BY j.journal_date, j.id, l.id
                           ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS cum_in_range
            FROM journal_lines l
            JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
            JOIN chart_of_accounts c ON c.id = l.account_id
            WHERE {$where}
            ORDER BY c.account_code ASC, j.journal_date ASC, j.id ASC, l.id ASC
        ";

        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute($params);
        $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $data = [];
        foreach ($rows as $r) {
            $data[] = [
                'account_id'      => $r['account_id'],
                'account_code'    => $r['account_code'],
                'account_name'    => $r['account_name'],
                'account_label'   => $r['account_code'] . ' - ' . $r['account_name'],
                'journal_date'    => $r['journal_date'],
                'ref_no'      => $r['ref_no'],
                'description'     => $r['line_desc'] ?: $r['header_desc'],
                'debit'           => floatval($r['debit']),
                'credit'          => floatval($r['credit']),
                'balance'         => floatval($r['opening_balance']) + floatval($r['cum_in_range'])
            ];
        }

        echo json_encode([
            'success'      => true,
            'account_label'=> $accountLabel,
            'period_name'  => $periodName,
            'totalCount'   => count($data),
            'data'         => $data
        ]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task)]);
}

## File: ./m.user.systemCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
set_time_limit(0);
if (!isset($_SESSION)) {
    session_start();
}
date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Jakarta');

include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";

$searchField = $_POST["query"] ?? $_REQUEST["query"] ?? '';
$offset      = isset($_POST["start"]) ? intval($_POST["start"]) : 0;
$limit       = isset($_POST["limit"]) ? intval($_POST["limit"]) : 250;

$itemInfoParam = $_POST["iteminfo"] ?? $_REQUEST["iteminfo"] ?? '';
$dataArray     = json2Array($itemInfoParam);
$CheckItemNum  = (empty($dataArray)) ? "[0]" : "[" . $dataArray . "]";
$CheckItemChar = (empty($dataArray)) ? "['0']" : "[" . $dataArray . "]";




// Tangkap task secara fleksibel dari POST maupun REQUEST
$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==========================================
    // 1. READ MAIN GRID (Daftar Master User System)
    // ==========================================
    case ('read'):
        $sResult   = searchData($searchField, array("s.login_name", "e.employee_name"), 250);
        $limit     = $sResult["limit"];
        $searchSQL = $sResult["searchSQL"];

        $fields = "
            s.id, s.login_name, s.employee_id, s.active,
            '' as password, '' as confirm_password,
            e.employee_name, e.gender, e.from_date, 
            d.location_init||' ~ '||d.department_name as department_name, 
            d.status_name
        ";

        $table = "
            m_user_system s
            left join m_employee e on e.id = s.employee_id
            left join view_employee_depart_status d on coalesce(d.id, 0) = coalesce(e.id, 0)
        ";

        $where = "1=1 " . $searchSQL;
        $order = "e.employee_name ASC";

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
        echo $db->getResult();
        break;

    // ==========================================
    // 2. READ DETAIL GRID (Rincian Hak Akses Menu User)
    // ==========================================
    case ("read_detail"):
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? $_POST['user_id'] ?? $_REQUEST['id'] ?? $_REQUEST['user_id'] ?? 0);

        $fields = "
            u.id, u.form_id, u.access_level, u.user_id,
            fr.group_id, fr.seq, coalesce(fr.title,'') form_name, coalesce(fr.remark,'') remark,
            g.name as group_name, g.seq as group_seq, 'true' as active
        ";

        $table = "
            m_user_access_menu u
            left join m_form_name fr on fr.id = u.form_id
            left join (
                select id, name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, seq
                from (
                    (select d.id, h.name||'/'||d.name as name, d.remark, d.seq
                    from m_form_group d
                    left join (
                        select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'
                    ) h on substring(h.seq,1,2)=substring(d.seq,1,2)
                    where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
                    union
                    (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
                    from m_form_group h
                    where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
                ) data
                order by seq
            ) g on g.id = fr.group_id
        ";

        $where = "u.user_id = " . $id;
        $order = "g.seq ASC, fr.seq ASC";

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order);
        echo $db->getResult();
        break;

    // ==========================================
    // 3. READ EDIT (Load Single Record untuk Dialog Edit)
    // ==========================================
    case ("read_edit"):
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? $_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);

        $fields = "
            s.id, s.login_name, s.employee_id, s.employee_id as name, s.active,
            '' as password, '' as confirm_password,
            e.employee_name, e.gender, e.from_date, 
            d.department_name, d.status_name
        ";

        $table = "
            m_user_system s
            left join m_employee e on e.id = s.employee_id
            left join view_employee_depart_status d on coalesce(d.id, 0) = coalesce(e.id, 0)
        ";

        $where = "s.id = " . $id;

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where);
        echo $db->getResult();
        break;

    // ==========================================
    // 4. ADD FORM (Load Daftar Form Pilihan)
    // ==========================================
    case ('addform'):
        $field = "f.id form_id, g.name as group_name, f.title form_name, f.remark, 1 as access_level, 'true' as active";
        $cleanItemNum = str_replace("'", "", $CheckItemNum);

        $table = "
            m_form_name f
            left join (
                select id, name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, seq
                from (
                    (select d.id, h.name||' -- '||d.name as name, d.remark, d.seq
                    from m_form_group d
                    left join (
                        select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'
                    ) h on substring(h.seq,1,2)=substring(d.seq,1,2)
                    where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
                    union
                    (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
                    from m_form_group h
                    where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
                ) data
            ) g on g.id = f.group_id
        ";

        $where = "f.id <> all (array" . $cleanItemNum . ")";
        if (!empty($searchField) && trim($searchField) != "") {
            $where .= " " . $searchField;
        }

        $orderBy = "g.name ASC, f.seq ASC";

        $db->select($table, $field, $where, $orderBy);
        $result = $db->getResult();
        
        if (empty($result) || trim($result) == "") {
            echo json_encode(array("totalRecord" => 0, "data" => array()));
        } else {
            echo $result;
        }
        break;

    // ==========================================
    // 5. CREATE (Tambah User Baru)
    // ==========================================
    case ('create'):
    case ('add'):
        $employeeId = intval($_POST["employee_id"] ?? $_POST["name"] ?? 0);

        // Validasi Foreign Key Karyawan
        if ($employeeId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode([
                "success" => "false", 
                "status"  => "Error", 
                "message" => "Gagal: Pilih Karyawan (Name) yang valid terlebih dahulu!"
            ]);
            exit;
        }

        $queryC = '';
        $table  = "m_user_system";
        $data   = array(
            "login_name"  => checksinglequote($_POST["login_name"] ?? ''),
            "employee_id" => $employeeId,
            "password"    => EncodeData($_POST["password"] ?? ''),
            "active"      => ($_POST["u_active"] === 'true' || $_POST["active"] === 'true') ? 'true' : 'false'
        );
        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

        $table = "m_user_login_status";
        $data  = array("user_id" => "currval('m_user_system_id_seq'::regclass)");
        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

        // Insert Detail Hak Akses Menu (baris dari gridEditor saat New User)
        $formAccessPayload = $_POST['form_access'] ?? $_POST['arrForm'] ?? null;

        if (!empty($formAccessPayload)) {
            // Bersihkan quote escape dari ExtJS
            $cleanJson = stripslashes($formAccessPayload);
            $dataForm  = json_decode($cleanJson, true);

            if (is_array($dataForm) && count($dataForm) > 0) {
                foreach ($dataForm as $row) {
                    $formId    = intval($row['form_id'] ?? 0);
                    $accessLvl = intval($row['access_level'] ?? 1);

                    if ($formId > 0) {
                        $tableD = "m_user_access_menu";
                        $dataD  = array(
                            "user_id"      => "currval('m_user_system_id_seq'::regclass)",
                            "form_id"      => $formId,
                            "access_level" => $accessLvl
                        );
                        $queryC .= insertQ($tableD, $dataD) . ";";
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        $db->cmdQ($queryC);
        echo $db->getResult();
        break;

    // ==========================================
    // 6. UPDATE (Simpan Header User & Detail Hak Akses)
    // ==========================================
    case ('update'):
    case ('edit'):
        $queryC = '';
        $userId = intval($_POST['user_id'] ?? $_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $employeeId = intval($_POST["employee_id"] ?? $_POST["name"] ?? 0);

        if ($userId > 0) {
            // Proteksi Fallback: Ambil employee_id dari DB jika nilainya 0
            if ($employeeId <= 0) {
                $checkStmt = $db->prepare("SELECT employee_id FROM m_user_system WHERE id = :id");
                $checkStmt->execute(['id' => $userId]);
                $existRow = $checkStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
                if ($existRow && !empty($existRow['employee_id'])) {
                    $employeeId = intval($existRow['employee_id']);
                }
            }

            if ($employeeId <= 0) {
                echo json_encode([
                    "success" => "false", 
                    "status"  => "Error", 
                    "message" => "Gagal Simpan: ID Karyawan tidak terdeteksi (Foreign Key Violation)."
                ]);
                exit;
            }

            // A. Update Master Header User System
            $table = "m_user_system";
            $data  = array(
                "login_name"  => checksinglequote($_POST["login_name"] ?? ''),
                "employee_id" => $employeeId,
                "active"      => ($_POST["u_active"] === 'true' || $_POST["u_active"] === true || $_POST["active"] === 'true') ? 'true' : 'false'
            );
            
            if (!empty($_POST["password"])) {
                $data["password"] = EncodeData($_POST["password"]);
            }

            $where = "id = " . $userId;
            $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";

            // B. Update / Insert Detail Hak Akses Menu
            $formAccessPayload = $_POST['form_access'] ?? $_POST['arrForm'] ?? null;

            if (!empty($formAccessPayload)) {
                // Bersihkan quote escape dari ExtJS
                $cleanJson = stripslashes($formAccessPayload);
                $dataForm  = json_decode($cleanJson, true);

                if (is_array($dataForm) && count($dataForm) > 0) {
                    $keepAccessIds = array();

                    foreach ($dataForm as $row) {
                        $accessId  = intval($row['id'] ?? 0);
                        $formId    = intval($row['form_id'] ?? 0);
                        $accessLvl = intval($row['access_level'] ?? 1);

                        if ($accessId > 0) {
                            // Update Detail Hak Akses Eksisting
                            $keepAccessIds[] = $accessId;
                            $tableD = "m_user_access_menu";
                            $dataD  = array("access_level" => $accessLvl);
                            $whereD = "id = " . $accessId;
                            $queryC .= updateQ($tableD, $dataD, $whereD) . ";";
                        } else if ($formId > 0) {
                            // Insert Detail Hak Akses Baru
                            // PENTING: dieksekusi langsung (di luar batch $queryC) dengan RETURNING id,
                            // supaya id barunya diketahui dan bisa dimasukkan ke $keepAccessIds SEBELUM
                            // statement DELETE cleanup di bawah dibangun. Kalau tidak, baris baru ini
                            // akan langsung ikut terhapus oleh DELETE ... id NOT IN (...) pada batch yang sama.
                            $insStmt = $db->prepare(
                                "INSERT INTO m_user_access_menu (user_id, form_id, access_level) VALUES (:uid, :fid, :lvl) RETURNING id"
                            );
                            $insStmt->execute([
                                'uid' => $userId,
                                'fid' => $formId,
                                'lvl' => $accessLvl
                            ]);
                            $newAccessId = $insStmt->fetchColumn();
                            if ($newAccessId) {
                                $keepAccessIds[] = intval($newAccessId);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Hapus Detail Menu yang Dibuang/Dihapus pada Grid Editor
                    if (!empty($keepAccessIds)) {
                        $idsString = implode(',', $keepAccessIds);
                        $queryC .= "DELETE FROM m_user_access_menu WHERE user_id = " . $userId . " AND id NOT IN (" . $idsString . ");";
                    }
                }
            }

            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
            echo $db->getResult();
        } else {
            echo json_encode([
                "success" => "false", 
                "status"  => "Error", 
                "message" => "ID User tidak valid untuk proses update."
            ]);
        }
        break;

    // ==========================================
    // 7. DELETE (Hapus User System)
    // ==========================================
    case ('delete'):
        $deleteId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? $_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);

        if ($deleteId > 0) {
            $table  = "m_user_system";
            $data   = array("active" => 'false');
            $where  = "id = " . $deleteId;
            $queryC = updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";

            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
            echo $db->getResult();
        } else {
            echo json_encode([
                "success" => "false", 
                "status"  => "Error", 
                "message" => "ID User tidak valid untuk proses penghapusan."
            ]);
        }
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode([
            "success" => "false", 
            "status"  => "Error", 
            "message" => "Perintah task '" . htmlspecialchars($task) . "' tidak valid."
        ]);
        break;
}
?>

## File: ./settingsCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/**
 * settingsCRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Backend Pengaturan Sistem (settings.js). Pola sama dengan rabCRD.php:
 * PDO (class crud), dispatch lewat $_REQUEST['task'], respons JSON {success, message, data}.
 *
 * Data:
 *  - company_profile  (1 baris): nama, alamat, kontak, NPWP, 2 logo (dokumen & aplikasi), pelengkap dokumen.
 *  - finance_settings (1 baris): mata uang dasar (pembukuan) + mata uang laporan keuangan final,
 *                                jenis kurs default, dasar kurs laba rugi, awal tahun fiskal, desimal.
 *
 * Task      : load_settings, save_company, save_document, save_finance,
 *             upload_logo, delete_logo, logo (GET, mengalirkan gambar)
 *
 * Aturan:
 *  - Semua task butuh login; task tulis butuh hak akses form >= SET_ACCESS_WRITE.
 *  - Logo: hanya PNG / JPEG / WEBP (isi file diperiksa, bukan hanya ekstensi), maks 1 MB, maks 4000 px.
 *    SVG sengaja ditolak (bisa memuat script).
 *  - Mata uang dasar terkunci begitu sudah ada transaksi (jurnal, stok, PO, pembayaran, invoice proyek).
 *    Mata uang laporan boleh diganti kapan saja (hanya cara penyajian laporan).
 *
 * Butuh: sql/2026-10-01.settings.sql
 * ============================================================
 */

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
ob_start();

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

const SET_ACCESS_WRITE = 3;
const SET_MAX_LOGO     = 1048576;          // 1 MB
const SET_MAX_PIXEL    = 4000;
const SET_RATE_TYPES   = ['KMK', 'BI', 'MANUAL'];
const SET_RATE_BASIS   = ['TRANSACTION', 'AVERAGE', 'PERIOD_END'];
const SET_LOGO_COLS    = ['document' => 'logo_document', 'app' => 'logo_app'];     // whitelist nama kolom
const SET_LOGO_MIMES   = ['image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/webp'];

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';
if ($task !== 'logo') {
    header('Content-Type: application/json');
}

$db = new crud();

class SetBusinessError extends Exception {}

function sendJsonResponse($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    $response = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    if ($success) {
        $response['data'] = $data;
    }
    if (ob_get_length()) { ob_clean(); }
    echo json_encode($response, JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
    exit;
}

function set_bool($v): bool {
    return $v === true || $v === 't' || $v === '1' || $v === 1;
}

function set_require_write(): void {
    if (intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0) < SET_ACCESS_WRITE) {
        sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk mengubah pengaturan.');
    }
}

function set_text($key, int $max, bool $required = false, string $label = ''): ?string {
    $v = trim((string)($_POST[$key] ?? ''));
    if ($required && $v === '') throw new SetBusinessError(($label ?: $key) . ' wajib diisi.');
    if (mb_strlen($v) > $max) throw new SetBusinessError(($label ?: $key) . " maksimal {$max} karakter.");
    return $v === '' ? null : $v;
}

/** Alasan mata uang dasar tidak boleh diganti (null = masih boleh). */
function set_base_lock_reason($db): ?string {
    $checks = [
        'journal_entries'    => 'jurnal',
        'stock_movements'    => 'pergerakan stok',
        'purchase_orders'    => 'PO',
        'supplier_payments'  => 'pembayaran supplier',
        'project_invoices'   => 'invoice proyek',
    ];
    foreach ($checks as $table => $label) {
        $reg = $db->query("SELECT to_regclass('public.{$table}')")->fetchColumn();
        if (!$reg) continue;
        if ((int)$db->query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.{$table}")->fetchColumn() > 0) {
            return "Sudah ada transaksi ({$label}).";
        }
    }
    return null;
}

function set_currency_exists($db, int $id): bool {
    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.m_currency WHERE id = :id");
    $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
    return (bool)$st->fetchColumn();
}

if (empty($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    if ($task === 'logo') { if (ob_get_length()) ob_clean(); exit; }
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.');
}
$userId = (int)$_SESSION['user_id'];

try {
    switch ($task) {

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Muat semua pengaturan
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'load_settings':
            $company = $db->query("
                SELECT company_name, legal_name, address, city, province, postal_code, country,
                       phone, email, website, tax_id, doc_footer, doc_signer_name, doc_signer_title,
                       (logo_document IS NOT NULL) AS has_logo_document,
                       (logo_app IS NOT NULL)      AS has_logo_app,
                       COALESCE(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM logo_document_updated_at)::bigint, 0) AS logo_document_v,
                       COALESCE(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM logo_app_updated_at)::bigint, 0)      AS logo_app_v
                  FROM public.company_profile WHERE id = 1")->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) ?: [];
            if ($company) {
                $company['has_logo_document'] = set_bool($company['has_logo_document']);
                $company['has_logo_app']      = set_bool($company['has_logo_app']);
                $company['logo_document_v']   = (int)$company['logo_document_v'];
                $company['logo_app_v']        = (int)$company['logo_app_v'];
            }

            $finance = $db->query("SELECT base_currency_id, report_currency_id, default_rate_type, rate_basis_pl,
                                          fiscal_year_start_month, amount_decimals
                                     FROM public.finance_settings WHERE id = 1")->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) ?: null;
            if ($finance) {
                $finance['base_currency_id']        = (int)$finance['base_currency_id'];
                $finance['report_currency_id']      = (int)$finance['report_currency_id'];
                $finance['fiscal_year_start_month'] = (int)$finance['fiscal_year_start_month'];
                $finance['amount_decimals']         = (int)$finance['amount_decimals'];
            }

            $currencies = $db->query("SELECT id, init, name FROM public.m_currency ORDER BY init ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($currencies as &$c) $c['id'] = (int)$c['id'];

            sendJsonResponse(true, [
                'company'          => $company,
                'finance'          => $finance,
                'currencies'       => $currencies,
                'base_lock_reason' => set_base_lock_reason($db),
            ]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Simpan profil perusahaan
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'save_company':
            set_require_write();
            try {
                $name  = set_text('company_name', 150, true, 'Nama perusahaan');
                $legal = set_text('legal_name', 200);
                $addr  = set_text('address', 1000);
                $city  = set_text('city', 100);
                $prov  = set_text('province', 100);
                $post  = set_text('postal_code', 20);
                $ctry  = set_text('country', 100);
                $phone = set_text('phone', 50);
                $email = set_text('email', 150);
                $web   = set_text('website', 150);
                $tax   = set_text('tax_id', 50);
                if ($email !== null && !filter_var($email, FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) {
                    throw new SetBusinessError('Format email tidak valid.');
                }
            } catch (SetBusinessError $e) {
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            }

            $st = $db->prepare("
                INSERT INTO public.company_profile
                    (id, company_name, legal_name, address, city, province, postal_code, country,
                     phone, email, website, tax_id, updated_by)
                VALUES (1, :name, :legal, :addr, :city, :prov, :post, :ctry, :phone, :email, :web, :tax, :uid)
                ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
                    company_name = EXCLUDED.company_name, legal_name = EXCLUDED.legal_name,
                    address = EXCLUDED.address, city = EXCLUDED.city, province = EXCLUDED.province,
                    postal_code = EXCLUDED.postal_code, country = EXCLUDED.country,
                    phone = EXCLUDED.phone, email = EXCLUDED.email, website = EXCLUDED.website,
                    tax_id = EXCLUDED.tax_id, updated_at = NOW(), updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by");
            $st->execute([':name' => $name, ':legal' => $legal, ':addr' => $addr, ':city' => $city, ':prov' => $prov,
                          ':post' => $post, ':ctry' => $ctry, ':phone' => $phone, ':email' => $email,
                          ':web' => $web, ':tax' => $tax, ':uid' => $userId]);
            sendJsonResponse(true, [], 'Profil perusahaan disimpan.');
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Simpan pelengkap dokumen (footer + penandatangan)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'save_document':
            set_require_write();
            try {
                $footer = set_text('doc_footer', 1000);
                $sname  = set_text('doc_signer_name', 150);
                $stitle = set_text('doc_signer_title', 100);
            } catch (SetBusinessError $e) {
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            }
            $st = $db->prepare("
                INSERT INTO public.company_profile (id, doc_footer, doc_signer_name, doc_signer_title, updated_by)
                VALUES (1, :f, :n, :t, :uid)
                ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
                    doc_footer = EXCLUDED.doc_footer, doc_signer_name = EXCLUDED.doc_signer_name,
                    doc_signer_title = EXCLUDED.doc_signer_title, updated_at = NOW(), updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by");
            $st->execute([':f' => $footer, ':n' => $sname, ':t' => $stitle, ':uid' => $userId]);
            sendJsonResponse(true, [], 'Pengaturan dokumen disimpan.');
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Simpan pengaturan keuangan
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'save_finance':
            set_require_write();
            $base   = (int)($_POST['base_currency_id'] ?? 0);
            $report = (int)($_POST['report_currency_id'] ?? 0);
            $rtype  = (string)($_POST['default_rate_type'] ?? '');
            $basis  = (string)($_POST['rate_basis_pl'] ?? '');
            $month  = (int)($_POST['fiscal_year_start_month'] ?? 0);
            $dec    = (int)($_POST['amount_decimals'] ?? -1);

            try {
                if (!$base || !set_currency_exists($db, $base))     throw new SetBusinessError('Mata uang dasar wajib dipilih.');
                if (!$report || !set_currency_exists($db, $report)) throw new SetBusinessError('Mata uang laporan keuangan wajib dipilih.');
                if (!in_array($rtype, SET_RATE_TYPES, true))        throw new SetBusinessError('Jenis kurs tidak valid.');
                if (!in_array($basis, SET_RATE_BASIS, true))        throw new SetBusinessError('Dasar kurs laba rugi tidak valid.');
                if ($month < 1 || $month > 12)                      throw new SetBusinessError('Bulan awal tahun fiskal harus 1-12.');
                if ($dec < 0 || $dec > 4)                           throw new SetBusinessError('Jumlah desimal harus 0-4.');

                $cur = $db->query("SELECT base_currency_id FROM public.finance_settings WHERE id = 1")->fetchColumn();
                if ($cur !== false && (int)$cur !== $base) {
                    $lock = set_base_lock_reason($db);
                    if ($lock !== null) {
                        throw new SetBusinessError('Mata uang dasar tidak dapat diubah. ' . $lock);
                    }
                }
            } catch (SetBusinessError $e) {
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $e->getMessage());
            }

            $st = $db->prepare("
                INSERT INTO public.finance_settings
                    (id, base_currency_id, report_currency_id, default_rate_type, rate_basis_pl,
                     fiscal_year_start_month, amount_decimals, updated_by)
                VALUES (1, :b, :r, :t, :p, :m, :d, :uid)
                ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
                    base_currency_id = EXCLUDED.base_currency_id, report_currency_id = EXCLUDED.report_currency_id,
                    default_rate_type = EXCLUDED.default_rate_type, rate_basis_pl = EXCLUDED.rate_basis_pl,
                    fiscal_year_start_month = EXCLUDED.fiscal_year_start_month,
                    amount_decimals = EXCLUDED.amount_decimals, updated_at = NOW(), updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by");
            $st->execute([':b' => $base, ':r' => $report, ':t' => $rtype, ':p' => $basis,
                          ':m' => $month, ':d' => $dec, ':uid' => $userId]);
            sendJsonResponse(true, [], 'Pengaturan keuangan disimpan.');
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Upload logo (multipart: type = document|app, file)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'upload_logo':
            set_require_write();
            $type = (string)($_POST['type'] ?? '');
            if (!isset(SET_LOGO_COLS[$type])) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Jenis logo tidak dikenal.');
            $col = SET_LOGO_COLS[$type];

            $f = $_FILES['file'] ?? null;
            if (!$f || $f['error'] !== UPLOAD_ERR_OK) {
                $msg = ($f && in_array($f['error'], [UPLOAD_ERR_INI_SIZE, UPLOAD_ERR_FORM_SIZE], true))
                    ? 'Ukuran file melebihi batas server.' : 'File logo belum dipilih atau gagal diunggah.';
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], $msg);
            }
            if ($f['size'] > SET_MAX_LOGO) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Ukuran logo maksimal 1 MB.');
            if (!is_uploaded_file($f['tmp_name'])) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Berkas tidak valid.');

            $mime = (new finfo(FILEINFO_MIME_TYPE))->file($f['tmp_name']);
            if (!in_array($mime, SET_LOGO_MIMES, true)) {
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Format logo harus PNG, JPEG, atau WEBP.');
            }
            $info = @getimagesize($f['tmp_name']);
            if (!$info) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'File bukan gambar yang valid.');
            if ($info[0] > SET_MAX_PIXEL || $info[1] > SET_MAX_PIXEL) {
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Dimensi logo maksimal ' . SET_MAX_PIXEL . ' x ' . SET_MAX_PIXEL . ' piksel.');
            }

            $db->exec("INSERT INTO public.company_profile (id) VALUES (1) ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING");
            $fp = fopen($f['tmp_name'], 'rb');
            $st = $db->prepare("UPDATE public.company_profile
                                   SET {$col} = :img, {$col}_mime = :mime, {$col}_updated_at = NOW(),
                                       updated_at = NOW(), updated_by = :uid
                                 WHERE id = 1");
            $st->bindParam(':img', $fp, PDO::PARAM_LOB);
            $st->bindValue(':mime', $mime);
            $st->bindValue(':uid', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $st->execute();
            fclose($fp);
            sendJsonResponse(true, [], 'Logo berhasil diunggah.');
            break;

        case 'delete_logo':
            set_require_write();
            $type = (string)($_POST['type'] ?? '');
            if (!isset(SET_LOGO_COLS[$type])) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Jenis logo tidak dikenal.');
            $col = SET_LOGO_COLS[$type];
            $db->prepare("UPDATE public.company_profile
                             SET {$col} = NULL, {$col}_mime = NULL, {$col}_updated_at = NOW(),
                                 updated_at = NOW(), updated_by = :uid WHERE id = 1")
               ->execute([':uid' => $userId]);
            sendJsonResponse(true, [], 'Logo dihapus.');
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Alirkan gambar logo (GET ?task=logo&type=document|app&v=...)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'logo':
            $type = (string)($_GET['type'] ?? '');
            if (!isset(SET_LOGO_COLS[$type])) { http_response_code(404); if (ob_get_length()) ob_clean(); exit; }
            $col = SET_LOGO_COLS[$type];

            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT {$col}, {$col}_mime FROM public.company_profile WHERE id = 1");
            $st->execute();
            $st->bindColumn(1, $img, PDO::PARAM_LOB);
            $st->bindColumn(2, $mime);
            if (!$st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_BOUND) || $img === null) {
                http_response_code(404);
                if (ob_get_length()) ob_clean();
                exit;
            }
            $bin = is_resource($img) ? stream_get_contents($img) : $img;
            if (is_string($bin) && strncmp($bin, '\\x', 2) === 0) {           // bytea format hex (jaga-jaga)
                $bin = hex2bin(substr($bin, 2));
            }
            if (!in_array($mime, SET_LOGO_MIMES, true) || $bin === '' || $bin === false) {
                http_response_code(404);
                if (ob_get_length()) ob_clean();
                exit;
            }
            if (ob_get_length()) { ob_clean(); }
            header('Content-Type: ' . $mime);
            header('Content-Length: ' . strlen($bin));
            header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
            header('Cache-Control: private, max-age=86400');
            echo $bin;
            exit;

        default:
            sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Aksi/Task tidak dikenal.');
            break;
    }
} catch (PDOException $e) {
    error_log('[settingsCRD] ' . $e->getMessage());
    if ($task === 'logo') { http_response_code(500); if (ob_get_length()) ob_clean(); exit; }
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Database Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    error_log('[settingsCRD] ' . $e->getMessage());
    if ($task === 'logo') { http_response_code(500); if (ob_get_length()) ob_clean(); exit; }
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'System Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
}

## File: ./balancesheetCRD.php
