<?php
/*
  Document      : Balance Sheet (Neraca) - CRUD & Report Handler
<<<<<<< Updated upstream
  Backend       : /rab-project/dev/php-crud/financial_balance_sheetCRD.php
=======
  Backend       : /rab-project/live/php-crud/financial_balance_sheetCRD.php
>>>>>>> Stashed changes
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task   = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read_balance_sheet';
$access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
$db     = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==============================================================
    // READ: Data Periode
    case 'read_periods':
        $sql = "SELECT id, period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods ORDER BY start_date DESC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // READ: Laporan Neraca Utama
    case 'read_balance_sheet':
        $periodId = intval($_POST['period_id'] ?? $_REQUEST['period_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($periodId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode akuntansi wajib dipilih.']);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtP = $db->prepare("SELECT period_name, end_date FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtP->execute(['id' => $periodId]);
        $period = $stmtP->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if (!$period) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode tidak ditemukan.']);
            exit;
        }
        $asOfDate = $period['end_date'];

        // Saldo Akun Riil
        $sql = "
            SELECT c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type,
                   COALESCE(SUM(
                       CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'asset' THEN l.debit - l.credit
                            WHEN c.account_type IN ('liability', 'equity') THEN l.credit - l.debit
                            ELSE 0 END
                   ), 0) AS balance
            FROM chart_of_accounts c
            LEFT JOIN journal_lines l ON l.account_id = c.id
            LEFT JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
                AND j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')
                AND j.journal_date <= :as_of_date
            WHERE c.is_active = true
              AND c.account_type IN ('asset', 'liability', 'equity')
            GROUP BY c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type
            ORDER BY c.account_code ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['as_of_date' => $asOfDate]);
        $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        // Laba/Rugi Berjalan s/d $asOfDate
        $sqlPL = "
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(
                       CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'revenue' THEN l.credit - l.debit
                            WHEN c.account_type = 'expense' THEN l.debit - l.credit
                            ELSE 0 END
                   ), 0) AS net_profit
            FROM chart_of_accounts c
            JOIN journal_lines l ON l.account_id = c.id
            JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
            WHERE j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')
              AND j.journal_date <= :as_of_date
              AND c.account_type IN ('revenue', 'expense')
        ";
        $stmtPL = $db->prepare($sqlPL);
        $stmtPL->execute(['as_of_date' => $asOfDate]);
        $currentEarnings = floatval($stmtPL->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['net_profit'] ?? 0);

        $assets      = [];
        $liabilities = [];
        $equities    = [];
        
        $totalAsset     = 0;
        $totalLiability = 0;
        $totalEquity    = 0;

        foreach ($rows as $r) {
            $balance = floatval($r['balance']);
            $item = [
                'id'           => $r['id'],
                'account_code' => $r['account_code'],
                'account_name' => $r['account_name'],
                'balance'      => $balance
            ];

            if ($r['account_type'] === 'asset') {
                $assets[]   = $item;
                $totalAsset += $balance;
            } else if ($r['account_type'] === 'liability') {
                $liabilities[]   = $item;
                $totalLiability += $balance;
            } else if ($r['account_type'] === 'equity') {
                $equities[]   = $item;
                $totalEquity += $balance;
            }
        }

        if (abs($currentEarnings) > 0.001) {
            $equities[] = [
                'id'           => 0,
                'account_code' => '-',
                'account_name' => 'Laba / (Rugi) Periode Berjalan',
                'balance'      => $currentEarnings
            ];
            $totalEquity += $currentEarnings;
        }

        $totalLiabilityEquity = $totalLiability + $totalEquity;

        echo json_encode([
            'success'                => true,
            'period_name'            => $period['period_name'],
            'as_of_date'             => $asOfDate,
            'assets'                 => $assets,
            'liabilities'            => $liabilities,
            'equities'               => $equities,
            'total_asset'            => $totalAsset,
            'total_liability'        => $totalLiability,
            'total_equity'           => $totalEquity,
            'total_liability_equity' => $totalLiabilityEquity,
            'is_balanced'            => abs($totalAsset - $totalLiabilityEquity) < 0.01
        ]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // CREATE: Tambah Akun Neraca Baru (Asset / Liability / Equity)
    case 'create':
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $code = trim($_POST['account_code'] ?? '');
        $name = trim($_POST['account_name'] ?? '');
        $type = trim($_POST['account_type'] ?? '');

        if (empty($code) || empty($name) || !in_array($type, ['asset', 'liability', 'equity'])) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Kode, Nama, dan Tipe (asset/liability/equity) wajib diisi.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->insert('chart_of_accounts', [
            'account_code' => $code,
            'account_name' => $name,
            'account_type' => $type,
            'is_active'    => true
        ]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Akun Neraca berhasil ditambahkan.']);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // UPDATE: Update Akun Neraca
    case 'update':
        if ($access < 3) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id   = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $code = trim($_POST['account_code'] ?? '');
        $name = trim($_POST['account_name'] ?? '');

        if ($id <= 0 || empty($code) || empty($name)) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID, Kode, dan Nama Akun tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->update('chart_of_accounts', [
            'account_code' => $code,
            'account_name' => $name
        ], "id = {$id}");
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Akun Neraca berhasil diperbarui.']);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // DELETE: Soft Delete Akun Neraca
    case 'delete':
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID Akun tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->update('chart_of_accounts', ['is_active' => false], "id = {$id}");
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Akun Neraca berhasil dinonaktifkan.']);
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task)]);
}

## File: ./m.fiscalperiodCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Fiscal Period - CRUD Handler
  Called from   : js/m.fiscalperiod.js
  Note          : Mengelola tabel accounting_periods (bukan fiscal_periods --
                  tabel itu sempat dibuat keliru & sudah di-drop). Struktur
                  aslinya lebih simpel: id, period_name, start_date, end_date,
                  is_closed -- tanpa period_year/period_month terpisah.
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read':
        $query = $_POST['query'] ?? null;
        $srch  = searchData($query, ['period_name']);

        $where = "1=1" . ($srch['searchSQL'] ?? '');
        $limit = $srch['limit'] ?? 250;

        $sql = "
            SELECT id, period_name, start_date, end_date, is_closed
            FROM accounting_periods
            WHERE {$where}
            ORDER BY start_date DESC
            LIMIT " . intval($limit);

        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'status' => 'Success', 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_edit':
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $stmt = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
        $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $row = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => (bool)$row, 'data' => $row ?: new stdClass()]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // Combo utk dropdown pilih periode di modul Journal Entry dkk
    // (hanya periode yg masih terbuka)
    case 'read_open':
        $sql = "SELECT id, period_name FROM accounting_periods WHERE is_closed = false ORDER BY start_date";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'create':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $periodName = trim($_POST['period_name'] ?? '');
        $startDate  = trim($_POST['start_date'] ?? '');
        $endDate    = trim($_POST['end_date'] ?? '');

        if (empty($periodName) || empty($startDate) || empty($endDate)) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Nama periode, tanggal mulai, dan tanggal selesai wajib diisi.']);
            exit;
        }
        if (strtotime($endDate) < strtotime($startDate)) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Tanggal selesai tidak boleh sebelum tanggal mulai.']);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM accounting_periods WHERE period_name = :name");
        $stmtChk->execute(['name' => $periodName]);
        if ($stmtChk->fetch()) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Periode dengan nama ini sudah ada.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->insert('accounting_periods', [
            'period_name' => $periodName,
            'start_date'  => $startDate,
            'end_date'    => $endDate,
            'is_closed'   => false
        ]);
        echo $db->getResult();
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // Tutup Buku -- generate jurnal penutup (pindahkan saldo Pendapatan/
    // Beban periode ini ke akun Laba Ditahan), lalu kunci periode.
    case 'close':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Hanya akses tertinggi yang boleh menutup periode.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

        $stmtPer = $db->prepare("SELECT period_name, start_date, end_date, is_closed FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtPer->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $period = $stmtPer->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if (!$period) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Periode tidak ditemukan.']);
            exit;
        }
        if ($period['is_closed']) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Periode ini sudah ditutup.']);
            exit;
        }

        // 1. Wajib: tidak boleh ada jurnal Draft yang belum diselesaikan di periode ini
        $stmtDraft = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM journal_entries WHERE period_id = :id AND status = 'draft'");
        $stmtDraft->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $draftCount = intval($stmtDraft->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['cnt']);
        if ($draftCount > 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => "Tidak bisa tutup buku: masih ada {$draftCount} jurnal berstatus Draft di periode ini. Post atau hapus dulu sebelum menutup periode."]);
            exit;
        }

        // 2. Cari akun Laba Ditahan (Retained Earnings) -- coba kode 3200 dulu,
        //    fallback cari berdasarkan nama kalau kode berbeda.
        $stmtRE = $db->prepare("SELECT id, account_name FROM chart_of_accounts WHERE account_code = '3200' AND is_active = true LIMIT 1");
        $stmtRE->execute();
        $reAccount = $stmtRE->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if (!$reAccount) {
            $stmtRE2 = $db->prepare("SELECT id, account_name FROM chart_of_accounts WHERE account_type = 'equity' AND account_name ILIKE '%laba ditahan%' AND is_active = true LIMIT 1");
            $stmtRE2->execute();
            $reAccount = $stmtRE2->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        }
        if (!$reAccount) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Akun Laba Ditahan tidak ditemukan (cari kode 3200 atau nama mengandung "Laba Ditahan"). Buat akun ini dulu di Chart of Accounts sebelum tutup buku.']);
            exit;
        }
        $retainedEarningsId = $reAccount['id'];

        // 3. Hitung saldo Pendapatan & Beban KHUSUS periode ini
        $sqlIS = "
            SELECT c.id, c.account_type,
                   COALESCE(SUM(
                       CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'revenue' THEN l.credit - l.debit
                            ELSE l.debit - l.credit END
                   ), 0) AS amount
            FROM chart_of_accounts c
            JOIN journal_lines l ON l.account_id = c.id
            JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
                AND j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')
                AND j.journal_date BETWEEN :start_date AND :end_date
            WHERE c.is_active = true AND c.account_type IN ('revenue', 'expense')
            GROUP BY c.id, c.account_type
            HAVING COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'revenue' THEN l.credit - l.debit ELSE l.debit - l.credit END), 0) != 0
        ";
        $stmtIS = $db->prepare($sqlIS);
        $stmtIS->execute(['start_date' => $period['start_date'], 'end_date' => $period['end_date']]);
        $isRows = $stmtIS->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $closingLines = [];
        $revenueTotal = 0;
        $expenseTotal = 0;
        foreach ($isRows as $r) {
            $amt = floatval($r['amount']);
            if ($r['account_type'] === 'revenue') {
                // Nolkan revenue: DEBIT sejumlah saldo kreditnya
                $closingLines[] = ['account_id' => $r['id'], 'debit' => $amt, 'credit' => 0];
                $revenueTotal += $amt;
            } else {
                // Nolkan expense: KREDIT sejumlah saldo debitnya
                $closingLines[] = ['account_id' => $r['id'], 'debit' => 0, 'credit' => $amt];
                $expenseTotal += $amt;
            }
        }

        $netIncome = $revenueTotal - $expenseTotal;
        $closingRefNo = null;

        // Kalau tidak ada aktivitas Pendapatan/Beban sama sekali di periode ini,
        // tidak perlu bikin jurnal penutup -- langsung kunci periode saja.
        if (!empty($closingLines)) {
            // Baris penyeimbang ke Laba Ditahan
            if ($netIncome >= 0) {
                $closingLines[] = ['account_id' => $retainedEarningsId, 'debit' => 0, 'credit' => $netIncome];
            } else {
                $closingLines[] = ['account_id' => $retainedEarningsId, 'debit' => abs($netIncome), 'credit' => 0];
            }

            // Verifikasi balance sebelum insert (jaga-jaga)
            $sumDebit = array_sum(array_column($closingLines, 'debit'));
            $sumCredit = array_sum(array_column($closingLines, 'credit'));
            if (abs($sumDebit - $sumCredit) > 0.01) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Perhitungan jurnal penutup tidak balance, tutup buku dibatalkan. Hubungi administrator.']);
                exit;
            }

            $closingRefNo = CreateRefNo('journal_entries', 'ref_no', 'JCL');
            $db->insert('journal_entries', [
                'ref_no'         => $closingRefNo,
                'journal_date'   => $period['end_date'],
                'period_id'      => $id,
                'reference_type' => 'closing_entry',
                'reference_id'   => 0,
                'description'    => 'Jurnal Penutup - ' . $period['period_name'],
                'status'         => 'posted',
                'posted_by'      => $_SESSION['user_id'],
                'posted_at'      => date('Y-m-d H:i:s')
            ]);

            $stmtNewId = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM journal_entries WHERE ref_no = :no");
            $stmtNewId->execute(['no' => $closingRefNo]);
            $closingJournalId = $stmtNewId->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['id'] ?? null;

            if ($closingJournalId) {
                foreach ($closingLines as $l) {
                    $db->insert('journal_lines', [
                        'journal_id'  => $closingJournalId,
                        'account_id'  => $l['account_id'],
                        'debit'       => $l['debit'],
                        'credit'      => $l['credit'],
                        'description' => 'Tutup buku ' . $period['period_name']
                    ]);
                }
            }
        }

        // 4. Kunci periode
        $db->update('accounting_periods', ['is_closed' => true], "id = {$id}");

        $msg = $closingRefNo
            ? "Periode berhasil ditutup. Jurnal penutup: {$closingRefNo} (Laba/Rugi periode: " . number_format($netIncome, 2, ',', '.') . ")"
            : "Periode berhasil ditutup. Tidak ada aktivitas Pendapatan/Beban di periode ini, jurnal penutup tidak diperlukan.";
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => $msg]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'reopen':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Hanya akses tertinggi yang boleh membuka kembali periode.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

        $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM journal_entries WHERE period_id = :id AND reference_type = 'closing_entry' AND status = 'posted'");
        $stmtChk->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $hasClosing = intval($stmtChk->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['cnt']) > 0;

        $db->update('accounting_periods', ['is_closed' => false], "id = {$id}");

        $msg = 'Periode berhasil dibuka kembali.';
        if ($hasClosing) {
            $msg .= ' PERHATIAN: periode ini punya jurnal penutup yang sudah posting -- kalau mau input jurnal baru lagi di periode ini, reverse dulu jurnal penutupnya lewat menu Journal Entry supaya tidak double-hitung Laba Ditahan saat tutup buku berikutnya.';
        }
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => $msg]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'delete':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

        $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT is_closed FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtChk->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $row = $stmtChk->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if ($row && $row['is_closed']) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Periode yang sudah ditutup tidak boleh dihapus.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->query("DELETE FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = {$id}");
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Periode berhasil dihapus.']);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'status' => 'Error', 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task)]);
}

## File: ./invoiceCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Sales Invoice - CRUD Handler
  Called from   : js/m.proyekoice.js
  Note          : - Status: draft -> final. Finalisasi terjadi otomatis saat
                    tombol Print diklik (task 'finalize', dipanggil sebelum
                    window print dibuka).
                  - Saat finalize, jurnal otomatis di-generate via
                    AutoPostJournal() (rule 'SLSCREDIT': Debit Piutang Usaha,
                    Kredit Pendapatan Penjualan) -- lihat function.all.php.
                  - Invoice draft masih bisa diedit/dihapus bebas. Invoice
                    final terkunci (tidak bisa edit/hapus lagi).
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read':
        $query = $_POST['query'] ?? null;
        $srch  = searchData($query, ['i.proyekoice_no']);

        $where = "1=1" . ($srch['searchSQL'] ?? '');
        $limit = $srch['limit'] ?? 250;

        $sql = "
            SELECT i.id, i.proyekoice_no, i.so_id, so.ref_no AS so_ref_no,
                   i.customer_id, c.name AS customer_name,
                   i.proyekoice_date, i.due_date, i.total_amount, i.paid_amount, i.status
            FROM proyekoices i
            LEFT JOIN sales_order so ON so.id = i.so_id
            LEFT JOIN m_customer c ON c.id = i.customer_id
            WHERE {$where}
            ORDER BY i.proyekoice_date DESC, i.id DESC
            LIMIT " . intval($limit);

        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'status' => 'Success', 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_edit':
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $stmt = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM proyekoices WHERE id = :id");
        $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $row = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => (bool)$row, 'data' => $row ?: new stdClass()]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_items':
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => 0, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }
        $stmt = $db->prepare("SELECT id AS line_id, description, qty, unit_price, subtotal FROM proyekoice_items WHERE proyekoice_id = :id ORDER BY id ASC");
        $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // Combo Sales Order (untuk field so_id, wajib diisi)
    case 'read_so':
        $sql = "
            SELECT so.id, so.ref_no, so.tx_date, mc.name AS legacy_customer_name
            FROM sales_order so 
            LEFT JOIN m_customer mc ON mc.id = so.customer_id
            WHERE (so.active = true or so.active IS null) 
                and so.id NOT IN (
            	    SELECT distinct i.so_id FROM proyekoice_items t 
            	    left join proyekoices i on i.id=t.proyekoice_id
            	    WHERE t.so_item_id IS NOT NULL
              	)
            ORDER BY so.tx_date DESC
            LIMIT 200
        ";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // Item dari Sales Order terpilih yang BELUM pernah dipakai di proyekoice
    // manapun (so_item_id belum muncul di proyekoice_items manapun).
    case 'read_so_items':
        $soId = intval($_POST['so_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($soId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }
        $sql = "
      SELECT soi.id AS so_item_id, soi.qty_unit as qty, 
       case
                when soi.disc_percent is true then (coalesce(soi.unit_price,0) - (coalesce(soi.unit_price,0) * (coalesce(soi.disc,0) / 100)))
                else (coalesce(soi.unit_price,0) - coalesce(soi.disc,0))
            end::numeric(14,2) as unit_price,
        case
                when soi.disc_percent is true then (coalesce(soi.unit_price,0) - (coalesce(soi.unit_price,0) * (coalesce(soi.disc,0) / 100))) * coalesce(soi.qty_unit,0)
                else (coalesce(soi.unit_price,0) - coalesce(soi.disc,0)) * soi.qty_unit
            end::numeric(14,2) as subtotal,
       i.name AS product_name
            FROM sales_order_item soi
            LEFT JOIN items i  ON i.id = soi.item_id
            WHERE soi.order_id= :so_id
              AND soi.id NOT IN (
                  SELECT so_item_id FROM proyekoice_items WHERE so_item_id IS NOT NULL
              )
            ORDER BY soi.id asc
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['so_id' => $soId]);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        // Fallback deskripsi: pakai nama produk kalau kolom description kosong
        foreach ($data as &$d) {
            if (empty($d['description'])) $d['description'] = $d['product_name'];
        }
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // Combo Customer (untuk pilih manual kalau perlu override)
    case 'read_customer':
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM m_customer WHERE active = true ORDER BY name ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'create':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        saveInvoice($db, null, $_POST);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'update':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 3) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID proyekoice tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }
        $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT status FROM proyekoices WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtChk->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $existing = $stmtChk->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if ($existing && $existing['status'] !== 'draft') {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Hanya proyekoice berstatus Draft yang boleh diedit.']);
            exit;
        }
        saveInvoice($db, $id, $_POST);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    // Finalize -- dipanggil otomatis oleh JS saat tombol Print diklik.
    // Ubah status draft -> final, DAN generate jurnal otomatis (Tahap 6).
    case 'finalize':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

        $stmtInv = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM proyekoices WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtInv->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $proyekoice = $stmtInv->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if (!$proyekoice) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Invoice tidak ditemukan.']);
            exit;
        }

        // Kalau sudah final sebelumnya, tidak perlu proses ulang (hindari jurnal dobel)
        if ($proyekoice['status'] === 'final') {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Invoice sudah final.', 'already_final' => true]);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtUpd = $db->prepare("UPDATE proyekoices SET status = 'final' WHERE id = :id AND status = 'draft'");
        $stmtUpd->execute(['id' => $id]);
        if ($stmtUpd->rowCount() === 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal finalisasi -- status proyekoice sudah berubah, silakan reload.']);
            exit;
        }

        // ---- Tahap 6: Auto-posting jurnal ----
        $jr = AutoPostJournal($db, [
            'rule_code'      => 'SLSCREDIT',
            'reference_type' => 'sales_proyekoice',
            'reference_id'   => $id,
            'journal_date'   => $proyekoice['proyekoice_date'],
            'amount'         => $proyekoice['total_amount'],
            'description'    => 'Invoice ' . $proyekoice['proyekoice_no']
        ]);

        if (!$jr['success']) {
            // Invoice tetap final (sudah terlanjur), tapi kasih tahu jurnal gagal
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Invoice berhasil difinalisasi, TAPI jurnal otomatis GAGAL dibuat: ' . $jr['message'] . ' -- perlu jurnal manual susulan.']);
            exit;
        }

        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Invoice berhasil difinalisasi. ' . $jr['message']]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'delete':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

        $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT status FROM proyekoices WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtChk->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $row = $stmtChk->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if ($row && $row['status'] !== 'draft') {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Hanya proyekoice berstatus Draft yang boleh dihapus.']);
            exit;
        }

        $db->query("DELETE FROM proyekoice_items WHERE proyekoice_id = {$id}");
        $db->query("DELETE FROM proyekoices WHERE id = {$id}");
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Invoice berhasil dihapus.']);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task)]);
}

function saveInvoice($db, $id, $post) {
    $soId       = intval($post['so_id'] ?? 0);
    $customerId = intval($post['customer_id'] ?? 0);
    $proyekDate    = trim($post['proyekoice_date'] ?? '');
    $dueDate    = trim($post['due_date'] ?? '') ?: null;
    $itemsJson  = $post['arrItems'] ?? '[]';
    $items      = json_decode($itemsJson, true);

    if ($soId <= 0) {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Sales Order wajib dipilih.']);
        return;
    }
    if ($customerId <= 0) {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Customer wajib dipilih.']);
        return;
    }
    if (empty($proyekDate)) {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Tanggal proyekoice wajib diisi.']);
        return;
    }
    if (!is_array($items) || count($items) < 1) {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Invoice minimal harus punya 1 item.']);
        return;
    }

    $validItems = [];
    $totalAmount = 0;
    foreach ($items as $it) {
        $desc = trim($it['description'] ?? '');
        $qty = Str2N($it['qty'] ?? 0);
        $price = Str2N($it['unit_price'] ?? 0);
        $soItemId = !empty($it['so_item_id']) ? intval($it['so_item_id']) : null;
        if (empty($desc) || $qty <= 0) continue;
        $subtotal = $qty * $price;
        $totalAmount += $subtotal;
        $validItems[] = ['description' => $desc, 'qty' => $qty, 'unit_price' => $price, 'subtotal' => $subtotal, 'so_item_id' => $soItemId];
    }
    if (count($validItems) < 1) {
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Tidak ada item valid (deskripsi & qty wajib diisi).']);
        return;
    }

    $headerData = [
        'so_id'        => $soId,
        'customer_id'  => $customerId,
        'proyekoice_date' => $proyekDate,
        'due_date'     => $dueDate,
        'total_amount' => $totalAmount
    ];

    if ($id === null) {
        $proyekoiceNo = CreateRefNo('proyekoices', 'proyekoice_no', 'INV');
        $headerData['proyekoice_no']  = $proyekoiceNo;
        $headerData['paid_amount'] = 0;
        $headerData['status']      = 'draft';
        $headerData['created_by']  = $_SESSION['user_id'];

        $db->insert('proyekoices', $headerData);

        $stmtId = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM proyekoices WHERE proyekoice_no = :no");
        $stmtId->execute(['no' => $proyekoiceNo]);
        $newId = $stmtId->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['id'] ?? null;

        if ($newId) insertItems($db, $newId, $validItems);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Invoice {$proyekoiceNo} berhasil disimpan."]);
    } else {
        $db->update('proyekoices', $headerData, "id = {$id}");
        $db->query("DELETE FROM proyekoice_items WHERE proyekoice_id = {$id}");
        insertItems($db, $id, $validItems);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Invoice berhasil diperbarui.']);
    }
}

function insertItems($db, $proyekoiceId, $items) {
    foreach ($items as $it) {
        $db->insert('proyekoice_items', [
            'proyekoice_id'  => $proyekoiceId,
            'so_item_id'  => $it['so_item_id'] ?? null,
            'description' => $it['description'],
            'qty'         => $it['qty'],
            'unit_price'  => $it['unit_price'],
            'subtotal'    => $it['subtotal']
        ]);
    }
}

## File: ./trialbalanceCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Trial Balance - Report Handler
  Called from   : js/m.trialbalance.js
  Note          : - Laporan read-only.
                  - Saldo dihitung sebagai akumulasi SEMUA transaksi posted/
                    reversed sampai dengan tanggal akhir periode terpilih
                    (bukan cuma mutasi dalam periode itu saja -- trial balance
                    adalah saldo KUMULATIF per titik waktu tertentu).
                  - Saldo ditampilkan di kolom Debit kalau positif untuk akun
                    normal-debit (asset/expense), sebaliknya di kolom Kredit.
                    Kalau saldo "terbalik" (kontra), tetap ditampilkan di
                    kolom lawan supaya total tetap balance secara matematis.
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once   '../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once   '../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read_trial_balance';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_periods':
        $sql = "SELECT id, period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods ORDER BY start_date DESC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_trial_balance':
        $periodId = intval($_POST['period_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($periodId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode wajib dipilih.']);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtP = $db->prepare("SELECT period_name, end_date FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtP->execute(['id' => $periodId]);
        $period = $stmtP->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if (!$period) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode tidak ditemukan.']);
            exit;
        }
        $asOfDate = $period['end_date'];

        $sql = "
            SELECT c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type,
                   COALESCE(SUM(
                       CASE WHEN c.account_type IN ('asset','expense') THEN l.debit - l.credit
                            ELSE l.credit - l.debit END
                   ), 0) AS balance
            FROM chart_of_accounts c
            LEFT JOIN journal_lines l ON l.account_id = c.id
            LEFT JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
                AND j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')
                AND j.journal_date <= :as_of_date
            WHERE c.is_active = true
            GROUP BY c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type
            ORDER BY c.account_code ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['as_of_date' => $asOfDate]);
        $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $data = [];
        $totalDebit = 0;
        $totalCredit = 0;
        foreach ($rows as $r) {
            $isDebitNormal = in_array($r['account_type'], ['asset', 'expense']);
            $balance = floatval($r['balance']);

            $debitCol = 0;
            $creditCol = 0;
            if ($isDebitNormal) {
                if ($balance >= 0) { $debitCol = $balance; } else { $creditCol = abs($balance); }
            } else {
                if ($balance >= 0) { $creditCol = $balance; } else { $debitCol = abs($balance); }
            }

            $totalDebit += $debitCol;
            $totalCredit += $creditCol;

            $data[] = [
                'account_code' => $r['account_code'],
                'account_name' => $r['account_name'],
                'account_type' => $r['account_type'],
                'debit'        => $debitCol,
                'credit'       => $creditCol
            ];
        }

        echo json_encode([
            'success'      => true,
            'period_name'  => $period['period_name'],
            'as_of_date'   => $asOfDate,
            'total_debit'  => $totalDebit,
            'total_credit' => $totalCredit,
            'is_balance'   => abs($totalDebit - $totalCredit) < 0.01,
            'totalCount'   => count($data),
            'data'         => $data
        ]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task)]);
}

## File: ./m.department.php

// add w/h id for direct update univ from production
// alter table department_section add column warehouse_id bigint

include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";

$searchField       = isset($_POST["query"]) ? $_POST["query"] : NULL;
$offset            = (isset($_POST["start"]) && $_POST["start"] !== null) ? intval($_POST["start"]) : 0;
$limit             = (isset($_POST["limit"]) && $_POST["limit"] !== null) ? intval($_POST["limit"]) : 250;
$departmentIdParam = isset($_POST["department_id"]) ? intval($_POST["department_id"]) : 0;
$task              = isset($_POST['task']) ? $_POST['task'] : 'read';

// Inisialisasi objek database secara global
$db = new crud();

switch ($task) {
	case ("create"):
	case ("add"):
		$queryC = '';
		$rawSection = $_POST["arrSection"] ?? '[]';
		$dataSec = !empty($rawSection) ? json_decode($rawSection) : [];

		$table = "m_department";
		$data = array(
			"name"        => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? ''),
			"seq_no"      => intval($_POST["seq_no"] ?? 0),
			"init"        => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),

			"location_id" => !empty($_POST["location_id"]) ? intval($_POST["location_id"]) : null,
			"active"      => true
		);
		$queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

		if (is_array($dataSec)) {
			foreach ($dataSec as $sec) {
				$table = "department_section";
				$data = array(
					"department_id" => "currval('m_department_id_seq')",
					"seq_no"        => intval($sec->seq_no ?? 0),
					"line_no"       => intval($sec->line_no ?? 0),

					"piece_rate"    => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $sec->piece_rate ?? 0)),
					"share_amt"     => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $sec->share_amt ?? 0)),
					"operator"      => !empty($sec->operator) ? intval($sec->operator) : null,
					"remark"        => checksinglequote($sec->remark ?? '')
				);

        $fields = "dt.id, dt.seq_no, dt.name, dt.init, coalesce(dt.remark,'') remark, l.id location_id, l.name location_name, l.init location_init, dt.active";
        
        $table  = "m_department dt left join m_location l on coalesce(dt.location_id,0)=coalesce(l.id,0)";
        $where  = "dt.active = true " . $searchSQL;
        $order  = "l.name ASC, coalesce(dt.seq_no,0) ASC, dt.name ASC";

        // Pastikan pemanggilan parameter berurutan dengan benar: ($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset)
        // Catatan: Sesuaikan urutan parameter ini dengan struktur asli method select() di crudClass.php Anda
        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
        echo $db->getResult();
	break;
	case ("read_edit"):
		$id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
		$fields = "id, name, seq_no, init, remark, location_id, active";
		$table  = "m_department";
		$where  = "id = " . $id;

		$db->select($table, $fields, $where);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ('read_detail'):
		$fields = "
			s.id, s.department_id, s.seq_no, s.section_name, s.line_no, s.data_output, s.warehouse_id, s.piece_rate,
			s.operator, s.share_amt, s.remark, w.warehouse_name warehouse
		";
		$table  = "
			department_section s
			left join view_warehouse_name_init w on s.warehouse_id = w.warehouse_id
		";
		$where  = "s.department_id = " . $departmentIdParam;
		$order  = "s.seq_no ASC";

		$db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ('HistoryData'):
	case ('read_history'):
		$id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
		$table  = "view_single_table_log";
		$fields = "
			tx_id, date, count,
			case amend_name
				when 'init' then '[EDIT] - Initial'
				when 'seq_no' then '[EDIT] - Seq No.'
				when 'name' then '[EDIT] - Name'
				when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
				when 'location_id' then '[EDIT] - Location'
				else amend_name
			end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
			before_amendment, after_amendment, user_name, user_time
		";
		$where  = "tx_id::int = " . $id . " and table_name = 'm_department'";
		$order  = "count desc";

		$db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ('update'):
		$queryC = '';
		$deptId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
		$rawSection = $_POST["arrSection"] ?? '[]';
		$dataSec = !empty($rawSection) ? json_decode($rawSection) : [];

		// Cek section lama menggunakan method select modular kelas crud
		$checkFields = "id, department_id";
		$checkTable  = "department_section";
		$checkWhere  = "department_id = " . $deptId;
		$db->select($checkTable, $checkFields, $checkWhere, "id ASC");
		$resCheckJSON = $db->getResult();
		$resCheckArr  = json_decode($resCheckJSON, true);
		$dataCheck    = $resCheckArr["data"] ?? [];

		for ($c = 0; $c < count($dataCheck); $c++) {
			$dataFound = 0;
			$cId = $dataCheck[$c]['id'];
			for ($a = 0; $a < count($dataSec); $a++) {
				if (isset($dataSec[$a]->id) && $dataSec[$a]->id == $cId) {
					$dataFound = 1;
					break;
				}
			}
			if ($dataFound == 0) {
				$queryC .= deleteQ("department_section", "id = " . intval($cId)) . ";";
			}
		}

		$table = "m_department";
		$data = array(
			"name"        => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? ''),
			"seq_no"      => intval($_POST["seq_no"] ?? 0),
			"init"        => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),
			"remark"      => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? ''),
			"location_id" => !empty($_POST["location_id"]) ? intval($_POST["location_id"]) : null,
			"active"      => $_POST['i_active'] ?? $_POST["active"] ?? 'true'
		);
		$where = "id = " . $deptId;
		$queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";

		if (is_array($dataSec)) {
			foreach ($dataSec as $sec) {
				$secId = intval($sec->id ?? 0);
				$secData = array(
					"seq_no"       => intval($sec->seq_no ?? 0),
					"line_no"      => intval($sec->line_no ?? 0),
					"section_name" => checksinglequote($sec->section_name ?? ''),
					"data_output"  => !empty($sec->data_output) ? intval($sec->data_output) : null,
					"warehouse_id" => !empty($sec->warehouse_id) ? intval($sec->warehouse_id) : null,
					"piece_rate"   => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $sec->piece_rate ?? 0)),
					"share_amt"    => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $sec->share_amt ?? 0)),
					"operator"     => !empty($sec->operator) ? intval($sec->operator) : null,
					"remark"       => checksinglequote($sec->remark ?? '')
				);

				if ($secId > 0) {
					$queryC .= updateQ("department_section", $secData, "id = " . $secId) . ";";
				} else {
					$secData["department_id"] = $deptId;
					$queryC .= insertQ("department_section", $secData) . ";";
				}
			}
		}

		$db->cmdQ($queryC);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ('delete'):
		$deleteId = intval($_POST["id"] ?? 0);
		if ($deleteId > 0) {
			$table = "m_department";
			$data  = array("active" => false);
			$where = "id = " . $deleteId;

			$queryC = updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";
			$db->cmdQ($queryC);
			echo $db->getResult();
		} else {
			echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID Department tidak valid."]);
		}
	break;

	default:
		echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "Task tidak dikenali."]);
	break;
}
?>

## File: ./purchaseinvoiceCRD.php

<?php
/* Document      : purchaseinvoiceCRD.php
   Description   : Backend CRUD untuk Purchase Invoice
   [FIX] - Tambah pengecekan koneksi DB eksplisit + fallback pesan error
           supaya tidak pernah tampil pesan kosong ke frontend.
         - JOIN supplier diperbaiki: m_suppliers (bukan clients), sesuai FK asli.
         - purchase_invoice_dtl: nama kolom diperbaiki ke invoice_hdr_id /
           grn_hdr_id (sesuai skema asli, sebelumnya invoice_id / grn_dtl_id
           yang TIDAK ADA di tabel -> selalu gagal saat detail dibaca/disimpan).
         - read_invoice_detail: karena grn_hdr_id mengarah ke grn_hdr (level
           dokumen, bukan level item), di-JOIN + GROUP BY ke grn_dtl supaya
           qty/harga/nama item tetap bisa ditampilkan. */

session_start();

if (file_exists('../php-func/function.all.php')) {
    require_once('../php-func/function.all.php');
}

$task = isset($_POST['task']) ? $_POST['task'] : (isset($_GET['task']) ? $_GET['task'] : '');

@ini_set('display_errors', 0);
header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=UTF-8');

// [FIX] Pengecekan koneksi eksplisit. Sebelumnya kode di sini hanya
// mengecek function_exists('pg_connect') === false, yang praktis tidak
// pernah true (pg_connect selalu ada kalau ekstensi pgsql terpasang),
// jadi baris pg_connect() manual di bawahnya tidak pernah jalan. Kalau
// function.all.php gagal membuka koneksi, pg_query() di bawah akan gagal
// TANPA pesan (pg_last_error() bisa balik string kosong kalau memang
// belum ada koneksi aktif sama sekali) -> di frontend muncul pesan
// default "Gagal memuat data main grid." tanpa detail.
if (!function_exists('pg_query')) {
    echo json_encode([
        'success' => false,
        'message' => 'Ekstensi PostgreSQL (pgsql) tidak tersedia di server.'
    ]);
    exit;
}

$testConn = @pg_query("SELECT 1");
if (!$testConn) {
    echo json_encode([
        'success' => false,
        'message' => 'Tidak ada koneksi database aktif. Cek path/isi function.all.php (../php-func/function.all.php) dan pastikan pg_connect() berhasil dipanggil sebelum file ini dieksekusi.'
    ]);
    exit;
}

// [FIX] Helper supaya pesan error TIDAK PERNAH kosong ke frontend.
function pgErrorOrFallback($fallback = 'Terjadi kesalahan pada database.') {
    $err = pg_last_error();
    return $err !== '' ? $err : $fallback;
}

try {
    switch ($task) {
        case 'read':
            $limit  = isset($_POST['limit']) ? intval($_POST['limit']) : 50;
            $start  = isset($_POST['start']) ? intval($_POST['start']) : 0;

            $sqlTotal = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM purchase_invoice_hdr";
            $resTotal = pg_query($sqlTotal);
            if (!$resTotal) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal menghitung total data invoice.'));

            $rowTotal = pg_fetch_assoc($resTotal);
            $totalCount = intval($rowTotal['total']);

            // [FIX] supplier_id FK sebenarnya mengarah ke m_suppliers(id),
            // bukan clients(id) — sebelumnya JOIN ke clients membuat
            // supplier_name selalu kosong/salah.
            $sql = "SELECT h.*, s.name AS supplier_name
                    FROM purchase_invoice_hdr h
                    LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = h.supplier_id
                    ORDER BY h.id DESC LIMIT $limit OFFSET $start";
            $result = pg_query($sql);
            if (!$result) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal memuat data main grid.'));

            $data = [];
            while ($row = pg_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $row['total_amount'] = floatval(isset($row['total_amount']) ? $row['total_amount'] : 0);
                $row['paid_amount'] = floatval(isset($row['paid_amount']) ? $row['paid_amount'] : 0);
                $data[] = $row;
            }

            echo json_encode([
                'success' => true,
                'totalCount' => $totalCount,
                'data' => $data
            ]);
            break;

        case 'read_available_grn':
            // [BARU] Daftar GRN (grn_hdr) yang bisa dipilih untuk di-match ke invoice.
            // Difilter berdasarkan supplier yang sama dengan invoice, status masih
            // open/partial. Agregasi qty/harga dari grn_dtl (lihat catatan di
            // read_invoice_detail soal grn_hdr_id mengarah ke level dokumen).
            $supplier_id = isset($_REQUEST['supplier_id']) ? intval($_REQUEST['supplier_id']) : 0;

            $sql = "SELECT h.id AS grn_hdr_id, h.ref_no AS grn_ref_no, h.grn_date, h.supplier_id,
                           string_agg(DISTINCT gd.item_name, ', ') AS item_name,
                           SUM(gd.qty_received) AS qty_received,
                           CASE WHEN SUM(gd.qty_received) > 0
                                THEN SUM(gd.qty_received * gd.unit_cost) / SUM(gd.qty_received)
                                ELSE 0 END AS unit_cost,
                           SUM(gd.qty_received * gd.unit_cost) AS grn_total,
                           COALESCE(im.already_matched, 0) AS already_matched,
                           SUM(gd.qty_received * gd.unit_cost) - COALESCE(im.already_matched, 0) AS remaining
                    FROM grn_hdr h
                    JOIN grn_dtl gd ON gd.grn_hdr_id = h.id
                    LEFT JOIN (
                        SELECT grn_hdr_id, SUM(matched_amount) AS already_matched
                        FROM purchase_invoice_dtl
                        GROUP BY grn_hdr_id
                    ) im ON im.grn_hdr_id = h.id
                    WHERE h.status IN ('open', 'partial')
                      AND ($1 = 0 OR h.supplier_id = $1)
                    GROUP BY h.id, h.ref_no, h.grn_date, h.supplier_id, im.already_matched
                    -- [BARU] Invoicing partial dibolehkan: GRN tetap muncul selama
                    -- masih ada sisa nilai yang belum di-match ke invoice manapun.
                    HAVING SUM(gd.qty_received * gd.unit_cost) - COALESCE(im.already_matched, 0) > 0
                    ORDER BY h.grn_date DESC";

            $result = pg_query_params($sql, [$supplier_id]);
            if (!$result) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal memuat daftar GRN.'));

            $data = [];
            while ($row = pg_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $row['qty_received'] = floatval($row['qty_received']);
                $row['unit_cost'] = floatval($row['unit_cost']);
                $row['grn_total'] = floatval($row['grn_total']);
                $row['already_matched'] = floatval($row['already_matched']);
                $row['remaining'] = floatval($row['remaining']);
                $data[] = $row;
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
            break;

        case 'read_supplier':
            // [FIX] konsisten pakai m_suppliers juga di sini.
            $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM m_suppliers WHERE active IS NOT FALSE ORDER BY name ASC";
            $result = pg_query($sql);
            if (!$result) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal memuat daftar supplier.'));
            $data = [];
            while ($row = pg_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $data[] = $row;
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
            break;

        case 'read_invoice_detail':
            $invoice_id = isset($_REQUEST['invoice_id']) ? intval($_REQUEST['invoice_id']) : 0;

            // [FIX] Kolom sebelumnya (d.invoice_id, d.grn_dtl_id) tidak ada
            // di tabel purchase_invoice_dtl -> query selalu gagal.
            // grn_hdr_id mengarah ke grn_hdr (dokumen), jadi di-JOIN +
            // GROUP BY ke grn_dtl untuk tetap dapat qty/harga/nama item.
            $sql = "SELECT d.id, d.invoice_hdr_id, d.grn_hdr_id, d.matched_amount, d.remark,
                           h.ref_no AS grn_ref_no,
                           string_agg(DISTINCT gd.item_name, ', ') AS item_name,
                           SUM(gd.qty_received) AS qty_received,
                           CASE WHEN SUM(gd.qty_received) > 0
                                THEN SUM(gd.qty_received * gd.unit_cost) / SUM(gd.qty_received)
                                ELSE 0 END AS unit_cost,
                           SUM(gd.qty_received * gd.unit_cost) AS grn_total
                    FROM purchase_invoice_dtl d
                    JOIN grn_hdr h ON h.id = d.grn_hdr_id
                    LEFT JOIN grn_dtl gd ON gd.grn_hdr_id = h.id
                    WHERE d.invoice_hdr_id = $1
                    GROUP BY d.id, d.invoice_hdr_id, d.grn_hdr_id, d.matched_amount, d.remark, h.ref_no";

            $result = pg_query_params($sql, [$invoice_id]);
            if (!$result) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal memuat detail invoice.'));

            $data = [];
            while ($row = pg_fetch_assoc($result)) {
                $row['matched_amount'] = floatval($row['matched_amount']);
                $row['qty_received'] = floatval($row['qty_received']);
                $row['unit_cost'] = floatval($row['unit_cost']);
                $row['grn_total'] = floatval($row['grn_total']);
                $data[] = $row;
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
            break;

        case 'create':
        case 'update':
            pg_query("BEGIN");

            $id = isset($_POST['id']) ? intval($_POST['id']) : 0;
            // [FIX] inv_no sekarang No. Ref Dokumen yang di-generate sistem,
            // bukan input user -> field ini sudah disabled di JS, jadi
            // $_POST['inv_no'] diabaikan sepenuhnya di sini.
            $supplier_invoice_no = isset($_POST['supplier_invoice_no']) ? $_POST['supplier_invoice_no'] : '';
            $invoice_date = isset($_POST['invoice_date']) ? $_POST['invoice_date'] : date('Y-m-d');
            $supplier_id = isset($_POST['supplier_id']) ? intval($_POST['supplier_id']) : 0;
            $remark = isset($_POST['remark']) ? $_POST['remark'] : '';

            $invoice_details = isset($_POST['invoice_details']) ? json_decode($_POST['invoice_details'], true) : [];

            if ($task == 'create') {
                // [FIX] Sebelumnya manggil CreateRefNo('PINV', 'purchase_invoice_hdr', 'inv_no')
                // dengan asumsi urutan param (prefix, table, column) -> ternyata SALAH.
                // Dari log server: fungsi ini pakai param pertama sebagai NAMA TABEL
                // ("FROM PINV") dan param kedua sebagai nama kolom di substring() ->
                // query di dalam CreateRefNo() error "relation pinv does not exist",
                // $inv_no jadi kosong/null, lalu INSERT gagal (kolom NOT NULL).
                // Sampai urutan param yang benar dikonfirmasi, generate ref no sendiri
                // di sini saja (self-contained, tidak bergantung fungsi lain).
                $prefix = 'PINV-' . date('ym') . '-';
                $sqlSeq = "SELECT COUNT(*) + 1 AS next_seq FROM purchase_invoice_hdr WHERE inv_no LIKE $1";
                $resSeq = pg_query_params($sqlSeq, [$prefix . '%']);
                if (!$resSeq) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal generate No. Ref Dokumen.'));
                $rowSeq = pg_fetch_assoc($resSeq);
                $inv_no = $prefix . str_pad($rowSeq['next_seq'], 5, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);

                $sqlHdr = "INSERT INTO purchase_invoice_hdr (inv_no, supplier_invoice_no, invoice_date, supplier_id, remark, status)
                           VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'posted') RETURNING id";
                $resHdr = pg_query_params($sqlHdr, [$inv_no, $supplier_invoice_no, $invoice_date, $supplier_id, $remark]);
                if (!$resHdr) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal membuat invoice baru.'));
                $rowHdr = pg_fetch_assoc($resHdr);
                $invoice_id = $rowHdr['id'];
            } else {
                $invoice_id = $id;
                // [FIX] inv_no sengaja tidak disertakan di SET -> No. Ref Dokumen immutable setelah dibuat.
                $sqlHdr = "UPDATE purchase_invoice_hdr SET supplier_invoice_no = $1, invoice_date = $2, supplier_id = $3, remark = $4
                           WHERE id = $5";
                $resHdr = pg_query_params($sqlHdr, [$supplier_invoice_no, $invoice_date, $supplier_id, $remark, $invoice_id]);
                if (!$resHdr) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal mengupdate invoice.'));

                // [FIX] invoice_hdr_id, bukan invoice_id
                pg_query_params("DELETE FROM purchase_invoice_dtl WHERE invoice_hdr_id = $1", [$invoice_id]);
            }

            $total_amount_header = 0;
            foreach ($invoice_details as $item) {
                // [FIX] field dari frontend juga perlu grn_hdr_id, lihat catatan di purchaseinvoice.js
                $grn_hdr_id = intval(isset($item['grn_hdr_id']) ? $item['grn_hdr_id'] : (isset($item['grn_dtl_id']) ? $item['grn_dtl_id'] : 0));
                $matched_amount = floatval($item['matched_amount']);
                $total_amount_header += $matched_amount;

                // [FIX] invoice_hdr_id, grn_hdr_id, bukan invoice_id, grn_dtl_id
                $sqlDtl = "INSERT INTO purchase_invoice_dtl (invoice_hdr_id, grn_hdr_id, matched_amount) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)";
                $resDtl = pg_query_params($sqlDtl, [$invoice_id, $grn_hdr_id, $matched_amount]);
                if (!$resDtl) throw new Exception(pgErrorOrFallback('Gagal menyimpan detail invoice.'));
            }

            pg_query_params("UPDATE purchase_invoice_hdr SET total_amount = $1 WHERE id = $2", [$total_amount_header, $invoice_id]);

            pg_query("COMMIT");
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Data purchase invoice berhasil disimpan.']);
            break;

        case 'delete':
            $id = isset($_POST['id']) ? intval($_POST['id']) : 0;
            pg_query("BEGIN");
            // [FIX] invoice_hdr_id, bukan invoice_id
            pg_query_params("DELETE FROM purchase_invoice_dtl WHERE invoice_hdr_id = $1", [$id]);
            pg_query_params("DELETE FROM purchase_invoice_hdr WHERE id = $1", [$id]);
            pg_query("COMMIT");
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Data berhasil dihapus.']);
            break;

        default:
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
            break;
    }
} catch (Exception $e) {
    if (function_exists('pg_query')) {
        @pg_query("ROLLBACK");
    }
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
}

<?php
session_start();
include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";

$searchField = isset($_POST["query"]) ? $_POST["query"] : NULL;
$offset      = (isset($_POST["start"]) && $_POST["start"] !== null) ? intval($_POST["start"]) : 0;
$limit       = (isset($_POST["limit"]) && $_POST["limit"] !== null) ? intval($_POST["limit"]) : 250;
$task        = isset($_POST['task']) ? $_POST['task'] : 'read';

$db = new crud();

switch ($task) {
    case ("create"):
    case ("add"):
        $tabel = "m_unit";
        $data = array(
            "init"        => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),
            "name"        => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? ''),
            "description" => checksinglequote($_POST["description"] ?? ''),
            "remark"      => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? '')
        );
        $db->insert($tabel, $data);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ('read'):
        $sResult   = searchData($searchField, array("name", "description"), 250);
        $limit     = $sResult["limit"];
        $searchSQL = $sResult["searchSQL"];

        $fields = "id, init, name, description, coalesce(remark,'') remark";
        $table  = "m_unit";
        $where  = "1=1 " . $searchSQL;
        $order  = "name ASC";

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ("read_edit"):
        $id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $fields = "id, init, name, description, coalesce(remark,'') remark";
        $table  = "m_unit";
        $where  = "id = " . $id;

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ('HistoryData'):
    case ('read_history'):
        $id      = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $table   = "view_single_table_log";
        $fields  = "
            tx_id, date, count,
            case amend_name
                when 'init' then '[EDIT] - Init'
                when 'name' then '[EDIT] - Name'
                when 'description' then '[EDIT] - Description'
                when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
                else amend_name
            end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
            before_amendment, after_amendment, user_name, user_time
        ";
        $where   = "tx_id::int = " . $id . " and table_name = 'm_unit'";
        $orderBy = "count desc";

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $orderBy);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    case ('update'):
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id > 0) {
            $tabel = "m_unit";
            $data = array(
                "init"        => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),
                "name"        => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? ''),
                "description" => checksinglequote($_POST["description"] ?? ''),
                "remark"      => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? ''),
            );
            $where = "id = " . $id;
            $db->update($tabel, $data, $where);
            echo $db->getResult();
        } else {
            echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk update."]);
        }
    break;

    case ('delete'):
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id > 0) {
            $tabel = "m_unit";
            $where = "id = " . $id;
            $db->delete($tabel, $where);
            echo $db->getResult();
        } else {
            echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk hapus."]);
        }
    break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "Task tidak dikenali."]);
    break;
}

## File: ./inventory.status.php
