<?php
session_start();
include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";

$offset=($_POST["start"]==null)? 0 : $_POST["start"];

switch ($_POST['task']){
	case ('read'):
    $sResult=searchData($_POST["query"],array("item_name","size","type_group","grade","wood"),250);
    $limit=$sResult["limit"];
    $searchField=$sResult["searchSQL"];
		$db = new crud();
		$field  = "*";
		$table = "
      (
        select type_group,item_id,
          item_name||case when length(btrim(coalesce(size,'')))>0 then ' - '||coalesce(size,'') else '' end as item_name,
          size,um_unit,um_packing,grade,coalesce(grade_id,0) grade_id,wood,coalesce(wood_type_id,0) wood_type_id,
          sum(coalesce(balance_qty,0)) balance_qty,sum(coalesce(balance_packing,0)) balance_packing,
          sum(coalesce(balance_volume,0)) balance_volume
        from
          (
            select i.type_group,b.item_id,i.item_name,i.size,i.um_unit,i.um_packing,
              b.balance_qty,b.balance_packing,
              case when lower(btrim(i.location_name)) like 'unit%5' and lower(btrim(i.type_group)) like '%finish%' then 0 else b.balance_volume end as balance_volume,
              g.name grade,g.id grade_id, w.name wood, w.id wood_type_id
            from wh_item_proyek_balance b
            left join mat_view_items i on coalesce(b.item_id,0)=coalesce(i.item_id,0)
            left join item_grade g on coalesce(b.grade_id,0)=coalesce(g.id,0)
            left join m_wood_type w on coalesce(b.wood_type_id,0)=coalesce(w.id,0)
            where coalesce(b.item_id,0)>0
            order by i.type_group,i.item_name,i.size,g.name
          ) d
        group by type_group,item_id,item_name,size,um_unit,um_packing,grade,coalesce(grade_id,0),wood,coalesce(wood_type_id,0)
     )dt
    ";
    //(balance_qty<>0 or balance_packing<>0 or balance_volume<>0) and 
    $where = "coalesce(item_id,0)>0".$searchField;
    $order = "(case when balance_qty >0 or balance_packing >0 or balance_volume>0 then 1 else 0 end) DESC, 
      type_group,item_name,size,grade,balance_qty DESC, balance_packing DESC, balance_volume DESC";
		$db->select('data',$table,$field,$where,$order,$limit, $offset);
		$result = $db->getResult();
		echo $result;
	break;
  case ('readDetail'):
    $db = new crud();
    $field  = "*";
    $table = "
      (
        select type_group,item_id,
          item_name||case when length(btrim(coalesce(size,'')))>0 then ' - '||coalesce(size,'') else '' end as item_name,
          size,um_unit,um_packing,grade,id_no,warehouse,grade_id,wood,coalesce(wood_type_id,0) wood_type_id,
          sum(coalesce(balance_qty,0)) balance_qty,sum(coalesce(balance_packing,0)) balance_packing,
          sum(coalesce(balance_volume,0)) balance_volume
        from 
          (
            select i.type_group,b.item_id,i.item_name,i.size,i.um_unit,i.um_packing,
              b.id_no,b.balance_qty,b.balance_packing,b.balance_volume,g.name grade,g.id grade_id,wo.name wood,wo.id wood_type_id,
              w.location_name||' ~ '||w.warehouse_name warehouse
            from wh_item_proyek_balance b
            left join mat_view_items i on coalesce(b.item_id,0)=coalesce(i.item_id,0)
            left join item_grade g on coalesce(b.grade_id,0)=coalesce(g.id,0)
            left join m_wood_type wo on coalesce(b.wood_type_id,0)=coalesce(wo.id,0)
            left join view_warehouse_name_init w on w.warehouse_id=b.warehouse_id
            where coalesce(b.item_id,0)=".$_POST['item_id']." 
              and coalesce(b.grade_id,0)=".$_POST['grade_id']." 
              and coalesce(b.wood_type_id,0)=".$_POST['wood_id']."
            order by i.type_group,i.item_name,i.size,g.name,w.location_name||' ~ '||w.warehouse_name
          ) d
        group by type_group,item_id,item_name,size,um_unit,um_packing,grade,grade_id,id_no,warehouse,wood,coalesce(wood_type_id,0)
     )dt
    ";
    $where = "(balance_qty<>0 or balance_packing<>0 or balance_volume<>0) 
      and item_id=".$_POST['item_id']." and coalesce(grade_id,0)=coalesce(".$_POST['grade_id'].",0) 
      and coalesce(wood_type_id,0)=coalesce(".$_POST['wood_id'].",0)";
    $order = "warehouse, coalesce(id_no,'')";
    $db->select('data',$table,$field,$where,$order,$limit, $offset);
    $result = $db->getResult();
    echo $result;
  break;
}
?>


## File: ./purchase.returnCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Purchase Return (Retur Pembelian) - Backend Controller
  Depends on    : wh_receiving_hdr, wh_receiving_dtl, purchase_return_hdr, purchase_return_dtl

  Data retur diambil dari RCV W/H (wh_receiving_hdr & wh_receiving_dtl).
  Perlakuan akuntansi ditentukan OTOMATIS per retur berdasarkan status invoicing GRN:
    - Belum pernah di-invoice -> rule PUR_RETURN_GRN (Debit GRN Clearing / Kredit Persediaan)
    - Sudah pernah di-invoice  -> rule PUR_RETURN_INV (Debit Hutang Usaha / Kredit Persediaan)
  reference_type = 'purchase_return'.
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. READ (Grid Utama)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read':
        $sql = "
            SELECT
                r.id, r.return_no, (r.return_date)::text AS return_date,
                r.wh_receiving_hdr_id, w.ref_no AS wh_rcv_ref_no,
                r.grn_hdr_id, g.ref_no AS grn_ref_no,
                r.warehouse_id, wh.name AS warehouse_name,
                r.supplier_id, s.name AS supplier_name,
                r.posting_basis, r.status, r.reason, r.remark,
                COALESCE((SELECT SUM(d.qty_returned * d.unit_cost) FROM purchase_return_dtl d WHERE d.return_hdr_id = r.id), 0) AS total_amount,
                COALESCE((SELECT SUM(d.qty_returned) FROM purchase_return_dtl d WHERE d.return_hdr_id = r.id), 0) AS total_qty
            FROM purchase_return_hdr r
            LEFT JOIN wh_receiving_hdr w ON w.id = r.wh_receiving_hdr_id
            LEFT JOIN grn_hdr g ON g.id = r.grn_hdr_id
            LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = r.supplier_id
            LEFT JOIN warehouses wh ON wh.id = r.warehouse_id
            ORDER BY r.return_date DESC, r.id DESC
            LIMIT 250
        ";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. READ SINGLE (Header + Detail)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_edit':
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $stmtH = $db->prepare("
            SELECT r.*, (r.return_date)::text AS return_date,
                   w.ref_no AS wh_rcv_ref_no, g.ref_no AS grn_ref_no,
                   s.name AS supplier_name, wh.name AS warehouse_name
            FROM purchase_return_hdr r
            LEFT JOIN wh_receiving_hdr w ON w.id = r.wh_receiving_hdr_id
            LEFT JOIN grn_hdr g ON g.id = r.grn_hdr_id
            LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = r.supplier_id
            LEFT JOIN warehouses wh ON wh.id = r.warehouse_id
            WHERE r.id = :id
        ");
        $stmtH->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $header = $stmtH->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if (!$header) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'data' => new stdClass()]);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtD = $db->prepare("
            SELECT id, wh_receiving_dtl_id, grn_dtl_id, item_id, item_name, qty_returned, unit_cost, remark
            FROM purchase_return_dtl
            WHERE return_hdr_id = :id
            ORDER BY id ASC
        ");
        $stmtD->execute(['id' => $id]);
        $header['items'] = $stmtD->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $header]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. READ COMBO: Penerimaan Gudang (RCV W/H) yang masih bisa di-retur
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_wh_rcv':
    case 'read_grn':
        $sql = "
            SELECT
                o.id, o.ref_no, (o.received_date)::text AS received_date,
                o.grn_hdr_id, o.grn_ref_no, o.warehouse_id, wh.name AS warehouse_name,
                o.supplier_id, s.name AS supplier_name
            FROM view_wh_rcv_hdr_returnable o
            LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = o.supplier_id
            LEFT JOIN warehouses wh ON wh.id = o.warehouse_id
            ORDER BY o.received_date DESC, o.ref_no ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. READ ITEM RCV W/H yang masih bisa di-retur
    //    return_id (opsional, dikirim saat EDIT) -- qty milik return ini
    //    sendiri ditambahkan balik ke qty_returnable.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_wh_rcv_items':
    case 'read_grn_items':
        $whReceivingHdrId = intval($_POST['wh_receiving_hdr_id'] ?? $_POST['grn_hdr_id'] ?? 0);
        $returnId         = intval($_POST['return_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($whReceivingHdrId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT
                s.wh_rcv_dtl_id, s.wh_receiving_hdr_id, s.grn_dtl_id, s.item_id, s.item_name,
                s.qty_received, s.unit_cost, s.grade_id, s.warehouse_id,
                s.qty_returned,
                (s.qty_returnable + COALESCE(prd.qty_returned, 0)) AS qty_returnable,
                COALESCE(prd.qty_returned, 0) AS qty_returned_this_return
            FROM view_wh_rcv_dtl_return_status s
            LEFT JOIN purchase_return_dtl prd ON prd.wh_receiving_dtl_id = s.wh_rcv_dtl_id AND prd.return_hdr_id = :return_id
            WHERE s.wh_receiving_hdr_id = :wh_receiving_hdr_id
              AND (s.qty_returnable > 0 OR prd.id IS NOT NULL)
            ORDER BY s.wh_rcv_dtl_id ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute(['wh_receiving_hdr_id' => $whReceivingHdrId, 'return_id' => $returnId]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    case 'read_return_items':
        $returnId = intval($_POST['return_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($returnId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $stmt = $db->prepare("
            SELECT d.id, d.wh_receiving_dtl_id, d.grn_dtl_id, d.item_id, d.item_name, d.qty_returned, d.unit_cost, (d.qty_returned * d.unit_cost) AS subtotal,
                   COALESCE(wrd.qty_received, 0) AS qty_received
            FROM purchase_return_dtl d
            LEFT JOIN wh_receiving_dtl wrd ON wrd.id = d.wh_receiving_dtl_id
            WHERE d.return_hdr_id = :return_id
            ORDER BY d.id ASC
        ");
        $stmt->execute(['return_id' => $returnId]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Preview perlakuan akuntansi (dipakai UI, read-only)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'preview_posting_basis':
        $grnHdrId         = intval($_POST['grn_hdr_id'] ?? 0);
        $whReceivingHdrId = intval($_POST['wh_receiving_hdr_id'] ?? 0);

        if ($grnHdrId <= 0 && $whReceivingHdrId > 0) {
            $stmtWh = $db->prepare("SELECT grn_hdr_id FROM wh_receiving_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtWh->execute(['id' => $whReceivingHdrId]);
            $grnHdrId = intval($stmtWh->fetchColumn());
        }

        if ($grnHdrId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'posting_basis' => null]);
            exit;
        }
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'posting_basis' => DeterminePostingBasis($db, $grnHdrId)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. CREATE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'create':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $whReceivingHdrId = intval($_POST['wh_receiving_hdr_id'] ?? $_POST['grn_hdr_id'] ?? 0);
        $returnDate       = trim($_POST['return_date'] ?? '');
        $reason           = trim($_POST['reason'] ?? '');
        $remark           = trim($_POST['remark'] ?? '');
        $items            = json_decode($_POST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);

        if ($whReceivingHdrId <= 0 || empty($returnDate) || !is_array($items) || count($items) === 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Lengkapi data retur dan minimal 1 item.']);
            exit;
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $stmtWhInfo = $db->prepare("
                SELECT w.id AS wh_receiving_hdr_id, w.ref_no AS wh_ref_no, w.grn_hdr_id, w.warehouse_id, g.supplier_id
                FROM wh_receiving_hdr w
                JOIN grn_hdr g ON g.id = w.grn_hdr_id
                WHERE w.id = :wh_id AND w.status = 'posted'
            ");
            $stmtWhInfo->execute(['wh_id' => $whReceivingHdrId]);
            $whInfo = $stmtWhInfo->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if (!$whInfo) {
                throw new Exception("Data Penerimaan Gudang (RCV W/H) tidak ditemukan atau belum diposting.");
            }

            $grnHdrId    = intval($whInfo['grn_hdr_id']);
            $warehouseId = intval($whInfo['warehouse_id']);
            $supplierId  = intval($whInfo['supplier_id']);
            $whRefNo     = $whInfo['wh_ref_no'];

            $returnNo = CreateRefNo('purchase_return_hdr', 'return_no', 'PRET');

            $postingBasis = DeterminePostingBasis($db, $grnHdrId);

            DbOrFail($db, $db->insert('purchase_return_hdr', [
                'return_no'           => $returnNo,
                'return_date'         => $returnDate,
                'wh_receiving_hdr_id' => $whReceivingHdrId,
                'warehouse_id'        => $warehouseId,
                'supplier_id'         => $supplierId,
                'grn_hdr_id'          => $grnHdrId,
                'reason'              => $reason,
                'posting_basis'       => $postingBasis,
                'status'              => 'posted',
                'remark'              => $remark,
                'created_by'          => $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null
            ]), 'insert purchase_return_hdr');

            $returnId = $db->lastInsertId();
            if (!$returnId) {
                $stmtR = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM purchase_return_hdr WHERE return_no = :no");
                $stmtR->execute(['no' => $returnNo]);
                $returnId = $stmtR->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['id'] ?? 0;
            }

            $totalAmount = InsertReturnItems($db, $returnId, $items);

            $ruleCode = ($postingBasis === 'accounts_payable') ? 'PUR_RETURN_INV' : 'PUR_RETURN_GRN';
            $result = AutoPostJournal($db, [
                'rule_code'      => $ruleCode,
                'reference_type' => 'purchase_return',
                'reference_id'   => $returnId,
                'journal_date'   => $returnDate,
                'amount'         => $totalAmount,
                'description'    => 'Retur Pembelian ' . $returnNo . ' (RCV W/H: ' . $whRefNo . ')',
            ]);
            if (!$result['success']) {
                throw new Exception($result['message']);
            }

            PostReturnStockMovements($db, $returnId, $returnNo, $returnDate, $whReceivingHdrId, $items);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Retur Pembelian {$returnNo}, Jurnal, & Stok Keluar berhasil disimpan (" . ($postingBasis === 'accounts_payable' ? 'mengurangi Hutang Usaha' : 'mengurangi GRN Clearing') . ")."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                $db->rollBack();
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal simpan data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. UPDATE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'update':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 3) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $id               = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $whReceivingHdrId = intval($_POST['wh_receiving_hdr_id'] ?? $_POST['grn_hdr_id'] ?? 0);
        $returnDate       = trim($_POST['return_date'] ?? '');
        $reason           = trim($_POST['reason'] ?? '');
        $remark           = trim($_POST['remark'] ?? '');
        $items            = json_decode($_POST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);

        if ($id <= 0 || $whReceivingHdrId <= 0 || empty($returnDate) || !is_array($items) || count($items) === 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Data edit retur tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $stmtOld = $db->prepare("SELECT return_no FROM purchase_return_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtOld->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $returnNo = $stmtOld->fetchColumn();
            if (!$returnNo) {
                throw new Exception("Data retur tidak ditemukan.");
            }

            $stmtWhInfo = $db->prepare("
                SELECT w.id AS wh_receiving_hdr_id, w.ref_no AS wh_ref_no, w.grn_hdr_id, w.warehouse_id, g.supplier_id
                FROM wh_receiving_hdr w
                JOIN grn_hdr g ON g.id = w.grn_hdr_id
                WHERE w.id = :wh_id AND w.status = 'posted'
            ");
            $stmtWhInfo->execute(['wh_id' => $whReceivingHdrId]);
            $whInfo = $stmtWhInfo->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if (!$whInfo) {
                throw new Exception("Data Penerimaan Gudang (RCV W/H) tidak ditemukan atau belum diposting.");
            }

            $grnHdrId    = intval($whInfo['grn_hdr_id']);
            $warehouseId = intval($whInfo['warehouse_id']);
            $supplierId  = intval($whInfo['supplier_id']);
            $whRefNo     = $whInfo['wh_ref_no'];

            ReverseJournalByReference($db, 'purchase_return', $id);
            ReverseStockMovementByReference($db, 'purchase_return', $id);

            $stmtDel = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM purchase_return_dtl WHERE return_hdr_id = :id");
            $stmtDel->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $postingBasis = DeterminePostingBasis($db, $grnHdrId);

            DbOrFail($db, $db->update('purchase_return_hdr', [
                'return_date'         => $returnDate,
                'wh_receiving_hdr_id' => $whReceivingHdrId,
                'warehouse_id'        => $warehouseId,
                'supplier_id'         => $supplierId,
                'grn_hdr_id'          => $grnHdrId,
                'reason'              => $reason,
                'posting_basis'       => $postingBasis,
                'remark'              => $remark
            ], "id = " . intval($id)), 'update purchase_return_hdr');

            $totalAmount = InsertReturnItems($db, $id, $items);

            $ruleCode = ($postingBasis === 'accounts_payable') ? 'PUR_RETURN_INV' : 'PUR_RETURN_GRN';
            $result = AutoPostJournal($db, [
                'rule_code'      => $ruleCode,
                'reference_type' => 'purchase_return',
                'reference_id'   => $id,
                'journal_date'   => $returnDate,
                'amount'         => $totalAmount,
                'description'    => 'Retur Pembelian ' . $returnNo . ' (RCV W/H: ' . $whRefNo . ')',
            ]);
            if (!$result['success']) {
                throw new Exception($result['message']);
            }

            PostReturnStockMovements($db, $id, $returnNo, $returnDate, $whReceivingHdrId, $items);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Retur Pembelian {$returnNo} berhasil diperbarui."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                $db->rollBack();
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal update data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. DELETE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'delete':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID data tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $stmtOld = $db->prepare("SELECT return_no FROM purchase_return_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtOld->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $returnNo = $stmtOld->fetchColumn();
            if (!$returnNo) {
                throw new Exception("Data retur tidak ditemukan.");
            }

            ReverseJournalByReference($db, 'purchase_return', $id);
            ReverseStockMovementByReference($db, 'purchase_return', $id);

            $stmtDelD = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM purchase_return_dtl WHERE return_hdr_id = :id");
            $stmtDelD->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $stmtDelH = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM purchase_return_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtDelH->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Retur Pembelian {$returnNo} berhasil dihapus & jurnal/stok dibatalkan."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                $db->rollBack();
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal menghapus data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
}

// ==================================================================
// HELPER: DeterminePostingBasis
// Cek apakah GRN ini SUDAH PERNAH di-invoice (via
// view_grn_invoice_status.invoiced_amount > 0). Kalau ya -> retur
// mengurangi Hutang Usaha (rule PUR_RETURN_INV). Kalau belum -> retur
// mengurangi GRN Clearing (rule PUR_RETURN_GRN).
// ==================================================================
function DeterminePostingBasis($db, $grnHdrId) {
    if ($grnHdrId <= 0) return 'grn_clearing';
    $stmt = $db->prepare("SELECT invoiced_amount FROM view_grn_invoice_status WHERE grn_hdr_id = :id");
    $stmt->execute(['id' => $grnHdrId]);
    $invoicedAmount = floatval($stmt->fetchColumn());
    return $invoicedAmount > 0.01 ? 'accounts_payable' : 'grn_clearing';
}

// ==================================================================
// HELPER: PostReturnStockMovements
// Catat stock_movements (OUT) per item retur -- barang keluar dari
// gudang penerimaan (RCV W/H) terkait.
// ==================================================================
function PostReturnStockMovements($db, $returnId, $returnNo, $returnDate, $whReceivingHdrId, array $items) {
    $stmtWh = $db->prepare("SELECT warehouse_id FROM wh_receiving_hdr WHERE id = :id");
    $stmtWh->execute(['id' => $whReceivingHdrId]);
    $warehouseId = intval($stmtWh->fetchColumn());

    if ($warehouseId <= 0) {
        throw new Exception("Gudang penerimaan (RCV W/H) tidak ditemukan -- tidak bisa memproses stok keluar retur.");
    }

    foreach ($items as $it) {
        $itemId   = intval($it['item_id'] ?? 0);
        $gradeId  = !empty($it['grade_id']) ? intval($it['grade_id']) : null;
        $qty      = round(floatval($it['qty_returned'] ?? 0), 4);
        $cost     = round(floatval($it['unit_cost'] ?? 0), 4);
        $itemName = trim($it['item_name'] ?? '');

        if ($itemId <= 0 || $qty <= 0) {
            continue;
        }

        $available = GetStockBalance($db, $itemId, $gradeId, $warehouseId);
        if ($qty > $available + 0.0001) {
            throw new Exception(
                "Stok tidak cukup untuk retur item " . ($itemName ? "'{$itemName}'" : "ID {$itemId}") . " di gudang penerimaan. " .
                "Saldo fisik tersedia: " . number_format($available, 4) . ", diminta retur: " . number_format($qty, 4) . "."
            );
        }

        PostStockMovement($db, [
            'movement_date'  => $returnDate,
            'item_id'        => $itemId,
            'grade_id'       => $gradeId,
            'warehouse_id'   => $warehouseId,
            'movement_type'  => 'OUT',
            'qty'            => $qty,
            'unit_cost'      => $cost,
            'reference_type' => 'purchase_return',
            'reference_id'   => $returnId,
            'reference_no'   => $returnNo,
            'remark'         => "Retur Pembelian {$returnNo}"
        ]);
    }
}

// ==================================================================
// HELPER: InsertReturnItems
// Validasi tiap item terhadap sisa qty_returnable di
// view_wh_rcv_dtl_return_status, lalu insert.
// ==================================================================
function InsertReturnItems($db, $returnId, array $items) {
    $totalAmount = 0.0;

    $stmtCheck = $db->prepare("
        SELECT qty_received, qty_returnable, item_name, unit_cost
        FROM view_wh_rcv_dtl_return_status
        WHERE wh_rcv_dtl_id = :wh_rcv_dtl_id
    ");

    foreach ($items as $it) {
        $whRcvDtlId = intval($it['wh_rcv_dtl_id'] ?? 0);
        $grnDtlId   = intval($it['grn_dtl_id'] ?? 0);
        $itemId     = intval($it['item_id'] ?? 0);
        $itemName   = trim($it['item_name'] ?? '');
        $qty        = round(floatval($it['qty_returned'] ?? 0), 4);
        $cost       = round(floatval($it['unit_cost'] ?? 0), 4);
        $remark     = trim($it['remark'] ?? '');

        if ($whRcvDtlId <= 0 || $itemId <= 0 || $qty <= 0) {
            continue;
        }

        $stmtCheck->execute(['wh_rcv_dtl_id' => $whRcvDtlId]);
        $status = $stmtCheck->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if (!$status) {
            throw new Exception("Baris RCV W/H (wh_rcv_dtl_id={$whRcvDtlId}) tidak ditemukan.");
        }

        if ($qty > floatval($status['qty_returnable']) + 0.0001) {
            throw new Exception(
                "Qty retur ({$qty}) untuk item " . ($itemName ? "'{$itemName}'" : "ID {$itemId}") . " melebihi sisa yang bisa di-retur. " .
                "Qty diterima W/H: {$status['qty_received']}, sisa bisa retur: {$status['qty_returnable']}."
            );
        }

        DbOrFail($db, $db->insert('purchase_return_dtl', [
            'return_hdr_id'       => $returnId,
            'wh_receiving_dtl_id' => $whRcvDtlId,
            'grn_dtl_id'          => $grnDtlId > 0 ? $grnDtlId : null,
            'item_id'             => $itemId,
            'item_name'           => $itemName !== '' ? $itemName : ($status['item_name'] ?? null),
            'qty_returned'        => $qty,
            'unit_cost'           => $cost > 0 ? $cost : floatval($status['unit_cost']),
            'remark'              => $remark
        ]), 'insert purchase_return_dtl');

        $totalAmount += round($qty * ($cost > 0 ? $cost : floatval($status['unit_cost'])), 2);
    }

    if ($totalAmount <= 0) {
        throw new Exception('Tidak ada item valid untuk diretur (total nilai = 0).');
    }

    return round($totalAmount, 2);
}

## File: ./stock.transferCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Stock Transfer (Pindah Stok Antar Gudang/Bin) - Backend
  Description   : Langsung posting saat disimpan (tanpa approval). Setiap
                   item menghasilkan SEPASANG stock_movements: OUT dari
                   lokasi asal, IN ke lokasi tujuan, reference_type
                   'stock_transfer' sama untuk keduanya (reference_id =
                   stock_transfer_hdr.id) supaya gampang ditelusuri di
                   Kartu Stok. TIDAK ada jurnal (lihat catatan di schema).
  Depends on    : stock_transfer_schema.sql, bin_location_schema.sql
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. READ (Grid Utama)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read':
        $sql = "
            SELECT
                h.id, h.transfer_no, h.transfer_date,
                h.warehouse_from_id, wf.name AS warehouse_from_name,
                h.bin_location_from_id, bf.code AS bin_from_code,
                h.warehouse_to_id, wt.name AS warehouse_to_name,
                h.bin_location_to_id, bt.code AS bin_to_code,
                h.status, h.remark,
                COALESCE((SELECT SUM(d.qty) FROM stock_transfer_dtl d WHERE d.transfer_hdr_id = h.id), 0) AS total_qty
            FROM stock_transfer_hdr h
            LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wf ON wf.id = h.warehouse_from_id
            LEFT JOIN m_warehouse wt ON wt.id = h.warehouse_to_id
            LEFT JOIN bin_locations bf ON bf.id = h.bin_location_from_id
            LEFT JOIN bin_locations bt ON bt.id = h.bin_location_to_id
            ORDER BY h.transfer_date DESC, h.id DESC
            LIMIT 250
        ";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. READ: item detail 1 transfer (buat panel detail read-only,
    //    pola sama kayak GRN/Sales Delivery)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_detail_items':
        $transferId = intval($_POST['transfer_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($transferId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }
        $stmt = $db->prepare("
            SELECT d.item_name, d.qty, ig.name AS grade_name
            FROM stock_transfer_dtl d
            LEFT JOIN item_grade ig ON ig.id = d.grade_id
            WHERE d.transfer_hdr_id = :id
            ORDER BY d.id ASC
        ");
        $stmt->execute(['id' => $transferId]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. READ COMBO: Gudang
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_warehouse':
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM m_warehouse WHERE active = true ORDER BY name ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. READ COMBO: Bin Location per Gudang
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_bin_by_warehouse':
        $warehouseId = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($warehouseId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }
        $stmt = $db->prepare("
            SELECT id, code, name
            FROM bin_locations
            WHERE warehouse_id = :warehouse_id AND active = true
            ORDER BY code ASC
        ");
        $stmt->execute(['warehouse_id' => $warehouseId]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. READ COMBO: Item (search remote)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_item_combo':
        $query = trim($_POST['query'] ?? '');
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM items WHERE is_active = true";
        $params = [];
        if ($query !== '') {
            $sql .= " AND name ILIKE :query";
            $params['query'] = '%' . $query . '%';
        }
        $sql .= " ORDER BY name ASC LIMIT 50";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute($params);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. READ COMBO: Grade
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_grade':
        $sql = "SELECT id, name FROM item_grade ORDER BY name ASC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. CEK SALDO: item+grade di gudang+bin asal (dipanggil tiap user
    //    pilih item/grade di grid, sebelum input qty)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'check_stock':
        $itemId         = intval($_POST['item_id'] ?? 0);
        $gradeId        = !empty($_POST['grade_id']) ? intval($_POST['grade_id']) : null;
        $warehouseId    = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        $binLocationId  = !empty($_POST['bin_location_id']) ? intval($_POST['bin_location_id']) : null;

        if ($itemId <= 0 || $warehouseId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'balance_qty' => 0]);
            exit;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN movement_type = 'IN' THEN qty ELSE -qty END), 0) AS balance_qty
            FROM stock_movements
            WHERE item_id = :item_id
              AND warehouse_id = :warehouse_id
              AND grade_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM :grade_id
              AND bin_location_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM :bin_location_id
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute([
            'item_id' => $itemId, 'warehouse_id' => $warehouseId,
            'grade_id' => $gradeId, 'bin_location_id' => $binLocationId
        ]);
        $balance = floatval($stmt->fetchColumn());
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'balance_qty' => $balance]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7b. READ SEMUA STOK DI LOKASI ASAL (dipanggil sekali begitu
    //     Gudang Asal / Bin Asal dipilih -> grid item auto-terisi
    //     semua item yang punya saldo > 0 di lokasi itu, qty transfer
    //     default = saldo tersedia (full transfer). User tinggal
    //     kosongkan/nolkan qty item yang tidak ikut ditransfer).
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_stock_by_location':
        $warehouseId   = intval($_POST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
        $binLocationId = !empty($_POST['bin_location_id']) ? intval($_POST['bin_location_id']) : null;

        if ($warehouseId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
            exit;
        }

        $sql = "
            SELECT
                sm.item_id,
                i.name AS item_name,
                sm.grade_id,
                ig.name AS grade_name,
                SUM(CASE WHEN sm.movement_type = 'IN' THEN sm.qty ELSE -sm.qty END) AS balance_qty
            FROM stock_movements sm
            JOIN items i ON i.id = sm.item_id
            LEFT JOIN item_grade ig ON ig.id = sm.grade_id
            WHERE sm.warehouse_id = :warehouse_id
              AND sm.bin_location_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM :bin_location_id
            GROUP BY sm.item_id, i.name, sm.grade_id, ig.name
            HAVING SUM(CASE WHEN sm.movement_type = 'IN' THEN sm.qty ELSE -sm.qty END) > 0.0001
            ORDER BY i.name ASC
        ";
        $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->execute([
            'warehouse_id'    => $warehouseId,
            'bin_location_id' => $binLocationId
        ]);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)]);
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 8. CREATE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'create':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 2) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }

        $warehouseFromId = intval($_POST['warehouse_from_id'] ?? 0);
        $binFromId        = !empty($_POST['bin_location_from_id']) ? intval($_POST['bin_location_from_id']) : null;
        $warehouseToId    = intval($_POST['warehouse_to_id'] ?? 0);
        $binToId          = !empty($_POST['bin_location_to_id']) ? intval($_POST['bin_location_to_id']) : null;
        $transferDate     = trim($_POST['transfer_date'] ?? '');
        $remark           = trim($_POST['remark'] ?? '');
        $items            = json_decode($_POST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);

        if ($warehouseFromId <= 0 || $warehouseToId <= 0 || empty($transferDate) || !is_array($items) || count($items) === 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Lengkapi gudang asal/tujuan, tanggal, dan minimal 1 item.']);
            exit;
        }
        if ($warehouseFromId === $warehouseToId && $binFromId === $binToId) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Lokasi asal dan tujuan tidak boleh sama persis.']);
            exit;
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $transferNo = CreateRefNo('stock_transfer_hdr', 'transfer_no', 'TRF');

            DbOrFail($db, $db->insert('stock_transfer_hdr', [
                'transfer_no'          => $transferNo,
                'transfer_date'        => $transferDate,
                'warehouse_from_id'    => $warehouseFromId,
                'bin_location_from_id' => $binFromId,
                'warehouse_to_id'      => $warehouseToId,
                'bin_location_to_id'   => $binToId,
                'status'               => 'posted',
                'remark'               => $remark,
                'created_by'           => $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null
            ]), 'insert stock_transfer_hdr');

            $transferId = $db->lastInsertId();
            if (!$transferId) {
                $stmtId = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM stock_transfer_hdr WHERE transfer_no = :no");
                $stmtId->execute(['no' => $transferNo]);
                $transferId = $stmtId->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['id'] ?? 0;
            }

            InsertTransferItemsAndMove(
                $db, $transferId, $transferNo, $transferDate,
                $warehouseFromId, $binFromId, $warehouseToId, $binToId, $items
            );

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Stock Transfer {$transferNo} berhasil disimpan & stok berpindah."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                $db->rollBack();
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal simpan data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 9. DELETE (reverse pergerakan stok)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'delete':
        $access = intval($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
        if ($access < 4) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak.']);
            exit;
        }
        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID tidak valid.']);
            exit;
        }

        try {
            $db->beginTransaction();

            $stmtOld = $db->prepare("SELECT transfer_no FROM stock_transfer_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtOld->execute(['id' => $id]);
            $transferNo = $stmtOld->fetchColumn();
            if (!$transferNo) {
                throw new Exception("Data transfer tidak ditemukan.");
            }

            // Tidak ada jurnal untuk direverse (lihat catatan di schema),
            // cukup reverse stock_movements.
            ReverseStockMovementByReference($db, 'stock_transfer', $id);

            $stmtDelD = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM stock_transfer_dtl WHERE transfer_hdr_id = :id");
            $stmtDelD->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $stmtDelH = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM stock_transfer_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtDelH->execute(['id' => $id]);

            $db->commit();
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "Stock Transfer {$transferNo} berhasil dihapus & stok dikembalikan."]);

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            if ($db->inTransaction()) {
                $db->rollBack();
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal menghapus data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
}

// ==================================================================
// HELPER: InsertTransferItemsAndMove
// Validasi saldo tersedia di lokasi asal (real-time, dalam transaksi
// yang sama), insert stock_transfer_dtl, lalu posting SEPASANG
// stock_movements per item (OUT asal, IN tujuan).
// ==================================================================
function InsertTransferItemsAndMove($db, $transferId, $transferNo, $transferDate,
                                      $warehouseFromId, $binFromId, $warehouseToId, $binToId, array $items) {

    $stmtBalance = $db->prepare("
        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN movement_type = 'IN' THEN qty ELSE -qty END), 0) AS balance_qty
        FROM stock_movements
        WHERE item_id = :item_id
          AND warehouse_id = :warehouse_id
          AND grade_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM :grade_id
          AND bin_location_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM :bin_location_id
    ");

    foreach ($items as $it) {
        $itemId   = intval($it['item_id'] ?? 0);
        $itemName = trim($it['item_name'] ?? '');
        $gradeId  = !empty($it['grade_id']) ? intval($it['grade_id']) : null;
        $qty      = round(floatval($it['qty'] ?? 0), 4);
        $remark   = trim($it['remark'] ?? '');

        if ($itemId <= 0 || $qty <= 0) {
            continue;
        }

        // Validasi saldo asal REAL-TIME (bukan percaya angka yang dikirim
        // client, karena bisa saja sudah berubah sejak user buka form).
        $stmtBalance->execute([
            'item_id' => $itemId, 'warehouse_id' => $warehouseFromId,
            'grade_id' => $gradeId, 'bin_location_id' => $binFromId
        ]);
        $available = floatval($stmtBalance->fetchColumn());

        if ($qty > $available + 0.0001) {
            throw new Exception("Item '{$itemName}': qty transfer ({$qty}) melebihi stok tersedia di lokasi asal ({$available}).");
        }

        DbOrFail($db, $db->insert('stock_transfer_dtl', [
            'transfer_hdr_id' => $transferId,
            'item_id'         => $itemId,
            'item_name'       => $itemName,
            'grade_id'        => $gradeId,
            'qty'             => $qty,
            'remark'          => $remark
        ]), 'insert stock_transfer_dtl');

        // OUT dari lokasi asal
        PostStockMovement($db, [
            'movement_date'   => $transferDate,
            'item_id'         => $itemId,
            'grade_id'        => $gradeId,
            'warehouse_id'    => $warehouseFromId,
            'bin_location_id' => $binFromId,
            'movement_type'   => 'OUT',
            'qty'             => $qty,
            'reference_type'  => 'stock_transfer',
            'reference_id'    => $transferId,
            'reference_no'    => $transferNo,
        ]);

        // IN ke lokasi tujuan
        PostStockMovement($db, [
            'movement_date'   => $transferDate,
            'item_id'         => $itemId,
            'grade_id'        => $gradeId,
            'warehouse_id'    => $warehouseToId,
            'bin_location_id' => $binToId,
            'movement_type'   => 'IN',
            'qty'             => $qty,
            'reference_type'  => 'stock_transfer',
            'reference_id'    => $transferId,
            'reference_no'    => $transferNo,
        ]);
    }
}

switch ($task) {

    // ==========================================
    // 1. READ (Ambil Data untuk Grid / List)
    // ==========================================
    case ('read'):
        $sResult   = searchData($queryParam, array("seq", "name", "remark"), 250);
        $limit     = $sResult["limit"];
        $searchSQL = $sResult["searchSQL"];

        $fields = "id, seq, name as grp_name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, coalesce(active, true) as active";
        $where  = "1=1 " . $searchSQL;
        $order  = "seq ASC, name ASC";

        // Pemanggilan yang BENAR: (tabel, fields, where, order, limit, offset)
        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 2. READ EDIT (Ambil Data Berdasarkan ID)
    // ==========================================

        $id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $fields = "id, seq, name as grp_name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, coalesce(active, true) as active";
        $where  = "id = " . $id;

        $db->select($table, $fields, $where);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 3. HISTORY DATA (Log Perubahan)
    // ==========================================
    case ('HistoryData'):
        $id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $fields = "
            tx_id, date, count,
            case amend_name
                when 'seq' then '[EDIT] - Sequence'
                when 'name' then '[EDIT] - Name'
                when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
                else amend_name
            end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
            before_amendment,
            after_amendment,
            user_name, user_time
        ";
        $where   = "tx_id::int = " . $id . " and table_name = '" . $table . "'";
        $orderBy = "count desc";

        $db->select("view_single_table_log", $fields, $where, $orderBy);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 4. CREATE / ADD
    // ==========================================
    case ('create'):
    case ('add'):
        $seq    = $_POST['seq'] ?? '';
        $name   = $_POST['grp_name'] ?? $_POST['name'] ?? '';
        $remark = $_POST['remark'] ?? '';
        // PDO pgsql tidak bisa bind boolean PHP native -- wajib string 't'/'f'.
        $active = ($_POST['active'] ?? 'true') === 'true' ? 't' : 'f';

        if (!empty($name)) {
            $data = array(
                "seq"    => checksinglequote($seq),
                "name"   => checksinglequote($name),
                "remark" => checksinglequote($remark),
                "active" => $active
            );

            if ($db->insert($table, $data)) {
                echo $db->getResult();
            } else {
                echo $db->getResult();
            }

            echo json_encode(array(
                "success" => "false",
                "status"  => "Error",
                "message" => "Nama Group wajib diisi."
            ));
        }
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 5. UPDATE / EDIT
    // ==========================================
    case ('update'):
    case ('edit'):
        $formId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $seq    = $_POST['seq'] ?? '';
        $name   = $_POST['grp_name'] ?? $_POST['name'] ?? '';
        $remark = $_POST['remark'] ?? '';
        // PDO pgsql tidak bisa bind boolean PHP native -- wajib string 't'/'f'.
        $active = ($_POST['active'] ?? 'true') === 'true' ? 't' : 'f';

        if ($formId > 0) {
            $data = array(
                "seq"    => checksinglequote($seq),
                "name"   => checksinglequote($name),
                "remark" => checksinglequote($remark),
                "active" => $active
            );
            $where = "id = " . $formId;

            if ($db->update($table, $data, $where)) {
                echo $db->getResult();
            } else {
                echo $db->getResult();
            }
        } else {
            echo json_encode(array(
                "success" => "false",
                "status"  => "Error",
                "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk proses update."
            ));
        }
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 6. DELETE
    // ==========================================
    case ('delete'):
        $deleteId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

        if ($deleteId > 0) {
            $where  = "id = " . $deleteId;
            $queryC = deleteQ($table, $where) . ";";

            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
            echo $db->getResult();
        } else {
            echo json_encode(array(
                "success" => "false",
                "status"  => "Error",
                "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk proses penghapusan."
            ));
        }
    break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(array(
            "success" => "false",
            "status"  => "Error",
            "message" => "Task atau perintah tidak dikenali."
        ));
    break;
}

			s.id,s.login_name,s.employee_id,s.active,s.password,

## File: ./whrcvCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/**
 * whrcvCRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Backend Controller untuk WH Receiving (Penerimaan Fisik Gudang)
 * Mengontrol alur penerimaan barang berdasarkan GRN, pencatatan
 * ke stock_movements, serta integrasi Auto Journal.
 *
 * PERBAIKAN (dari versi sebelumnya):
 * - [FIX UTAMA] Versi lama memakai DUA KONEKSI/TRANSAKSI SEKALIGUS: pg_query
 *   native (BEGIN/COMMIT) untuk header+detail, dan $db PDO (beginTransaction/
 *   commit) untuk PostStockMovement()/AutoPostJournal(). Kalau salah satu sisi
 *   gagal commit SETELAH sisi lain sudah berhasil, hasilnya header WH Rcv
 *   tersimpan "posted" tapi detail/stok/jurnalnya hilang -- persis gejala
 *   "total nilai 0.00, tidak ada item detail" yang muncul di produksi.
 *   Sekarang SEMUA query (termasuk yang tadinya pg_query_params) memakai satu
 *   koneksi PDO ($db) yang sama, satu transaksi tunggal.
 * - m_bin_location -> bin_locations (perbaikan sebelumnya, dipertahankan).
 * - Validasi "total qty diterima harus lebih dari 0" sekarang dicek SEBELUM
 *   ada INSERT apa pun (dari total qty, bukan dari qty*unit_cost) -- supaya
 *   item dengan unit_cost=0 (data GRN yang belum diisi harga) tetap valid
 *   tersimpan, tidak keliru dianggap "tidak ada yang diterima".
 *
 * [FIX] Versi sebelumnya memuat BLOK TEMPELAN dari Master Proyek CRD (switch ($action) ... case 'read','save',
 *   'delete' milik m_proyek) tepat sebelum kode ini. Untuk request ber-'task' blok itu mencetak
 *   {"success":false,"message":"Aksi tidak valid!"} LALU kode WH Rcv mencetak respons asli -> dua JSON
 *   tersambung, ExtJS gagal decode. Blok tempelan sudah dibuang.
 *
 * MULTI CURRENCY (sql/2026-10-01.grn_currency.sql):
 *  - wh_receiving_dtl.unit_cost = harga per unit dalam MATA UANG DASAR, diambil dari grn_dtl.unit_cost_base
 *    (server yang menentukan; unit_cost kiriman klien diabaikan). Inilah dasar layer FIFO, stock_movements,
 *    dan jurnal GRN_RCV.
 *  - unit_cost_txn / currency_id / exch_rate menyimpan harga & kurs asal sebagai jejak audit.
 * ============================================================
 */

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }
header('Content-Type: application/json');

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';
$db   = new crud(); // PDO Connection (satu-satunya koneksi yang dipakai file ini)

function PrepOrFail($stmt, $context = '') {
    if ($stmt === false) {
        throw new Exception(($context ? "[{$context}] " : '') . 'Query gagal dijalankan.');
    }
    return $stmt;
}

/** SELECT dan fetchAll dalam satu langkah. */
function fetchAll($db, $sql, array $params = [], $ctx = '') {
    $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
    PrepOrFail($stmt, $ctx);
    $stmt->execute($params);
    return $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
}

/** SELECT dan fetch satu baris. */
function fetchOne($db, $sql, array $params = [], $ctx = '') {
    $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
    PrepOrFail($stmt, $ctx);
    $stmt->execute($params);
    $row = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
    return $row === false ? null : $row;
}

/** INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE biasa (tanpa perlu baris kembalian). */
function execSql($db, $sql, array $params = [], $ctx = '') {
    $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
    PrepOrFail($stmt, $ctx);
    $stmt->execute($params);
    return $stmt;
}

try {
    switch ($task) {

        case 'list_grn_receivable':
            $sql = "SELECT g.id, g.ref_no, g.grn_date, g.status,
                           s.name AS supplier_name,
                           UPPER(TRIM(cur.init)) AS currency_code, COALESCE(NULLIF(g.exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate
                    FROM grn_hdr g
                    LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = g.supplier_id
                    LEFT JOIN m_currency cur ON cur.id = g.currency_id
                    JOIN grn_dtl gd ON gd.grn_hdr_id = g.id
                    LEFT JOIN (
                        SELECT d.grn_dtl_id, SUM(d.qty_received) AS qty_wh_sum
                        FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
                        JOIN wh_receiving_hdr h ON h.id = d.wh_receiving_hdr_id AND h.status = 'posted'
                        GROUP BY d.grn_dtl_id
                    ) wd ON wd.grn_dtl_id = gd.id
                    WHERE g.status IN ('open','partial')
                    GROUP BY g.id, g.ref_no, g.grn_date, g.status, s.name, cur.init, g.exch_rate
                    HAVING SUM(gd.qty_received) - COALESCE(SUM(wd.qty_wh_sum), 0) > 0.0001
                    ORDER BY g.grn_date DESC";
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => fetchAll($db, $sql, [], 'list_grn_receivable')]);
            break;

        case 'load_grn_dtl':
            $grn_hdr_id = (int)($_REQUEST['grn_hdr_id'] ?? 0);
            $sql = "SELECT gd.id AS grn_dtl_id, gd.item_id, gd.item_name,
                           gd.qty_received AS qty_grn, gd.grade_id, gd.unit_cost,
                           COALESCE(NULLIF(gd.unit_cost_base, 0), gd.unit_cost * COALESCE(NULLIF(g.exch_rate,0), 1)) AS unit_cost_base,
                           COALESCE(wd.qty_wh_sum, 0) AS qty_wh_received,
                           (gd.qty_received - COALESCE(wd.qty_wh_sum, 0)) AS qty_remaining
                    FROM grn_dtl gd
                    LEFT JOIN (
                        SELECT d.grn_dtl_id, SUM(d.qty_received) AS qty_wh_sum
                        FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
                        JOIN wh_receiving_hdr h ON h.id = d.wh_receiving_hdr_id AND h.status = 'posted'
                        GROUP BY d.grn_dtl_id
                    ) wd ON wd.grn_dtl_id = gd.id
                    JOIN grn_hdr g ON g.id = gd.grn_hdr_id
                    WHERE gd.grn_hdr_id = :grn_hdr_id
                      AND (gd.qty_received - COALESCE(wd.qty_wh_sum, 0)) > 0.0001
                    ORDER BY gd.id";
            $data = fetchAll($db, $sql, ['grn_hdr_id' => $grn_hdr_id], 'load_grn_dtl');
            $curInfo = fetchOne($db,
                "SELECT UPPER(TRIM(c.init)) AS code, COALESCE(NULLIF(g.exch_rate,0), 1) AS exch_rate
                   FROM grn_hdr g LEFT JOIN m_currency c ON c.id = g.currency_id WHERE g.id = :id",
                ['id' => $grn_hdr_id], 'load_grn_dtl currency');
            $baseCode = 'IDR';
            if (fetchOne($db, "SELECT to_regclass('public.finance_settings') AS t", [], 'cek finance_settings')['t']) {
                $b = fetchOne($db, "SELECT UPPER(TRIM(c.init)) AS code FROM finance_settings f JOIN m_currency c ON c.id = f.base_currency_id WHERE f.id = 1", [], 'base currency');
                if ($b && $b['code']) $baseCode = $b['code'];
            }
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data,
                              'currency' => ['code' => ($curInfo['code'] ?? null) ?: $baseCode, 'base_code' => $baseCode,
                                             'exch_rate' => (float)($curInfo['exch_rate'] ?? 1)]]);
            break;

        case 'list_bin_locations':
            $warehouse_id = (int)($_REQUEST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
            $sql = "SELECT id, code, name, zone, rack, level
                    FROM bin_locations
                    WHERE warehouse_id = :warehouse_id AND active IS TRUE
                    ORDER BY code";
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => fetchAll($db, $sql, ['warehouse_id' => $warehouse_id], 'list_bin_locations')]);
            break;

        case 'list_warehouses':
            $sql = "SELECT id, code AS init, name
                    FROM warehouses
                    WHERE active IS TRUE AND id IN (SELECT warehouse_id FROM bin_locations WHERE active IS TRUE)
                    ORDER BY name";
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => fetchAll($db, $sql, [], 'list_warehouses')]);
            break;

        case 'list':
            $sql = "SELECT w.id, w.ref_no, w.received_date, w.status,
                           wh.name AS warehouse_name, g.ref_no AS grn_ref_no,
                           s.name AS supplier_name,
                           (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(qty_received * unit_cost), 0)
                            FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
                            WHERE d.wh_receiving_hdr_id = w.id) AS total_amount,
                           (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(qty_received * COALESCE(unit_cost_txn, unit_cost)), 0)
                            FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
                            WHERE d.wh_receiving_hdr_id = w.id) AS total_txn,
                           UPPER(TRIM(cur.init)) AS currency_code
                    FROM wh_receiving_hdr w
                    JOIN grn_hdr g ON g.id = w.grn_hdr_id
                    LEFT JOIN m_currency cur ON cur.id = g.currency_id
                    LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = g.supplier_id
                    LEFT JOIN warehouses wh ON wh.id = w.warehouse_id
                    ORDER BY w.received_date DESC, w.id DESC";
            $all = fetchAll($db, $sql, [], 'list');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($all), 'data' => $all]);
            break;

        case 'read_edit':
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            $hdr = fetchOne($db, "SELECT * FROM wh_receiving_hdr WHERE id = :id", ['id' => $id], 'read_edit hdr');
            if (!$hdr) throw new Exception('Data tidak ditemukan');

            $hdr['items'] = fetchAll($db,
                "SELECT d.*, b.code AS bin_code, b.name AS bin_name
                   FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
                   LEFT JOIN bin_locations b ON b.id = d.bin_location_id
                  WHERE d.wh_receiving_hdr_id = :id
                  ORDER BY d.id",
                ['id' => $id], 'read_edit dtl');

            $curRow = fetchOne($db,
                "SELECT UPPER(TRIM(c.init)) AS code FROM grn_hdr g LEFT JOIN m_currency c ON c.id = g.currency_id WHERE g.id = :id",
                ['id' => $hdr['grn_hdr_id']], 'read_edit currency');
            $hdr['currency_code'] = $curRow['code'] ?? null;

            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $hdr]);
            break;

        case 'create':
            $grn_hdr_id    = (int)($_REQUEST['grn_hdr_id'] ?? 0);
            $warehouse_id  = (int)($_REQUEST['warehouse_id'] ?? 0);
            $received_date = $_REQUEST['received_date'] ?? date('Y-m-d');
            $remark        = $_REQUEST['remark'] ?? null;
            $received_by   = $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null;
            $items         = json_decode($_REQUEST['items'] ?? '[]', true);

            if (!$grn_hdr_id) throw new Exception('GRN belum dipilih');
            if (!$warehouse_id) throw new Exception('Gudang penerima belum dipilih');
            if (empty($items)) throw new Exception('Minimal 1 item harus diisi qty diterima');

            $totalQtyCheck = 0.0;
            foreach ($items as $it) { $totalQtyCheck += (float)($it['qty_received'] ?? 0); }
            if ($totalQtyCheck <= 0) {
                throw new Exception('Total qty diterima harus lebih dari 0');
            }

            $db->beginTransaction();
            try {
                $ref_no = function_exists('CreateRefNoDb')
                    ? CreateRefNoDb($db, 'wh_receiving_hdr', 'ref_no', 'WHR')
                    : CreateRefNo('wh_receiving_hdr', 'ref_no', 'WHR');
                if ($ref_no === '' || $ref_no === null) throw new Exception('Gagal generate No. Ref WH Rcv.');

                $wh_receiving_hdr_id = (int)fetchOne($db,
                    "INSERT INTO wh_receiving_hdr
                        (ref_no, grn_hdr_id, warehouse_id, received_date, remark, received_by, status, created_at)
                     VALUES (:ref_no, :grn_hdr_id, :warehouse_id, :received_date, :remark, :received_by, 'posted', NOW())
                     RETURNING id",
                    [
                        'ref_no' => $ref_no, 'grn_hdr_id' => $grn_hdr_id, 'warehouse_id' => $warehouse_id,
                        'received_date' => $received_date, 'remark' => $remark, 'received_by' => $received_by
                    ],
                    'insert wh_receiving_hdr')['id'];

                $total_amount = 0.0;

                foreach ($items as $it) {
                    $qty = (float)($it['qty_received'] ?? 0);
                    if ($qty <= 0) continue;

                    $chk = fetchOne($db,
                        "SELECT (gd.qty_received - COALESCE(wd.qty_wh_sum,0)) AS qty_remaining, gd.grade_id,
                                gd.unit_cost AS unit_cost_txn,
                                COALESCE(NULLIF(gd.unit_cost_base, 0), gd.unit_cost * COALESCE(NULLIF(g.exch_rate,0), 1)) AS unit_cost_base,
                                g.currency_id, COALESCE(NULLIF(g.exch_rate,0), 1) AS exch_rate
                           FROM grn_dtl gd
                           JOIN grn_hdr g ON g.id = gd.grn_hdr_id
                           LEFT JOIN (
                               SELECT d.grn_dtl_id, SUM(d.qty_received) AS qty_wh_sum
                                 FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
                                 JOIN wh_receiving_hdr h ON h.id = d.wh_receiving_hdr_id AND h.status = 'posted'
                                WHERE d.grn_dtl_id = :grn_dtl_id
                                GROUP BY d.grn_dtl_id
                           ) wd ON wd.grn_dtl_id = gd.id
                          WHERE gd.id = :grn_dtl_id AND gd.grn_hdr_id = :grn_hdr_id
                          FOR UPDATE OF gd",
                        ['grn_dtl_id' => $it['grn_dtl_id'], 'grn_hdr_id' => $grn_hdr_id], 'cek sisa qty GRN');
                    if (!$chk) throw new Exception('Baris GRN tidak ditemukan (grn_dtl_id=' . $it['grn_dtl_id'] . ')');
                    if ($qty > (float)$chk['qty_remaining'] + 0.0001) {
                        throw new Exception("Qty WH Rcv untuk {$it['item_name']} ({$qty}) melebihi sisa GRN (" . round((float)$chk['qty_remaining'], 4) . ")");
                    }

                    // Biaya MATA UANG DASAR ditentukan server dari GRN (unit_cost kiriman klien diabaikan)
                    $cost    = round((float)$chk['unit_cost_base'], 4);
                    $costTxn = (float)$chk['unit_cost_txn'];
                    $total_amount += round($qty * $cost, 2);
                    $grade_id = !empty($it['grade_id']) ? $it['grade_id'] : $chk['grade_id'];

                    execSql($db,
                        "INSERT INTO wh_receiving_dtl
                            (wh_receiving_hdr_id, grn_dtl_id, item_id, item_name, qty_received, unit_cost,
                             unit_cost_txn, currency_id, exch_rate,
                             bin_location_id, lot_no, grade_id, remark)
                         VALUES (:hdr_id, :grn_dtl_id, :item_id, :item_name, :qty, :cost,
                                 :cost_txn, :currency_id, :exch_rate,
                                 :bin_location_id, :lot_no, :grade_id, :remark)",
                        [
                            'hdr_id' => $wh_receiving_hdr_id, 'grn_dtl_id' => $it['grn_dtl_id'],
                            'item_id' => $it['item_id'], 'item_name' => $it['item_name'],
                            'qty' => $qty, 'cost' => $cost,
                            'cost_txn' => $costTxn, 'currency_id' => $chk['currency_id'], 'exch_rate' => (float)$chk['exch_rate'],
                            'bin_location_id' => $it['bin_location_id'] ?? null, 'lot_no' => $it['lot_no'] ?? null,
                            'grade_id' => $grade_id, 'remark' => $it['remark'] ?? null
                        ],
                        'insert wh_receiving_dtl');

                    PostStockMovement($db, [
                        'movement_date'  => $received_date,
                        'item_id'        => $it['item_id'],
                        'grade_id'       => $grade_id,
                        'warehouse_id'   => $warehouse_id,
                        'movement_type'  => 'IN',
                        'qty'            => $qty,
                        'unit_cost'      => $cost,
                        'reference_type' => 'wh_receiving',
                        'reference_id'   => $wh_receiving_hdr_id,
                        'reference_no'   => $ref_no,
                        'remark'         => $it['remark'] ?? 'Terima Fisik Gudang'
                    ]);
                }

                $journalResult = AutoPostJournal($db, [
                    'rule_code'      => 'GRN_RCV',
                    'reference_type' => 'wh_receiving',
                    'reference_id'   => $wh_receiving_hdr_id,
                    'journal_date'   => $received_date,
                    'amount'         => round($total_amount, 2),
                    'description'    => "Penerimaan fisik gudang - {$ref_no}"
                ]);
                if (empty($journalResult['success'])) {
                    throw new Exception('Gagal posting jurnal: ' . ($journalResult['message'] ?? 'tanpa pesan'));
                }

                RecalcGrnStatus($grn_hdr_id);

                $db->commit();
                echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'id' => $wh_receiving_hdr_id, 'ref_no' => $ref_no, 'message' => "Penerimaan gudang {$ref_no} berhasil disimpan."]);
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                throw $e;
            }
            break;

        case 'reverse':
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            if (!$id) throw new Exception('ID WH Receiving tidak valid');

            $db->beginTransaction();
            try {
                $hdr = fetchOne($db,
                    "SELECT * FROM wh_receiving_hdr WHERE id = :id AND status = 'posted' FOR UPDATE",
                    ['id' => $id], 'reverse hdr check');
                if (!$hdr) throw new Exception('Data tidak ditemukan atau sudah di-reverse');

                ReverseStockMovementByReference($db, 'wh_receiving', $id);
                ReverseJournalByReference($db, 'wh_receiving', $id);

                execSql($db,
                    "UPDATE wh_receiving_hdr SET status = 'reversed', reversed_at = NOW(), reversed_by = :uid WHERE id = :id",
                    ['uid' => $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null, 'id' => $id],
                    'update status reversed');

                RecalcGrnStatus($hdr['grn_hdr_id']);

                $db->commit();
                echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Transaksi WH Receiving berhasil di-reverse.']);
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                throw $e;
            }
            break;

        default:
            throw new Exception('Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task));
    }
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage() !== '' ? $e->getMessage() : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.']);
}

## File: ./m.form.nameCRD.php

<?php

    session_start();

try {
    // 1. Amankan parameter input pagination & pencarian dari ExtJS
    $startParam = $_POST["start"] ?? 0;
    $offset     = (empty($startParam)) ? 0 : intval($startParam);

    $queryParam  = $_POST["query"] ?? '';
    $sResult     = searchData($queryParam, array("dt.title", "dt.php_url", "dt.js_url", "g.name"), 100);
    $limit       = isset($sResult["limit"]) ? intval($sResult["limit"]) : 100;
    $searchField = isset($sResult["searchSQL"]) ? $sResult["searchSQL"] : '';

    // 2. Amankan konversi data array iteminfo
    $itemInfoParam = $_POST["iteminfo"] ?? '';
    $dataArray = '';
    if (!empty($itemInfoParam)) {
        $decodedInfo = json_decode($itemInfoParam, true);
        if (is_array($decodedInfo)) {
            $dataArray = implode(",", array_map('intval', $decodedInfo));
        }
    }
    $cleanItemNum = (empty($dataArray)) ? "0" : $dataArray;

    $task = $_POST['task'] ?? 'read';

    switch ($task) {
        case "create":
            $queryC = '';
            $rawForm = $_POST["arrForm"] ?? '[]';
            $dataForm = !empty($rawForm) ? json_decode($rawForm) : [];

            $table = "m_form_name";
            $data = array(
                "tna"           => $_POST["_tna"] ?? null,
                "group_id"      => intval($_POST["group_id"] ?? 0),
                "seq"           => checksinglequote($_POST["seq"] ?? ''),
                "title"         => checksinglequote($_POST["title"] ?? ''),

                "js_url"        => checksinglequote($_POST["js_url"] ?? ''),

                "main_table"    => checksinglequote($_POST["main_table"] ?? ''),
                "remark"        => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? ''),
                "active"           => $_POST["_active"] ?? null,

            );
            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

                foreach ($dataForm as $form) {
                    $table = "m_form_links";
                    $data = array(
                        "form_id"      => "currval('m_form_name_id_seq'::regclass)",
                        "link_form_id" => intval($form->link_form_id ?? 0)
                    );
                    $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                }
            }

            $db = new crud();
            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
            echo $db->getResult();
            break;

        case 'read':
            $db = new crud();

                dt.id, dt.group_id, dt.seq, dt.title, dt.php_url, dt.js_url, dt.remark, g.name as group_name,
                g.seq as group_seq, dt.tna, dt.active
            ";

                (
                    select fr.id, fr.group_id, fr.seq, coalesce(fr.title,'') title,
                        coalesce(fr.php_url,'') php_url, coalesce(fr.js_url,'') js_url,
                        coalesce(fr.remark,'') remark, tna, active
                    from m_form_name fr
                ) dt
                left join (
                    select id, name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, seq
                    from (
                        (select d.id, h.name||' -- '||d.name as name, d.remark, d.seq
                        from m_form_group d
                        left join (
                            select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'
                        ) h on substring(h.seq,1,2)=substring(d.seq,1,2)
                        where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
                        union
                        (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
                        from m_form_group h
                        where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
                    ) data
                ) g on g.id = dt.group_id
            ";
            
            //$where = "length(btrim(dt.title)) > 0";
            $where = "coalesce(trim(dt.title), '') <> ''";
            if (!empty($searchField) && trim($searchField) != "") {
                $where .= " " . $searchField;
            }
            
            $order = "g.seq ASC, g.name ASC, dt.seq ASC";

            $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
            echo $db->getResult();
            break;

        case "read_edit":
            $db = new crud();
            $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $table = "m_form_name";
            $field = "id, group_id, seq, title, php_url, js_url, remark, main_table, department_id, tna, active";
            $where = "id = " . $id;

            $db->select($table, $field, $where);
            echo $db->getResult();
            break;

        case "read_detail":
            $db = new crud();
            $formId = intval($_POST['form_id'] ?? 0);
            $field = "l.id link_id, l.link_form_id, f.id form_id, g.name as group_name, f.title, f.remark";
            $table = "
                m_form_links l
                left join m_form_name f on f.id = l.link_form_id
                left join (
                    select id, name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, seq
                    from (
                        (select d.id, h.name||' -- '||d.name as name, d.remark, d.seq
                        from m_form_group d
                        left join (
                            select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'

                        where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
                        union
                        (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
                        from m_form_group h
                        where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
                    ) data
                ) g on g.id = f.group_id
            ";
            $where = "l.form_id = " . $formId;
            $order = "g.name ASC, f.seq ASC";

            $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order);
            echo $db->getResult();

            $db = new crud();

                left join (

                        where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
                    ) data
                ) g on g.id = f.group_id
            ";

            if (!empty($searchField) && trim($searchField) != "") {
                $where .= " " . $searchField;
            }
            $order = "g.name ASC, f.seq ASC";

            $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order);
            echo $db->getResult();

            $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $table = "view_single_table_log";
            $field = "
                tx_id, date, count,
                case amend_name
                    when 'group_id' then '[EDIT] - Group'
                    when 'seq' then '[EDIT] - Seq'
                    when 'title' then '[EDIT] - Title'
                    when 'php_url' then '[EDIT] - PHP URL'
                    when 'js_url' then '[EDIT] - JS URL'
                    when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
                    else amend_name
                end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
                CASE
                    WHEN amend_name = 'group_id'
                        then (select name from view_form_group where id::int = before_amendment::int)
                    else before_amendment
                end as before_amendment,
                CASE
                    WHEN amend_name = 'group_id'
                        then (select name from view_form_group where id::int = after_amendment::int)
                    else after_amendment
                end as after_amendment,
                user_name, user_time
            ";
            $where = "tx_id::int = " . $id . " and table_name = 'm_form_name'";
            $orderBy = "count desc";

            $db->select('history', $table, $field, $where, $orderBy);
            echo $db->getResult();
            break;

            $queryC = '';
            $formId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $rawForm = $_POST["arrForm"] ?? '[]';
            $dataForm = !empty($rawForm) ? json_decode($rawForm) : [];

            // Memeriksa dan membersihkan relasi form_links lama
            $dbCheck = new crud();
            $dbCheck->select('m_form_links', 'id, form_id', "form_id = " . $formId, "id ASC");
            $resCheckJSON = $dbCheck->getResult();
            $resCheckArr = json_decode($resCheckJSON, true);
            $dataCheck = $resCheckArr["data"] ?? [];

            for ($c = 0; $c < count($dataCheck); $c++) {
                $dataFound = 0;
                $cId = $dataCheck[$c]['id'];
                for ($a = 0; $a < count($dataForm); $a++) {
                    if (isset($dataForm[$a]->link_id) && $dataForm[$a]->link_id == $cId) {
                        $dataFound = 1;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if ($dataFound == 0) {
                    $queryC .= deleteQ("m_form_links", "id = " . intval($cId)) . ";";
                }
            }

                "group_id"      => intval($_POST["group_id"] ?? 0),
                "seq"           => checksinglequote($_POST["seq"] ?? ''),
                "title"         => checksinglequote($_POST["title"] ?? ''),
                "php_url"       => checksinglequote($_POST["php_url"] ?? ''),

                "active"        => $_POST["_active"] ?? null,

            );

            $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";
