                            "link_form_id" => intval($form->link_form_id ?? 0)
                        );
                        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                    }
                }
            }

            $db = new crud();
            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
            echo $db->getResult();
            break;

        case 'delete':
            $queryC = '';
            $deleteId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);

            if ($deleteId > 0) {
                $queryC .= deleteQ("m_form_links", "form_id = " . $deleteId) . ";";
                $queryC .= deleteQ("m_form_links", "link_form_id = " . $deleteId) . ";";

                $queryC .= deleteQ("m_form_name", "id = " . $deleteId) . ";";

                $db = new crud();
                $db->cmdQ($queryC);
                echo $db->getResult();

                echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID Form tidak valid."]);
            }
            break;

        default:
            echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "Task tidak dikenali."]);
            break;
    }

} catch (Exception $e) {
    echo json_encode([
        "success" => false, 
        "status"  => "Error", 
        "message" => "Terjadi kesalahan sistem: " . $e->getMessage()
    ]);
}

## File: ./m.currency.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
session_start();
include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";

$searchField = isset($_POST["query"]) ? $_POST["query"] : NULL;
$offset      = (isset($_POST["start"]) && $_POST["start"] !== null) ? intval($_POST["start"]) : 0;
$limit       = (isset($_POST["limit"]) && $_POST["limit"] !== null) ? intval($_POST["limit"]) : 250;
$task        = isset($_POST['task']) ? $_POST['task'] : 'read';

$db = new crud();

switch ($task) {
	case ("create"):
	case ("add"):
		$tabel = "m_currency";
		$data = array(
			"init" => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),
			"name" => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? '')
		);
		$db->insert($tabel, $data);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ('read'):
		$sResult   = searchData($searchField, array("name", "init"), 250);
		$limit     = $sResult["limit"];
		$searchSQL = $sResult["searchSQL"];

		$fields = "id, init, name";
		$table  = "m_currency";
		$where  = "1=1 " . $searchSQL;
		$order  = "name ASC";

		$db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ("read_edit"):
		$id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
		$fields = "id, init, name";
		$table  = "m_currency";
		$where  = "id = " . $id;

		$db->select($table, $fields, $where);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ('HistoryData'):
	case ('read_history'):
		$id      = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
		$table   = "view_single_table_log";
		$fields  = "
			tx_id, date, count,
			case amend_name
				when 'init' then '[EDIT] - Init'
				when 'name' then '[EDIT] - Name'
				else amend_name
			end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
			before_amendment, after_amendment, user_name, user_time
		";
		$where   = "tx_id::int = " . $id . " and table_name = 'm_currency'";
		$orderBy = "count desc";

		$db->select($table, $fields, $where, $orderBy);
		echo $db->getResult();
	break;

	case ('update'):
		$id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
		if ($id > 0) {
			$tabel = "m_currency";
			$data = array(
				"init"   => checksinglequote($_POST["init"] ?? ''),
				"name"   => checksinglequote($_POST["name"] ?? ''),
			);
			$where = "id = " . $id;
			$db->update($tabel, $data, $where);
			echo $db->getResult();
		} else {
			echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk proses update."]);
		}
	break;

	case ('delete'):
		$id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
		if ($id > 0) {
			$tabel = "m_currency";
			$where = "id = " . $id;
			$db->delete($tabel, $where);
			echo $db->getResult();
		} else {
			echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk proses penghapusan."]);
		}
	break;

	default:
		echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "Task tidak dikenali."]);
	break;
}
?>

## File: ./financialreportCRD.php
----------------------------------------
<?php
/*
  Document      : Financial Report - Neraca & Laba Rugi
  Called from   : js/m.financialreport.js
  Note          : - Neraca (Aset/Liabilitas/Ekuitas): saldo KUMULATIF sampai
                    akhir periode terpilih (sama seperti Trial Balance).
                  - Laba Rugi (Pendapatan/Beban): HANYA mutasi DALAM periode
                    terpilih (bukan kumulatif -- laporan per-periode).
                  - Sistem ini belum ada mekanisme closing entries, jadi
                    "Laba (Rugi) Kumulatif" ditambahkan otomatis ke sisi
                    Ekuitas Neraca (dihitung dari akumulasi Pendapatan-Beban
                    sejak awal sampai akhir periode) supaya identitas
                    Aset = Liabilitas + Ekuitas tetap balance.
*/
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }

require_once  '../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once  '../php-class/crudClass.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION["user_id"]) || empty($_SESSION["user_id"])) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Session expired. Silakan login kembali."]);
    exit;
}

$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read_report';
$db   = new crud();

switch ($task) {

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_periods':
        $sql = "SELECT id, period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods ORDER BY start_date DESC";
        $stmt = $db->query($sql);
        $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $data]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    case 'read_report':
        $periodId = intval($_POST['period_id'] ?? 0);
        if ($periodId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode wajib dipilih.']);
            exit;
        }

        $stmtP = $db->prepare("SELECT period_name, start_date, end_date FROM accounting_periods WHERE id = :id");
        $stmtP->execute(['id' => $periodId]);
        $period = $stmtP->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        if (!$period) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Periode tidak ditemukan.']);
            exit;
        }
        $startDate = $period['start_date'];
        $endDate   = $period['end_date'];

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // NERACA -- saldo kumulatif per akun (asset/liability/equity)
        // sampai dengan akhir periode.
        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        $sqlBS = "
            SELECT c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type,
                   COALESCE(SUM(
                       CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'asset' THEN l.debit - l.credit
                            ELSE l.credit - l.debit END
                   ), 0) AS balance
            FROM chart_of_accounts c
            LEFT JOIN journal_lines l ON l.account_id = c.id
            LEFT JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
                AND j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')
                AND j.journal_date <= :end_date
            WHERE c.is_active = true AND c.account_type IN ('asset', 'liability', 'equity')
            GROUP BY c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type
            HAVING COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'asset' THEN l.debit - l.credit ELSE l.credit - l.debit END), 0) != 0
            ORDER BY c.account_code ASC
        ";
        $stmtBS = $db->prepare($sqlBS);
        $stmtBS->execute(['end_date' => $endDate]);
        $bsRows = $stmtBS->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $asset = []; $liability = []; $equity = [];
        $assetTotal = 0; $liabilityTotal = 0; $equityTotal = 0;
        foreach ($bsRows as $r) {
            $bal = floatval($r['balance']);
            $item = ['account_code' => $r['account_code'], 'account_name' => $r['account_name'], 'balance' => $bal];
            if ($r['account_type'] === 'asset')     { $asset[] = $item;     $assetTotal += $bal; }
            if ($r['account_type'] === 'liability')  { $liability[] = $item; $liabilityTotal += $bal; }
            if ($r['account_type'] === 'equity')     { $equity[] = $item;    $equityTotal += $bal; }
        }

        // Laba (Rugi) KUMULATIF sejak awal s/d akhir periode -- plug ke Ekuitas
        $sqlNI = "
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(
                CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'revenue' THEN l.credit - l.debit
                     ELSE -(l.debit - l.credit) END
            ), 0) AS net_income
            FROM journal_lines l
            JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id AND j.status IN ('posted','reversed') AND j.journal_date <= :end_date
            JOIN chart_of_accounts c ON c.id = l.account_id
            WHERE c.account_type IN ('revenue', 'expense')
        ";
        $stmtNI = $db->prepare($sqlNI);
        $stmtNI->execute(['end_date' => $endDate]);
        $netIncomeCumulative = floatval($stmtNI->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)['net_income']);
        $equityTotal += $netIncomeCumulative;

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LABA RUGI -- HANYA mutasi DALAM periode terpilih (bukan kumulatif)
        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        $sqlIS = "
            SELECT c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type,
                   COALESCE(SUM(
                       CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'revenue' THEN l.credit - l.debit
                            ELSE l.debit - l.credit END
                   ), 0) AS amount
            FROM chart_of_accounts c
            JOIN journal_lines l ON l.account_id = c.id
            JOIN journal_entries j ON j.id = l.journal_id
                AND j.status IN ('posted', 'reversed')
                AND j.journal_date BETWEEN :start_date AND :end_date
            WHERE c.is_active = true AND c.account_type IN ('revenue', 'expense')
            GROUP BY c.id, c.account_code, c.account_name, c.account_type
            HAVING COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN c.account_type = 'revenue' THEN l.credit - l.debit ELSE l.debit - l.credit END), 0) != 0
            ORDER BY c.account_code ASC
        ";
        $stmtIS = $db->prepare($sqlIS);
        $stmtIS->execute(['start_date' => $startDate, 'end_date' => $endDate]);
        $isRows = $stmtIS->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $revenue = []; $expense = [];
        $revenueTotal = 0; $expenseTotal = 0;
        foreach ($isRows as $r) {
            $amt = floatval($r['amount']);
            $item = ['account_code' => $r['account_code'], 'account_name' => $r['account_name'], 'amount' => $amt];
            if ($r['account_type'] === 'revenue') { $revenue[] = $item; $revenueTotal += $amt; }
            if ($r['account_type'] === 'expense')  { $expense[] = $item; $expenseTotal += $amt; }
        }
        $netIncomePeriod = $revenueTotal - $expenseTotal;

        echo json_encode([
            'success'     => true,
            'period_name' => $period['period_name'],
            'start_date'  => $startDate,
            'end_date'    => $endDate,
            'balance_sheet' => [
                'asset' => $asset, 'liability' => $liability, 'equity' => $equity,
                'asset_total' => $assetTotal, 'liability_total' => $liabilityTotal, 'equity_total' => $equityTotal,
                'net_income_cumulative' => $netIncomeCumulative,
                'is_balance' => abs($assetTotal - ($liabilityTotal + $equityTotal)) < 0.01
            ],
            'income_statement' => [
                'revenue' => $revenue, 'expense' => $expense,
                'revenue_total' => $revenueTotal, 'expense_total' => $expenseTotal,
                'net_income_period' => $netIncomePeriod
            ]
        ]);
        break;

    // ==============================================================
    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task)]);
}


## File: ./purchase.orderCRD.php

<?php
if (!isset($_SESSION)) {
    session_start();
}
date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Jakarta');

include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";

// 1. Amankan penangkapan parameter awal & paging
$startParam  = $_POST["start"] ?? 0;
$offset      = ($startParam === null || $startParam === '') ? 0 : intval($startParam);

$queryParam  = $_POST["query"] ?? '';
$sResult     = searchData($queryParam, array("supplier_name", "ref_no"), 250);
$limit       = intval($sResult["limit"] ?? 250);
$searchSQL   = is_array($sResult["searchSQL"]) ? '' : strval($sResult["searchSQL"]);

$rawItemInfo = $_POST["iteminfo"] ?? '';
$dataArray   = !empty($rawItemInfo) ? json2Array($rawItemInfo) : [];

if (empty($dataArray)) {
    $CheckItemNum  = "[0]";
    $CheckItemChar = "['0']";
} else {
    $formattedData = is_array($dataArray) ? implode(",", $dataArray) : $dataArray;
    $CheckItemNum  = "[" . $formattedData . "]";
    $CheckItemChar = "['" . $formattedData . "']";
}

$db   = new crud();
$task = $_POST['task'] ?? 'read';

switch ($task) {

    // ==========================================
    // 1. CREATE / ADD (Tambah Purchase Order)
    // ==========================================
    case ("create"):
    case ("add"):
        $AddRefNo = CreateRefNo('po_hdr', 'ref_no', 'KMGI/PO');
        $ETADate  = empty($_POST["eta_date"]) ? null : date('Y-m-d', strtotime($_POST["eta_date"]));
        $queryC   = '';

        // Master PO Header
        $table = "po_hdr";
        $data  = array(
            "po_ppn"            => $_POST["ppn"] ?? 'false',
            "price_include_ppn" => $_POST["include_ppn"] ?? 'false',
            "ref_no"            => $AddRefNo,
            "tx_date"           => "now()",
            "eta_date"          => $ETADate,
            "supplier_id"       => intval($_POST["supplier_id"] ?? 0),
            "payment_period"    => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["payment_period"] ?? 0)),
            "curr_id"           => intval($_POST["currency_id"] ?? 0),
            "exch_rate"         => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["exch_rate"] ?? 1)),
            "disc"              => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["disc_po"] ?? 0)),
            "disc_percent"      => $_POST["disc_pcnt"] ?? 'false',
            "allowance"         => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["allowance"] ?? 0)),
            "allow_percent"     => $_POST["allow_pcnt"] ?? 'false',
            "remark"            => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? '')
        );
        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

        // Biaya Tambahan (Costs)
        $rawCost  = $_POST["arrCostDtl"] ?? '[]';
        $dataCost = !empty($rawCost) ? json_decode($rawCost) : [];
        if (is_array($dataCost)) {
            foreach ($dataCost as $cost) {
                if (isset($cost->cost_id)) {
                    $table = "po_costs";
                    $data  = array(
                        "po_id"      => "currval('po_hdr_id_seq'::regclass)",
                        "cost_id"    => intval($cost->cost_id),
                        "amount"     => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $cost->amount ?? 0)),
                        "po_include" => $cost->po_include ?? 'false'
                    );
                    $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                }
            }
        }

        // Detail Item PO
        $rawItem  = $_POST["arrItemDtl"] ?? '[]';
        $dataItem = !empty($rawItem) ? json_decode($rawItem) : [];
        if (is_array($dataItem)) {
            foreach ($dataItem as $item) {
                $itemId = intval($item->item_id ?? 0);
                if ($itemId > 0) {
                    $table = "po_dtl";
                    $data  = array(
                        "po_hdr_id"         => "currval('po_hdr_id_seq'::regclass)",
                        "qty"               => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->qty ?? 0)),
                        "unit_price"        => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->unit_price ?? 0)),
                        "remark"            => checksinglequote($item->remark ?? ''),
                        "additional_qty"    => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->additional_qty ?? 0)),
                        "disc_item"         => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->disc_item ?? 0)),
                        "disc_item_percent" => $item->disc_item_percent ?? 'false'
                    );
                    $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

                    // Cek Binding Document / Purchase Request via PDO select()
                    $checkReqDb = new crud();
                    $reqField   = "b.id";
                    $reqTable   = "view_item_req_status s left join item_req_binding_doc b on b.hdr_tx_id = s.hdr_tx_id and b.dtl_tx_id = s.dtl_tx_id";
                    $reqWhere   = "s.item_id = " . $itemId . " and coalesce(b.id::bigint, 0) > 0";
                    
                    $checkReqDb->select($reqTable, $reqField, $reqWhere);
                    $resReqJSON = $checkReqDb->getResult();
                    $resReqArr  = json_decode($resReqJSON, true);
                    $reqList    = $resReqArr["data"] ?? [];

                    foreach ($reqList as $reqObj) {
                        $reqBindingId = intval($reqObj["id"] ?? 0);
                        if ($reqBindingId > 0) {
                            $table = "po_dtl_doc_req";
                            $data  = array(
                                "po_dtl_id"      => "currval('po_dtl_id_seq'::regclass)",
                                "req_binding_id" => $reqBindingId
                            );
                            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        $db->cmdQ($queryC);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 2. READ (Daftar PO Header)
    // ==========================================
  // ==========================================
    // 1. READ (Daftar Purchase Order Main Grid)
    // ==========================================
    case ('read'):
        $queryParam = $_POST["query"] ?? '';
        $sResult    = searchData($queryParam, array("supplier_name", "ref_no"), 250);
        
        $limit     = intval($sResult["limit"] ?? 250);
        $searchSQL = is_array($sResult["searchSQL"]) ? '' : strval($sResult["searchSQL"]);
        
        $startParam = $_POST["start"] ?? 0;
        $offset     = ($startParam === null || $startParam === '') ? 0 : intval($startParam);

        $field = "
            id, ref_no, tx_date, eta_date, po_ppn, price_include_ppn, supplier_id, curr_id as currency_id, payment_period, payment, currency, currency_init,
            supplier_name as supplier, address, office_phone, office_fax, office_email, costs_list as cost_name, cost_amt, coalesce(remark, '') remark,
            allowance, allow_percent, disc as disc_po, disc_percent,
            (case when disc_percent = 't' then to_char(disc, '999D99') || ' %' else '0' end)::varchar(100) as disc, disc_amt as disc_payment,
            total_amt as total_amount, dpp,
            round((
                case when coalesce(dpp, 0) = 0 then total_amt - disc_amt + cost_amt
                else (((dpp * .1) + dpp) - disc_amt) + cost_amt
                end), CASE WHEN lower(currency_init) ~~ '%idr%'::text THEN 0 else 2 end) as total_payment
        ";
        $table = "view_po_hdr";
        $where = "1=1 " . $searchSQL;
        $order = "ref_no DESC";

        $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 2. READ EDIT (Header Single PO untuk Edit)
    // ==========================================
    case ("read_edit"):
    case ("readedit"):
        $poId  = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $field = "
            id, ref_no, tx_date, eta_date, po_ppn, price_include_ppn, supplier_id, curr_id as currency_id, payment_period, payment, currency, currency_init,
            supplier_name as supplier, address, office_phone, office_fax, office_email, costs_list as cost_name, cost_amt, coalesce(remark, '') remark,
            allowance, allow_percent, disc as disc_po, disc_percent,
            (case when disc_percent = 't' then to_char(disc, '999D99') || ' %' else '0' end)::varchar(100) as disc, disc_amt as disc_payment,
            total_amt as total_amount, dpp,
            round((
                case when coalesce(dpp, 0) = 0 then total_amt - disc_amt + cost_amt
                else (((dpp * .1) + dpp) - disc_amt) + cost_amt
                end), CASE WHEN lower(currency_init) ~~ '%idr%'::text THEN 0 else 2 end) as total_payment
        ";
        $table = "view_po_hdr";
        $where = "id = " . $poId;

        $db->select($table, $field, $where);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 3. READ DETAIL (List Item PO)
    // ==========================================
    case ("read_detail"):
        $poHdrId = intval($_POST['po_hdr_id'] ?? 0);
        $field   = "
            po_hdr_id, po_dtl_id, item_id, incl_ppn, item_name, um_unit, curr,
            coalesce(additional_qty, 0) as additional_qty, coalesce(req_qty, 0) as req_qty, po_qty as total_qty,
            unit_price, disc_item, disc_percent as disc_item_percent, disc, disc_amount as item_disc,
            line_total as total_item, coalesce(remark, '') remark
        ";
        $table   = "view_po_dtl";
        $where   = "po_hdr_id = " . $poHdrId;
        $order   = "item_name ASC";

        $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 4. ADD REQ (Lookup Item Request)
    // ==========================================
    case ('addreq'):
        $queryParam = $_POST["query"] ?? '';
        $sResult    = searchData($queryParam, array("dt.item_name"), $limit);
        $searchSQL  = is_array($sResult["searchSQL"]) ? '' : strval($sResult["searchSQL"]);

        $startParam = $_POST["start"] ?? 0;
        $offset     = ($startParam === null || $startParam === '') ? 0 : intval($startParam);

        $field = "item_id, item_name, size, um_unit, req_qty, issue_qty, os_qty";
        $table = "
            (
                select s.item_id, s.item_name, s.size, s.um_unit,
                    sum(coalesce(s.order_qty, 0)) as req_qty,
                    sum(coalesce(p.po_qty, 0)) as issue_qty,
                    (sum(coalesce(s.order_qty, 0)) - sum(coalesce(p.po_qty, 0))) as os_qty
                from view_item_req_status s
                left join item_req_binding_doc b on b.hdr_tx_id = s.hdr_tx_id and b.dtl_tx_id = s.dtl_tx_id
                left join (
                    select pr.req_binding_id, sum(coalesce(dt.qty, 0)) as po_qty
                    from po_dtl dt
                    left join po_dtl_doc_req pr ON dt.id = pr.po_dtl_id
                    group by pr.req_binding_id
                ) p on coalesce(p.req_binding_id, 0) = coalesce(b.id, 0)
                where b.purchase_status = true and b.completed = false
                group by s.item_id, s.item_name, s.size, s.um_unit
            ) dt
        ";
        $where = "
            dt.item_id <> all (array" . str_replace("'", "", $CheckItemNum) . ")
            and dt.os_qty > 0 " . $searchSQL;
        $order = "lower(dt.item_name) ASC";

        $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 5. READ COSTS (Pilihan Biaya Tambahan)
    // ==========================================
    case ('readcosts'):
        $costStatus = $_POST['costStatus'] ?? 'local';
        $poId       = intval($_POST['po_id'] ?? 0);
        $where      = ($costStatus === 'local') ? "cs.purchase_local is true" : "cs.purchase_overseas is true";

        $field = "cs.id as cost_id, cs.name as cost_name, dt.po_id, dt.po_cost_id, dt.amount, dt.po_include";
        $table = "
            m_costs cs
            left join (
                select c.id, c.name, p.po_id, p.id as po_cost_id, p.amount, p.po_include
                from m_costs c
                left join po_costs p on c.id = p.cost_id and p.po_id = " . $poId . "
            ) dt on coalesce(cs.id, 0) = coalesce(dt.id, 0)
        ";
        $order = "cs.name ASC";

        $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;
    // ==========================================
    // 7. HISTORY DATA LOG
    // ==========================================
    case ('HistoryData'):
        $poId  = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $field = "
            tx_id, date, count,
            case amend_name
                when 'ref_no' then '[EDIT] - Ref No'
                when 'supplier_id' then '[EDIT] - Supplier'
                when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
                else amend_name
            end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
            before_amendment, after_amendment, user_name, user_time
        ";
        $table   = "view_single_table_log";
        $where   = "tx_id::int = " . $poId . " and table_name = 'po_hdr'";
        $orderBy = "count desc";

        $db->select('history', $table, $field, $where, $orderBy);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 8. UPDATE (Ubah / Edit PO)
    // ==========================================
    case ('update'):
    case ('edit'):
        $poId     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $ETADate  = empty($_POST["eta_date"]) ? null : date('Y-m-d', strtotime($_POST["eta_date"]));
        $queryC   = '';

        if ($poId <= 0) {
            echo json_encode(["success" => "false", "status" => "Error", "message" => "ID PO tidak valid."]);
            break;
        }

        $rawItem  = $_POST["arrItemDtl"] ?? '[]';
        $dataItem = !empty($rawItem) ? json_decode($rawItem) : [];
        $rawCost  = $_POST["arrCostDtl"] ?? '[]';
        $dataCost = !empty($rawCost) ? json_decode($rawCost) : [];

        // 1. Cek & Hapus Item PO yang dihilangkan
        $checkItemDb = new crud();
        $checkItemDb->select("po_dtl", "id", "po_hdr_id = " . $poId, "id ASC");
        $resItemJSON = $checkItemDb->getResult();
        $resItemArr  = json_decode($resItemJSON, true);
        $dataCheckItem = $resItemArr["data"] ?? [];

        foreach ($dataCheckItem as $rowOld) {
            $oldDtlId = $rowOld['id'];
            $found    = false;

            if (is_array($dataItem)) {
                foreach ($dataItem as $item) {
                    if (isset($item->po_dtl_id) && intval($item->po_dtl_id) === intval($oldDtlId)) {
                        $found = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!$found) {
                $queryC .= deleteQ("po_dtl_doc_req", "po_dtl_id = " . intval($oldDtlId)) . ";";
                $queryC .= deleteQ("po_dtl", "id = " . intval($oldDtlId)) . ";";
            }
        }

        // 2. Cek & Hapus Biaya (Costs) PO yang dihilangkan
        $checkCostDb = new crud();
        $checkCostDb->select("po_costs", "id", "po_id = " . $poId, "id ASC");
        $resCostJSON = $checkCostDb->getResult();
        $resCostArr  = json_decode($resCostJSON, true);
        $dataCheckCost = $resCostArr["data"] ?? [];

        foreach ($dataCheckCost as $rowOld) {
            $oldCostId = $rowOld['id'];
            $found     = false;

            if (is_array($dataCost)) {
                foreach ($dataCost as $cost) {
                    if (isset($cost->po_cost_id) && intval($cost->po_cost_id) === intval($oldCostId)) {
                        $found = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!$found) {
                $queryC .= deleteQ("po_costs", "id = " . intval($oldCostId)) . ";";
            }
        }

        // 3. Update Header PO
        $table = "po_hdr";
        $data  = array(
            "po_ppn"            => $_POST["ppn"] ?? 'false',
            "price_include_ppn" => $_POST["include_ppn"] ?? 'false',
            "eta_date"          => $ETADate,
            "supplier_id"       => intval($_POST["supplier_id"] ?? 0),
            "payment_period"    => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["payment_period"] ?? 0)),
            "curr_id"           => intval($_POST["currency_id"] ?? 0),
            "exch_rate"         => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["exch_rate"] ?? 1)),
            "disc"              => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["disc_po"] ?? 0)),
            "disc_percent"      => $_POST["disc_pcnt"] ?? 'false',
            "allowance"         => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $_POST["allowance"] ?? 0)),
            "allow_percent"     => $_POST["allow_pcnt"] ?? 'false',
            "remark"            => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? '')
        );
        $where = "id = " . $poId;
        $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";

        // 4. Save / Update Detail Item PO
        if (is_array($dataItem)) {
            foreach ($dataItem as $item) {
                $poDtlId = intval($item->po_dtl_id ?? 0);
                $itemId  = intval($item->item_id ?? 0);

                if ($poDtlId <= 0) {
                    // Tambah Item Baru
                    $table = "po_dtl";
                    $data  = array(
                        "po_hdr_id"         => $poId,
                        "qty"               => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->qty ?? 0)),
                        "unit_price"        => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->unit_price ?? 0)),
                        "remark"            => checksinglequote($item->remark ?? ''),
                        "additional_qty"    => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->additional_qty ?? 0)),
                        "disc_item"         => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->disc_item ?? 0)),
                        "disc_item_percent" => $item->disc_item_percent ?? 'false'
                    );
                    $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";

                    // Bind Item Request
                    if ($itemId > 0) {
                        $checkReqDb = new crud();
                        $reqField   = "b.id";
                        $reqTable   = "view_item_req_status s left join item_req_binding_doc b on b.hdr_tx_id = s.hdr_tx_id and b.dtl_tx_id = s.dtl_tx_id";
                        $reqWhere   = "s.item_id = " . $itemId . " and coalesce(b.id::bigint, 0) > 0";

                        $checkReqDb->select($reqTable, $reqField, $reqWhere);
                        $resReqJSON = $checkReqDb->getResult();
                        $resReqArr  = json_decode($resReqJSON, true);
                        $reqList    = $resReqArr["data"] ?? [];

                        foreach ($reqList as $reqObj) {
                            $reqBindingId = intval($reqObj["id"] ?? 0);
                            if ($reqBindingId > 0) {
                                $table = "po_dtl_doc_req";
                                $data  = array(
                                    "po_dtl_id"      => "currval('po_dtl_id_seq'::regclass)",
                                    "req_binding_id" => $reqBindingId
                                );
                                $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    // Update Item yang Ada
                    $table = "po_dtl";
                    $data  = array(
                        "qty"               => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->qty ?? 0)),
                        "unit_price"        => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->unit_price ?? 0)),
                        "remark"            => checksinglequote($item->remark ?? ''),
                        "additional_qty"    => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->additional_qty ?? 0)),
                        "disc_item"         => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $item->disc_item ?? 0)),
                        "disc_item_percent" => $item->disc_item_percent ?? 'false'
                    );
                    $where  = "id = " . $poDtlId;
                    $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";
                }
            }
        }

        // 5. Save / Update Detail Costs PO
        if (is_array($dataCost)) {
            foreach ($dataCost as $cost) {
                $poCostId = intval($cost->po_cost_id ?? 0);

                if ($poCostId <= 0) {
                    // Tambah Cost Baru
                    $table = "po_costs";
                    $data  = array(
                        "po_id"      => $poId,
                        "cost_id"    => intval($cost->cost_id ?? 0),
                        "amount"     => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $cost->amount ?? 0)),
                        "po_include" => $cost->po_include ?? 'false'
                    );
                    $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
                } else {
                    // Update Cost yang Ada
                    $table = "po_costs";
                    $data  = array(
                        "amount"     => floatval(str_replace(",", "", $cost->amount ?? 0)),
                        "po_include" => $cost->po_include ?? 'false'
                    );
                    $where  = "id = " . $poCostId;
                    $queryC .= updateQ($table, $data, $where) . ";";
                }
            }
        }

        $db->cmdQ($queryC);
        echo $db->getResult();
    break;

    // ==========================================
    // 9. DELETE (Hapus Master PO Beserta Relasinya)
    // ==========================================
    case ('delete'):
        $poId   = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $queryC = '';

        if ($poId > 0) {
            // Hapus Biaya
            $table  = "po_costs";
            $where  = "po_id = " . $poId;
            $queryC .= deleteQ($table, $where) . ";";

            // Hapus Relasi Item Req & Detail Item
            $queryC .= "DELETE FROM po_dtl_doc_req WHERE po_dtl_id IN (SELECT id FROM po_dtl WHERE po_hdr_id = " . $poId . ");";
            $queryC .= deleteQ("po_dtl", "po_hdr_id = " . $poId) . ";";

            // Hapus Header
            $queryC .= deleteQ("po_hdr", "id = " . $poId) . ";";

            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
            echo $db->getResult();
        } else {
            echo json_encode([
                "success" => "false",
                "status"  => "Error",
                "message" => "ID PO tidak valid untuk proses penghapusan."
            ]);
        }
    break;

    default:
        echo json_encode([
            "success" => "false",
            "status"  => "Error",
            "message" => "Task atau perintah tidak dikenali oleh sistem."
        ]);
    break;
}
?>

## File: ./grnCRD.php

<?php
/**
 * grn00CRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Backend untuk grn00.js (Proses GRN tanpa Simpan Warehouse).
 * Kontrak task: list_po_open, read_po_items, read, 
 *               read_grn_items, read_edit, create, update, delete
 *
 * MULTI CURRENCY (sql/2026-10-01.grn_currency.sql):
 *  - GRN membekukan mata uang & kurs PO (grn_hdr.currency_id / exch_rate).
 *  - grn_dtl.unit_cost      = harga per unit dalam MATA UANG PO (bisa diedit saat GRN).
 *  - grn_dtl.unit_cost_base = unit_cost x kurs PO = MATA UANG DASAR (dihitung server, bukan dari klien);
 *    inilah yang dipakai WH Rcv untuk FIFO, stok, dan jurnal.
 *  - GRN hanya boleh dibuat dari PO berstatus Disetujui (GRN_PO_STATUS_LIST). PO Draft/Diajukan/Dibatalkan ditolak.
 * ============================================================
 */

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';

session_start();
header('Content-Type: application/json');

const GRN_PO_STATUS_LIST = ['Disetujui'];     // status PO yang boleh di-GRN

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';
$db = new crud();

function PgOrFail($result, $context = '') {
    if ($result === false) {
        throw new Exception(($context ? "[{$context}] " : '') . pg_last_error());
    }
    return $result;
}

/** Kode mata uang dasar (Pengaturan Sistem); belum diatur -> IDR. Hanya untuk tampilan. */
function GrnBaseCode() {
    $r = pg_query("SELECT to_regclass('public.finance_settings') AS t");
    if ($r && ($row = pg_fetch_assoc($r)) && $row['t']) {
        $q = pg_query("SELECT UPPER(TRIM(c.init)) AS code FROM finance_settings f
                         JOIN m_currency c ON c.id = f.base_currency_id WHERE f.id = 1");
        if ($q && ($rw = pg_fetch_assoc($q)) && $rw['code']) return $rw['code'];
    }
    return 'IDR';
}

/** Menetapkan variabel sesi PostgreSQL untuk pencatatan log audit/trigger */
/*function SetPgAppUser() {
    $username = $_SESSION['username'] ?? $_SESSION['login_name'] ?? 'system';
    $res = pg_query_params("SELECT set_config('app.username', $1, true)", [$username]);
    PgOrFail($res, 'Gagal menetapkan app.username PostgreSQL');
}*/
/** Info mata uang untuk tampilan: dari snapshot GRN bila $grn_id > 0, selain itu dari PO. */
function GrnCurrencyInfo($po_id, $grn_id) {
    if ($grn_id > 0) {
        $res = pg_query_params("SELECT UPPER(TRIM(c.init)) AS code, COALESCE(NULLIF(g.exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate
                                  FROM grn_hdr g LEFT JOIN m_currency c ON c.id = g.currency_id WHERE g.id = $1", [$grn_id]);
    } else {
        $res = pg_query_params("SELECT UPPER(TRIM(c.init)) AS code, COALESCE(NULLIF(p.exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate
                                  FROM purchase_orders p LEFT JOIN m_currency c ON c.id = p.currency_id WHERE p.id = $1", [$po_id]);
    }
    PgOrFail($res, 'Gagal load mata uang');
    $row = pg_fetch_assoc($res) ?: [];
    $base = GrnBaseCode();
    return ['code' => $row['code'] ?: $base, 'base_code' => $base, 'exch_rate' => (float)($row['exch_rate'] ?? 1)];
}

function GetPoDtlTolerance($po_dtl_id, $po_qty, $exclude_grn_hdr_id = 0) {
    // Total Qty yang sudah diterima GRN lain untuk item PO ini
    $sql = "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(qty_received),0) AS received_other
            FROM grn_dtl 
            WHERE po_item_id = $1 AND grn_hdr_id <> $2";
    $res = pg_query_params($sql, [$po_dtl_id, (int)$exclude_grn_hdr_id]);
    PgOrFail($res, 'Gagal cek akumulasi qty GRN');
    $row = pg_fetch_assoc($res);
    $receivedOther = (float)($row['received_other'] ?? 0);

    $qtyMaxAllowed = round($po_qty * 1.10, 4);
    $qtyReceivableMax = max(0, $qtyMaxAllowed - $receivedOther);

    return [
        'others_qty'         => $receivedOther,
        'qty_max_allowed'    => $qtyMaxAllowed,
        'qty_receivable_max' => $qtyReceivableMax
    ];
}

try {
    switch ($task) {

        case 'list_po_open':
            // HANYA MENGAMBIL PO YANG MEMILIKI SISA QTY BELUM DI-GRN
            $sql = "SELECT p.id, p.po_number AS ref_no, p.po_date AS tx_date, 
                           p.supplier_id, 
                           COALESCE(sup.id, p.supplier_id) AS legacy_supplier_id, 
                           sup.name AS supplier_name,
                           p.currency_id, UPPER(TRIM(cur.init)) AS currency_code,
                           COALESCE(NULLIF(p.exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate
                    FROM purchase_orders p
                    LEFT JOIN m_suppliers sup ON sup.id = p.supplier_id
                    LEFT JOIN m_currency cur ON cur.id = p.currency_id
                    JOIN po_items pd ON pd.po_id = p.id
                    LEFT JOIN (
                        SELECT po_item_id, SUM(qty_received) AS total_rcv
                        FROM grn_dtl
                        GROUP BY po_item_id
                    ) g ON g.po_item_id = pd.id
                    WHERE p.status IN ('" . implode("','", GRN_PO_STATUS_LIST) . "')
                    GROUP BY p.id, p.po_number, p.po_date, p.supplier_id, sup.id, sup.name, p.currency_id, cur.init, p.exch_rate
                    HAVING SUM(pd.qty) - COALESCE(SUM(g.total_rcv), 0) > 0.0001
                    ORDER BY p.po_date DESC";
            $result = pg_query($sql);
            PgOrFail($result, 'Gagal load daftar PO');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => pg_fetch_all($result) ?: []]);
            break;

        case 'read_po_items':
            $po_id  = (int)($_REQUEST['po_id'] ?? 0);
            $grn_id = (int)($_REQUEST['grn_id'] ?? 0);

            $sql = "SELECT pd.id AS po_dtl_id, pd.material_id AS item_id, pd.description AS item_name, 
                           pd.qty AS qty_ordered, pd.unit_price
                    FROM po_items pd
                    WHERE pd.po_id = $1
                    ORDER BY pd.id";
            $result = pg_query_params($sql, [$po_id]);
            PgOrFail($result, 'Gagal load detail PO');
            $rows = pg_fetch_all($result) ?: [];

            $data = [];
            foreach ($rows as $r) {
                $poQty = (float)$r['qty_ordered'];
                $tol   = GetPoDtlTolerance($r['po_dtl_id'], $poQty, $grn_id);

                // Cek apakah item ini milik GRN yang sedang diedit
                $ownResult = pg_query_params(
                    "SELECT qty_received, unit_cost FROM grn_dtl WHERE po_item_id = $1 AND grn_hdr_id = $2",
                    [$r['po_dtl_id'], $grn_id]
                );
                PgOrFail($ownResult, 'Gagal cek baris grn_dtl existing');
                $own = pg_fetch_assoc($ownResult);

                // Hitung sisa murni PO (PO_Qty - Received_Others)
                $sisaQtyPure = max(0, $poQty - $tol['others_qty']);

                // Sisa maksimal yang diizinkan (Toleransi 10%)
                $sisaQtyBolehditerima = $tol['qty_receivable_max'];

                // Jika Sisa Qty Murni <= 0 dan bukan dalam mode Edit item terkait, skip (jangan tampilkan)
                if ($sisaQtyPure <= 0.0001 && !$own) {
                    continue;
                }

                $data[] = [
                    'po_dtl_id'             => $r['po_dtl_id'],
                    'item_id'               => $r['item_id'],
                    'item_name'             => $r['item_name'],
                    'qty_ordered'           => $poQty,
                    'unit_price'            => (float)$r['unit_price'],
                    'qty_received'          => $tol['others_qty'],
                    'sisa_qty_po'           => $sisaQtyPure,
                    'qty_max_allowed'       => $tol['qty_max_allowed'],
                    'qty_receivable_max'    => $sisaQtyBolehditerima,
                    'qty_received_this_grn' => $own ? (float)$own['qty_received'] : $sisaQtyPure,
                    'unit_cost_this_grn'    => $own ? (float)$own['unit_cost'] : (float)$r['unit_price']
                ];
            }

            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($data), 'data' => $data,
                              'currency' => GrnCurrencyInfo($po_id, $grn_id)]);
            break;

        case 'read':
            $sql = "SELECT g.id, g.ref_no, g.grn_date, g.status, g.remark,
                           p.po_number AS po_ref_no, g.po_id, s.id AS supplier_id, s.name AS supplier_name,
                           (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(qty_received),0) FROM grn_dtl d WHERE d.grn_hdr_id = g.id) AS total_qty,
                           (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(qty_received * unit_cost),0) FROM grn_dtl d WHERE d.grn_hdr_id = g.id) AS total_amount,
                           (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(qty_received * unit_cost_base),0) FROM grn_dtl d WHERE d.grn_hdr_id = g.id) AS total_base,
                           UPPER(TRIM(cur.init)) AS currency_code, COALESCE(NULLIF(g.exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate
                    FROM grn_hdr g
                    LEFT JOIN purchase_orders p ON p.id = g.po_id
                    LEFT JOIN m_suppliers s ON s.id = g.supplier_id
                    LEFT JOIN m_currency cur ON cur.id = g.currency_id
                    ORDER BY g.grn_date DESC, g.id DESC";
            $result = pg_query($sql);
            PgOrFail($result, 'Gagal load daftar GRN');
            $all = pg_fetch_all($result) ?: [];
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($all), 'data' => $all]);
            break;

        case 'read_grn_items':
            $grn_id = (int)($_REQUEST['grn_id'] ?? 0);
            $sql = "SELECT item_name, qty_received AS qty, unit_cost AS unit_price, unit_cost_base
                    FROM grn_dtl WHERE grn_hdr_id = $1 ORDER BY id";
            $result = pg_query_params($sql, [$grn_id]);
            PgOrFail($result, 'Gagal load item GRN');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => pg_fetch_all($result) ?: [],
                              'currency' => GrnCurrencyInfo(0, $grn_id)]);
            break;

        case 'read_edit':
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            $hdrResult = pg_query_params("SELECT * FROM grn_hdr WHERE id = $1", [$id]);
            PgOrFail($hdrResult, 'Gagal load GRN');
            $hdr = pg_fetch_assoc($hdrResult);
            if (!$hdr) throw new Exception('Data tidak ditemukan');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $hdr]);
            break;

        case 'create':
            $po_id       = (int)($_REQUEST['po_id'] ?? 0);
            $supplier_id = (int)($_REQUEST['supplier_id'] ?? 0);
            $grn_date    = $_REQUEST['grn_date'] ?? date('Y-m-d');
            $remark      = $_REQUEST['remark'] ?? null;
            $arrItems    = json_decode($_REQUEST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);
            $created_by  = $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null;

            if (!$po_id) throw new Exception('PO belum dipilih');

            if (!$supplier_id) {
                $supRes = pg_query_params("SELECT supplier_id FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = $1", [$po_id]);
                if ($supRes && $supRow = pg_fetch_assoc($supRes)) {
                    $supplier_id = (int)$supRow['supplier_id'];
                }
            }

            if (!$supplier_id) throw new Exception('Supplier tidak ditemukan dari PO');
            if (empty($arrItems)) throw new Exception('Minimal 1 item harus diisi qty diterima');

            // PO harus berstatus Disetujui; mata uang & kurs PO dibekukan di GRN
            $poRes = pg_query_params("SELECT status, currency_id, COALESCE(NULLIF(exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate
                                        FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = $1", [$po_id]);
            PgOrFail($poRes, 'Gagal cek PO');
            $poRow = pg_fetch_assoc($poRes);
            if (!$poRow) throw new Exception('PO tidak ditemukan');
            if (!in_array($poRow['status'], GRN_PO_STATUS_LIST, true)) {
                throw new Exception('GRN hanya dapat dibuat dari PO berstatus ' . implode('/', GRN_PO_STATUS_LIST) .
                                    ' (status PO saat ini: ' . $poRow['status'] . ').');
            }
            $po_currency = $poRow['currency_id'] !== null ? (int)$poRow['currency_id'] : null;
            $po_rate     = (float)$poRow['exch_rate'];

            pg_query('BEGIN');
            SetPgAppUser();
            $ref_no = CreateRefNo('grn_hdr', 'ref_no', 'GRN');

            $hdrResult = pg_query_params(
                "INSERT INTO grn_hdr (ref_no, grn_date, po_id, supplier_id, status, remark, created_by, created_at, currency_id, exch_rate)
                 VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,'open',$5,$6,NOW(),$7,$8) RETURNING id",
                [$ref_no, $grn_date, $po_id, $supplier_id, $remark, $created_by, $po_currency, $po_rate]
            );
            PgOrFail($hdrResult, 'Gagal insert grn_hdr');
            $grn_hdr_id = pg_fetch_result($hdrResult, 0, 'id');

            InsertGrnItems($grn_hdr_id, $arrItems, 0, $po_rate);

            pg_query('COMMIT');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => "GRN {$ref_no} berhasil disimpan.", 'id' => $grn_hdr_id, 'ref_no' => $ref_no]);
            break;

        case 'update':
            $id          = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            $po_id       = (int)($_REQUEST['po_id'] ?? 0);
            $supplier_id = (int)($_REQUEST['supplier_id'] ?? 0);
            $grn_date    = $_REQUEST['grn_date'] ?? date('Y-m-d');
            $remark      = $_REQUEST['remark'] ?? null;
            $arrItems    = json_decode($_REQUEST['arrItems'] ?? '[]', true);

            if (!$id) throw new Exception('GRN tidak valid');

            if (!$supplier_id && $po_id) {
                $supRes = pg_query_params("SELECT supplier_id FROM purchase_orders WHERE id = $1", [$po_id]);
                if ($supRes && $supRow = pg_fetch_assoc($supRes)) {
                    $supplier_id = (int)$supRow['supplier_id'];
                }
            }

            if (!$supplier_id) throw new Exception('Supplier belum dipilih');
            if (empty($arrItems)) throw new Exception('Minimal 1 item harus diisi qty diterima');

            $hRes = pg_query_params("SELECT COALESCE(NULLIF(exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate FROM grn_hdr WHERE id = $1", [$id]);
            PgOrFail($hRes, 'Gagal load kurs GRN');
            $hRow = pg_fetch_assoc($hRes);
            if (!$hRow) throw new Exception('GRN tidak ditemukan');
            $grn_rate = (float)$hRow['exch_rate'];                       // kurs snapshot GRN tidak berubah saat edit

            pg_query('BEGIN');
            SetPgAppUser();

            GuardNoWhReceiving($id, 'diedit');

            $delResult = pg_query_params("DELETE FROM grn_dtl WHERE grn_hdr_id = $1", [$id]);
            PgOrFail($delResult, 'Gagal hapus baris lama grn_dtl');

            $upd = pg_query_params(
                "UPDATE grn_hdr SET supplier_id = $1, grn_date = $2, remark = $3 WHERE id = $4",
                [$supplier_id, $grn_date, $remark, $id]
            );
            PgOrFail($upd, 'Gagal update grn_hdr');

            InsertGrnItems($id, $arrItems, $id, $grn_rate);

            pg_query('COMMIT');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'GRN berhasil diupdate.', 'id' => $id]);
            break;

        case 'delete':
            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
            if (!$id) throw new Exception('GRN tidak valid');

            pg_query('BEGIN');
            SetPgAppUser();

            GuardNoWhReceiving($id, 'dihapus');

            $delDtl = pg_query_params("DELETE FROM grn_dtl WHERE grn_hdr_id = $1", [$id]);
            PgOrFail($delDtl, 'Gagal hapus grn_dtl');

            $delHdr = pg_query_params("DELETE FROM grn_hdr WHERE id = $1", [$id]);
            PgOrFail($delHdr, 'Gagal hapus grn_hdr');

            pg_query('COMMIT');
            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'GRN berhasil dihapus.']);
            break;

        default:
            throw new Exception('Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task));
    }
} catch (Exception $e) {
    @pg_query('ROLLBACK');
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
}

function InsertGrnItems($grn_hdr_id, $arrItems, $exclude_grn_hdr_id, $rate = 1.0) {
    $any_line = false;

    foreach ($arrItems as $it) {
        $qty = (float)($it['qty_received'] ?? 0);
        if ($qty <= 0) continue;

        $poResult = pg_query_params("SELECT qty AS po_qty FROM po_items WHERE id = $1", [$it['po_dtl_id']]);
        PgOrFail($poResult, 'Gagal cek qty PO');
        $poRow = pg_fetch_assoc($poResult);
        if (!$poRow) throw new Exception('Baris PO tidak ditemukan (po_dtl_id=' . $it['po_dtl_id'] . ')');

        $tol = GetPoDtlTolerance($it['po_dtl_id'], (float)$poRow['po_qty'], $exclude_grn_hdr_id);
        if ($qty > $tol['qty_receivable_max'] + 0.0001) {
            throw new Exception("Qty GRN untuk {$it['item_name']} ({$qty}) melebihi batas toleransi +10% PO (sisa maksimal: " . round($tol['qty_receivable_max'], 4) . ")");
        }

        $dtlResult = pg_query_params(
            "INSERT INTO grn_dtl (grn_hdr_id, po_item_id, item_id, item_name, qty_received, unit_cost, unit_cost_base, remark)
             VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7,$8)",
            [
                $grn_hdr_id, $it['po_dtl_id'], $it['item_id'], $it['item_name'],
                $qty, $it['unit_cost'], round((float)$it['unit_cost'] * $rate, 4), $it['remark'] ?? null
            ]
        );
        PgOrFail($dtlResult, 'Gagal insert grn_dtl');

        $any_line = true;
    }

    if (!$any_line) {
        throw new Exception('Tidak ada baris dengan qty > 0');
    }
}

function GuardNoWhReceiving($grn_hdr_id, $actionLabel) {
    $chkResult = pg_query_params(
        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(d.qty_received),0) AS total_wh
         FROM wh_receiving_dtl d
         JOIN wh_receiving_hdr h ON h.id = d.wh_receiving_hdr_id AND h.status = 'posted'
         JOIN grn_dtl gd ON gd.id = d.grn_dtl_id
         WHERE gd.grn_hdr_id = $1",
        [$grn_hdr_id]
    );
    PgOrFail($chkResult, 'Gagal cek qty yang sudah di-WH Rcv');
    $chk = pg_fetch_assoc($chkResult);
    if ((float)$chk['total_wh'] > 0) {
        throw new Exception("GRN tidak bisa {$actionLabel} -- sudah ada WH Rcv terkait. Reverse WH Rcv-nya dulu.");
    }
}

## File: ./m.taxexchangerateCRD.php

<?php
/**
 * Document      : m.taxexchangerateCRD.php
 * Description   : CRUD Backend Controller for Master Kurs Pajak Kemenkeu
 */
set_time_limit(0);
if (!isset($_SESSION)) {
    session_start();
}
date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Jakarta');

require_once 'db_connection.PHP';

$task   = $_POST['task'] ?? $_REQUEST['task'] ?? 'read';
$start  = intval($_POST['start'] ?? $_REQUEST['start'] ?? 0);
$limit  = intval($_POST['limit'] ?? $_REQUEST['limit'] ?? 250);
$id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? $_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);

header('Content-Type: application/json');

switch ($task) {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. READ HEADER GRID (Daftar Periode KMK)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read':
        try {
            $stmtCount = $pdo->query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr");
            $totalCount = $stmtCount->fetchColumn();

            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("
                SELECT id, kmk_no, start_date, end_date, file_url, remark, created_at
                FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr
                ORDER BY start_date DESC, id DESC
                LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset
            ");
            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':offset', $start, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();
            $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            echo json_encode([
                'success' => true,
                'totalRecord' => $totalCount,
                'data' => $data
            ]);
        } catch (Exception $e) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. READ DETAIL GRID (Rincian Kurs per Mata Uang)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'read_detail':
        try {
            $hdrId = intval($_POST['hdr_id'] ?? $_REQUEST['hdr_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($hdrId <= 0) {
                // Default ke header terbaru jika tidak spesifik
                $hdrId = $pdo->query("SELECT id FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr ORDER BY start_date DESC LIMIT 1")->fetchColumn();
            }

            if (!$hdrId) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
                exit;
            }

            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("
                SELECT 
                    d.id, d.hdr_id, d.currency_id, d.currency_code, d.currency_name, 
                    d.unit_multiplier, d.rate AS rate_kmk, d.rate_per_unit AS rate_effective,
                    d.prev_rate, d.change_amount, d.change_direction
                FROM tax_exchange_rate_dtl d
                WHERE d.hdr_id = :hdr_id
                ORDER BY d.currency_code ASC
            ");
            $stmt->execute(['hdr_id' => $hdrId]);
            $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            echo json_encode([
                'success' => true,
                'hdr_id' => $hdrId,
                'data' => $data
            ]);
        } catch (Exception $e) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. FETCH NOW (Tarik Otomatis dari Kemenkeu)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'fetch_now':
    case 'sync':
        try {
            require_once __DIR__ . '/../cron/fetch_kurs_pajak.php';
            $res = fetchAndStoreTaxExchangeRates($pdo);

            if ($res['success']) {
                echo json_encode([
                    'success' => true,
                    'message' => "Berhasil memperbarui Kurs Pajak! KMK: {$res['kmk_no']} ({$res['period']}) - Total {$res['items_count']} mata uang."
                ]);
            } else {
                echo json_encode([
                    'success' => false,
                    'message' => $res['message']
                ]);
            }
        } catch (Exception $e) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => "Error sync: " . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. DELETE RECORD
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    case 'delete':
        try {
            if ($id <= 0) {
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID data tidak valid.']);
                exit;
            }
            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr WHERE id = :id");
            $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);

            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Data Kurs Pajak berhasil dihapus.']);
        } catch (Exception $e) {
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
        }
        break;

    default:
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
        break;
}


## File: ./rabCRD.php

<?php
/**
 * rabCRD.php
 * ============================================================
 * Backend Controller untuk RAB Material per Proyek (rab.js).
 *
 * PERBAIKAN dari versi sebelumnya:
 * - [FIX FATAL] $db tidak pernah diinisialisasi ($db = new crud() hilang) --
 *   penyebab "Call to a member function prepare() on null".
 * - [FIX SKEMA] Tabel sumber SUDAH TIDAK ADA: m_materials, rab_materials,
 *   v_material_budget_monitoring (dihapus saat konsolidasi ke materials/
 *   budget_items). Ditulis ulang memakai materials + budget_items, dengan
 *   status ON TRACK/WARNING/OVERBUDGET dihitung inline (menggantikan view
 *   yang sudah tidak ada) dari budget_items + material_out_items.
 * - Kontrak JSON (nama field: budget_qty, unit_price, tolerance_pct, dst)
 *   DIPERTAHANKAN PERSIS -- rab.js tidak perlu diubah sama sekali.
 * - budget_items.item_name (NOT NULL) diisi otomatis dari materials.material_name.
 * - [MULTI CURRENCY] Satu mata uang anggaran per proyek (projects.budget_currency_id) + kurs ke
 *   mata uang dasar. Harga satuan RAB = mata uang anggaran. Nilai pemakaian aktual (material out,
 *   dalam mata uang dasar) dikonversi ke mata uang anggaran memakai kurs anggaran.
 *   Mata uang tidak bisa diganti bila proyek sudah punya PO (non-batal) atau pemakaian material.
 *   Butuh sql/2026-10-01.rab_currency.sql.
 * - Butuh migration_rab_budget_items.sql (unique index project_id+material_id
 *   utk category='Material') supaya ON CONFLICT di save_rab berfungsi.
 * ============================================================
 */

require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/fx.func.php';

session_start();
header('Content-Type: application/json');

$db = new crud(); // [FIX] sebelumnya tidak ada -- inilah penyebab fatal error

function sendJsonResponse($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    $response = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    if ($success) {
        if (is_array($data) && isset($data['data'])) {
            $response['data'] = $data['data'];
            if (isset($data['totalCount'])) $response['totalCount'] = $data['totalCount'];
        } else {
            $response['data'] = $data;
        }
    }
    echo json_encode($response);
    exit;
}

/** Alasan mata uang anggaran proyek tidak boleh diganti (null = masih boleh). */
function rab_currency_lock_reason($db, int $projectId): ?string {
    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.po_items poi
                          JOIN public.purchase_orders po ON po.id = poi.po_id
                         WHERE po.project_id = :p AND po.status <> 'Dibatalkan' LIMIT 1");
    $st->execute([':p' => $projectId]);
    if ($st->fetchColumn()) return 'Proyek ini sudah memiliki PO.';

    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.material_out_header WHERE project_id = :p AND status = 'posted' LIMIT 1");
    $st->execute([':p' => $projectId]);
    if ($st->fetchColumn()) return 'Proyek ini sudah memiliki pemakaian material (issue).';
    return null;
}

// Pecahan status RAB dipakai di dua tempat (list_projects & load_rab) --
// didefinisikan sekali di sini supaya konsisten.
// - OVERBUDGET : qty terpakai melebihi qty_plan + toleransi
// - WARNING    : qty terpakai melebihi qty_plan, tapi masih dalam toleransi
// - ON TRACK   : sisanya
const RAB_USAGE_CTE = "
    WITH usage AS (
        SELECT moh.project_id, moi.material_id,
               SUM(moi.qty_out) AS qty_out,
               SUM(moi.qty_out * moi.unit_cost) AS actual_cost
          FROM material_out_items moi
          JOIN material_out_header moh ON moh.id = moi.material_out_id AND moh.status = 'posted'
         GROUP BY moh.project_id, moi.material_id
    )
";

$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';

try {
    switch ($task) {

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * 1. LIST_PROJECTS
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_projects':
            $start = (int)($_REQUEST['start'] ?? 0);
            $limit = (int)($_REQUEST['limit'] ?? 250);
            $query = trim($_REQUEST['query'] ?? '');

            $whereClauses = [];
            $params = [];
            if ($query !== '') {
                $whereClauses[] = "(p.name ILIKE :query OR c.name ILIKE :query)";
                $params[':query'] = '%' . $query . '%';
            }
            $searchWhere = $whereClauses ? 'WHERE ' . implode(' AND ', $whereClauses) : '';

            $stmtCount = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.projects p LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON c.id = p.client_id {$searchWhere}");
            $stmtCount->execute($params);
            $totalCount = (int)$stmtCount->fetchColumn();

            $sql = RAB_USAGE_CTE . "
                SELECT p.id, p.name, c.name AS client_name, p.status,
                       COALESCE(UPPER(TRIM(cur.init)), 'IDR') AS currency_code,
                       COALESCE(NULLIF(p.budget_exch_rate, 0), 1) AS budget_exch_rate,
                       COALESCE(agg.material_count, 0) AS material_count,
                       COALESCE(agg.total_budget_cost, 0) AS total_budget_cost,
                       COALESCE(agg.total_budget_cost, 0) * COALESCE(NULLIF(p.budget_exch_rate, 0), 1) AS total_budget_base,
                       COALESCE(agg.total_actual_cost, 0) / COALESCE(NULLIF(p.budget_exch_rate, 0), 1) AS total_actual_cost,
                       CASE COALESCE(agg.status_rank, 0)
                           WHEN 3 THEN 'OVERBUDGET'
                           WHEN 2 THEN 'WARNING'
                           WHEN 1 THEN 'ON TRACK'
                           ELSE 'NO BUDGET'
                       END AS rab_status
                  FROM public.projects p
                  LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON c.id = p.client_id
                  LEFT JOIN public.m_currency cur ON cur.id = p.budget_currency_id
                  LEFT JOIN (
                      SELECT bi.project_id,
                             COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE COALESCE(bi.qty_plan,0) > 0) AS material_count,
                             SUM(bi.qty_plan * bi.unit_price_plan) AS total_budget_cost,
                             SUM(COALESCE(u.actual_cost, 0)) AS total_actual_cost,
                             MAX(
                                 CASE
                                     WHEN COALESCE(u.qty_out,0) > bi.qty_plan * (1 + COALESCE(bi.tolerance_pct,0)/100) THEN 3
                                     WHEN COALESCE(u.qty_out,0) > bi.qty_plan THEN 2
                                     ELSE 1
                                 END
                             ) AS status_rank
                        FROM public.budget_items bi
                        LEFT JOIN usage u ON u.project_id = bi.project_id AND u.material_id = bi.material_id
                       WHERE bi.category = 'Material'
                       GROUP BY bi.project_id
                  ) agg ON agg.project_id = p.id
                  {$searchWhere}
                  ORDER BY p.id DESC
                  LIMIT :limit OFFSET :start";

            $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
            foreach ($params as $k => $v) { $stmt->bindValue($k, $v); }
            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':start', $start, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();
            $projects = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            foreach ($projects as &$p) {
                $p['id'] = (int)$p['id'];
                $p['material_count'] = (int)$p['material_count'];
                $p['total_budget_cost'] = (float)$p['total_budget_cost'];
                $p['total_budget_base'] = (float)$p['total_budget_base'];
                $p['total_actual_cost'] = (float)$p['total_actual_cost'];
                $p['budget_exch_rate'] = (float)$p['budget_exch_rate'];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, ['data' => $projects, 'totalCount' => $totalCount]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * 1b. LIST_CURRENCIES + 1c. GET_EXCHANGE_RATE (kurs default KMK pada tanggal tertentu)
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_currencies':
            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, init, name FROM public.m_currency ORDER BY init ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            $baseCur = fx_base_currency($db);
            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
                $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
                $r['is_base'] = $baseCur['id'] !== null && $r['id'] === $baseCur['id'];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows);
            break;

        case 'get_exchange_rate':
            $cid = (int)($_REQUEST['currency_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($cid <= 0) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Mata uang tidak valid.');
            sendJsonResponse(true, fx_default_rate($db, $cid, (string)($_REQUEST['date'] ?? '')));
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * 2. LIST_MATERIALS
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'list_materials':
            $stmt = $db->query("SELECT id, material_code, material_name, unit, COALESCE(estimated_price,0) AS estimated_price
                                   FROM public.materials WHERE active IS TRUE ORDER BY material_name ASC");
            $materials = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($materials as &$m) {
                $m['id'] = (int)$m['id'];
                $m['estimated_price'] = (float)$m['estimated_price'];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, $materials);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * 3. LOAD_RAB
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'load_rab':
            $projectId = (int)($_REQUEST['project_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($projectId <= 0) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'ID Proyek tidak valid.');

            $stmtP = $db->prepare("SELECT p.name, p.budget_currency_id, COALESCE(NULLIF(p.budget_exch_rate, 0), 1) AS budget_exch_rate,
                                          p.budget_rate_date, UPPER(TRIM(cur.init)) AS currency_code
                                     FROM public.projects p
                                     LEFT JOIN public.m_currency cur ON cur.id = p.budget_currency_id
                                    WHERE p.id = :id");
            $stmtP->execute([':id' => $projectId]);
            $proj = $stmtP->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            if (!$proj) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Proyek tidak ditemukan.');
            $projectName = $proj['name'];

            $baseCur = fx_base_currency($db);
            $curId   = $proj['budget_currency_id'] !== null ? (int)$proj['budget_currency_id'] : (int)$baseCur['id'];
            $currencyInfo = [
                'id'            => $curId,
                'code'          => $proj['currency_code'] ?: ($baseCur['code'] ?: 'IDR'),
                'is_base'       => $baseCur['id'] !== null && $curId === $baseCur['id'],
                'base_code'     => $baseCur['code'] ?: 'IDR',
                'exch_rate'     => (float)$proj['budget_exch_rate'],
                'rate_date'     => $proj['budget_rate_date'],
                'locked_reason' => rab_currency_lock_reason($db, $projectId),
            ];

            $sql = RAB_USAGE_CTE . "
                SELECT bi.id, bi.project_id, bi.material_id,
                       m.material_code, m.material_name, m.unit,
                       COALESCE(bi.qty_plan, 0) AS budget_qty,
                       COALESCE(bi.unit_price_plan, 0) AS unit_price,
                       COALESCE(bi.tolerance_pct, 0) AS tolerance_pct,
                       COALESCE(u.qty_out, 0) AS total_qty_out,
                       (COALESCE(bi.qty_plan,0) - COALESCE(u.qty_out, 0)) AS remaining_qty,
                       CASE WHEN COALESCE(bi.qty_plan,0) > 0
                            THEN (COALESCE(u.qty_out, 0) / bi.qty_plan) * 100
                            ELSE 0 END AS usage_pct,
                       CASE
                           WHEN COALESCE(u.qty_out,0) > COALESCE(bi.qty_plan,0) * (1 + COALESCE(bi.tolerance_pct,0)/100) THEN 'OVERBUDGET'
                           WHEN COALESCE(u.qty_out,0) > COALESCE(bi.qty_plan,0) THEN 'WARNING'
                           ELSE 'ON TRACK'
                       END AS status
                  FROM public.budget_items bi
                  JOIN public.materials m ON m.id = bi.material_id
                  LEFT JOIN usage u ON u.project_id = bi.project_id AND u.material_id = bi.material_id
                 WHERE bi.project_id = :project_id AND bi.category = 'Material'
                 ORDER BY m.material_name ASC";

            $stmt = $db->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->execute([':project_id' => $projectId]);
            $items = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            foreach ($items as &$item) {
                $item['id'] = (int)$item['id'];
                $item['material_id'] = (int)$item['material_id'];
                $item['budget_qty'] = (float)$item['budget_qty'];
                $item['unit_price'] = (float)$item['unit_price'];
                $item['tolerance_pct'] = (float)$item['tolerance_pct'];
                $item['total_qty_out'] = (float)$item['total_qty_out'];
                $item['remaining_qty'] = (float)$item['remaining_qty'];
                $item['usage_pct'] = (float)$item['usage_pct'];
            }
            sendJsonResponse(true, ['name' => $projectName, 'items' => $items, 'currency' => $currencyInfo]);
            break;

        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
         * 4. SAVE_RAB
         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
        case 'save_rab':
            $projectId = (int)($_POST['project_id'] ?? 0);
            $items = json_decode($_POST['items'] ?? '[]', true);

            if ($projectId <= 0) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'ID Proyek tidak valid.');
            if (!is_array($items)) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Format data item RAB tidak valid.');

            // --- Mata uang anggaran + kurs (klien lama yang tidak mengirim currency_id -> pertahankan yang tersimpan)
            $stmtCur = $db->prepare("SELECT budget_currency_id FROM public.projects WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtCur->execute([':id' => $projectId]);
            $storedCur = $stmtCur->fetchColumn();
            if ($storedCur === false) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Proyek tidak ditemukan.');
            $storedCur = $storedCur !== null ? (int)$storedCur : null;

            $baseCur    = fx_base_currency($db);
            $currencyId = (int)($_POST['currency_id'] ?? 0);
            if ($currencyId <= 0) $currencyId = $storedCur ?: (int)$baseCur['id'];
            if ($currencyId <= 0) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Mata uang anggaran belum dapat ditentukan. Jalankan migrasi mata uang.');

            $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.m_currency WHERE id = :id");
            $stmtChk->execute([':id' => $currencyId]);
            if (!$stmtChk->fetchColumn()) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Mata uang anggaran tidak ditemukan.');

            $exchRate = (float)($_POST['exch_rate'] ?? 1);
            if ($baseCur['id'] !== null && $currencyId === $baseCur['id']) $exchRate = 1.0;      // mata uang dasar selalu berkurs 1
            if ($exchRate <= 0) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Kurs harus lebih besar dari 0.');

            $rateDate = (string)($_POST['rate_date'] ?? '');
            $rd = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $rateDate);
            if (!$rd || $rd->format('Y-m-d') !== $rateDate) $rateDate = date('Y-m-d');

            if ($storedCur !== null && $currencyId !== $storedCur) {
                $lock = rab_currency_lock_reason($db, $projectId);
                if ($lock !== null) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Mata uang anggaran tidak dapat diubah. ' . $lock);
            }

            $db->beginTransaction();
            try {
                $stmtProj = $db->prepare("UPDATE public.projects
                                             SET budget_currency_id = :c, budget_exch_rate = :r, budget_rate_date = CAST(:d AS date)
                                           WHERE id = :id");
                $stmtProj->execute([':c' => $currencyId, ':r' => $exchRate, ':d' => $rateDate, ':id' => $projectId]);

                $activeMaterialIds = [];

                // item_name (NOT NULL di budget_items) diambil dari materials.material_name.
                $stmtUpsert = $db->prepare("
                    INSERT INTO public.budget_items
                        (project_id, material_id, category, item_name, unit, qty_plan, unit_price_plan, tolerance_pct, created_at)
                    SELECT :project_id, :material_id, 'Material', m.material_name, m.unit,
                           :budget_qty, :unit_price, :tolerance_pct, NOW()
                      FROM public.materials m WHERE m.id = :material_id
                    ON CONFLICT (project_id, material_id) WHERE category = 'Material' AND material_id IS NOT NULL
                    DO UPDATE SET
                        qty_plan = EXCLUDED.qty_plan,
                        unit_price_plan = EXCLUDED.unit_price_plan,
                        tolerance_pct = EXCLUDED.tolerance_pct,
                        item_name = EXCLUDED.item_name,
                        unit = EXCLUDED.unit
                ");

                foreach ($items as $item) {
                    $materialId = (int)($item['material_id'] ?? 0);
                    $budgetQty  = (float)($item['budget_qty'] ?? 0);
                    $unitPrice  = (float)($item['unit_price'] ?? 0);
                    $tolerance  = (float)($item['tolerance_pct'] ?? 0);

                    if ($materialId > 0 && $budgetQty > 0) {
                        $activeMaterialIds[] = $materialId;
                        $ok = $stmtUpsert->execute([
                            ':project_id' => $projectId, ':material_id' => $materialId,
                            ':budget_qty' => $budgetQty, ':unit_price' => $unitPrice, ':tolerance_pct' => $tolerance
                        ]);
                        if (!$ok || $stmtUpsert->rowCount() === 0) {
                            throw new Exception("Material (id {$materialId}) tidak ditemukan di master materials.");
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Hapus baris RAB Material proyek ini yang tidak lagi ada di grid.
                // Catatan: po_items.budget_item_id -> ON DELETE SET NULL, jadi PO
                // yang sudah pernah dikaitkan ke baris RAB ini tidak ikut terhapus,
                // cuma kaitannya ke baris RAB tersebut hilang.
                if ($activeMaterialIds) {
                    $ph = [];
                    $params = [':project_id' => $projectId];
                    foreach (array_values($activeMaterialIds) as $i => $mid) {
                        $ph[] = ":m{$i}"; $params[":m{$i}"] = $mid;
                    }
                    $stmtDel = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM public.budget_items
                                               WHERE project_id = :project_id AND category = 'Material'
                                                 AND (material_id IS NULL OR material_id NOT IN (" . implode(',', $ph) . "))");
                    $stmtDel->execute($params);
                } else {
                    $stmtDel = $db->prepare("DELETE FROM public.budget_items WHERE project_id = :project_id AND category = 'Material'");
                    $stmtDel->execute([':project_id' => $projectId]);
                }

                $db->commit();
                sendJsonResponse(true, [], 'RAB material berhasil disimpan.');
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                if ($db->inTransaction()) { $db->rollBack(); }
                sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Gagal menyimpan RAB: ' . $e->getMessage());
            }
            break;

        default:
            sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Aksi/Task tidak dikenal.');
            break;
    }
} catch (PDOException $e) {
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Database Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'System Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
}
